Evaluating Prompting Strategies for Zero-Shot Multilingual Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis with Large Language Models
评估大语言模型在零样本多语言方面情感分析中的提示策略
While large language models (LLMs) have advanced natural language understanding, their ability to perform complex target-aspect-sentiment detection (TASD) -- a key task within aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) -- in zero-shot multilingual settings remains underexplored. Existing ABSA research predominantly focuses on English or relies on fine-tuned task-specific models, leaving a gap in understanding how open-weight LLMs handle compound sentiment triplet extraction across diverse language families and domains. We present a systematic evaluation of zero-shot multilingual TASD across 12 languages and three domains using seven instruction-tuned open-weight models -- ranging from 8B to 35B parameters across dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures -- under four prompting paradigms: monolithic, chain-of-thought, sequential, and sequential refine. Our results show that direct monolithic prompting achieves strong zero-shot performance, outperforming multi-step pipelines, while dense models consistently outperform sparse MoE architectures. Through fine-grained error taxonomy profiling and step-level pipeline tracking, we demonstrate that initial aspect term recall forms a hard bottleneck, whereas multi-step decomposition degrades extraction quality; iterative refinement only partially recovers this loss on larger models while inducing net-negative over-refinement on smaller architectures. Finally, generation volume analysis shows that multi-step strategies incur up to 2.38x token overhead without F1 gains, establishing monolithic prompting as the most effective and efficient paradigm for multilingual ABSA.
中文翻译
尽管大语言模型（LLMs）在自然语言理解方面取得了进展，但其在零样本多语言环境下执行复杂的目标-方面-情感检测（TASD）——方面情感分析（ABSA）中的关键任务——的能力仍缺乏充分探索。现有ABSA研究主要集中在英语或依赖微调的任务特定模型，导致对开放权重LLMs在跨多种语言家族和领域进行复合情感三元组提取的表现理解不足。我们系统评估了在12种语言和三个领域中使用七种指令微调的开放权重模型（参数量从8B到35B，涵盖密集型和混合专家MoE架构）的零样本多语言TASD，采用四种提示范式：单体式、思维链、顺序式和顺序优化式。结果显示，直接的单体式提示取得强零样本性能，优于多步流水线；密集型模型始终优于稀疏MoE架构。通过细致的错误分类分析和步骤级流水线追踪，我们发现初始方面词召回构成硬瓶颈，而多步分解会降低提取质量；迭代优化仅在大模型上部分恢复损失，而在小模型上反而因过度优化导致净负效果。最后，生成量分析表明，多步策略带来高达2.38倍的token开销却未提升F1分数，确立单体式提示是多语言ABSA中最有效且高效的方法。
👤 Jakub Šm{í}d, Pavel Kr{á}l 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

RoSci: Exploring Robustness in Science VLMs
RoSci：探索科学视觉语言模型的鲁棒性
Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable significant progress in scientific tasks. However, these models are generally assumed to be presented with complete and accurate information, overlooking the challenges of erroneous/missing information in wild scenarios. To address this gap, we introduce the concept of Scientific Model Robustness, which defines a model's ability to detect anomalies, assess solvability, and perform self-calibration when faced with Information Error (inconsistencies) or Information Loss (insufficiency). Specifically, we present RoSci-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,220 samples using a seed-variant strategy across six degradation types. To address VLM fragility, we further propose RoSci-Agent, a framework that employs a Parse-Verify-Decide pipeline to vet premises before reasoning. The evaluated VLMs sometimes answer questions whose premises are invalid or incomplete. RoSci-Agent achieves a higher Overall Robustness Score than the evaluated standalone VLMs. RoSci-Bench gives the multimodal reasoning community a common test of when models answer, request clarification, or reject a scientific question.
中文翻译
近期视觉语言模型（VLMs）的发展在科学任务中取得了显著进展。然而，这些模型通常假设输入信息完整且准确，忽视了真实场景中可能出现的错误或缺失信息问题。为填补这一空白，我们提出“科学模型鲁棒性”概念，定义模型在面对信息错误（不一致）或信息缺失（不足）时，检测异常、评估可解性并进行自我校准的能力。具体而言，我们构建了RoSci-Bench，一个包含1,220个样本的诊断基准，采用种子变异策略覆盖六种退化类型。为应对VLM脆弱性，我们进一步提出RoSci-Agent框架，采用解析-验证-决策的流水线，在推理前审核前提。评估发现，某些VLM会回答前提无效或不完整的提问。RoSci-Agent在整体鲁棒性得分上优于被评估的独立VLM。RoSci-Bench为多模态推理社区提供了一个共同测试平台，用于判断模型何时应作答、请求澄清或拒绝科学问题。
👤 Junying Wang, Yijin Guo, Yalun Wu, Ziheng Jiao, Farong Wen et al. 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

AutoOntologyBench: A Task-Grounded Benchmark for Agentic Knowledge Engineering
AutoOntologyBench：面向智能体知识工程的任务导向基准测试
Foundation-model agents are increasingly extending beyond software engineering (SWE) into autonomous scientific research, where acquiring and integrating domain knowledge remains a critical challenge. Recent industrial advances have increasingly adopted knowledge graphs and ontology-based semantic layers to organize heterogeneous knowledge into reusable representations. We ask: Can autonomous agents construct ontologies that help them solve real tasks, and do they offer measurable benefits over alternative knowledge representations? Existing benchmarks evaluate ontology correctness or agent task success in isolation, leaving the value of autonomously acquired knowledge unresolved. We therefore introduce AutoOntologyBench, a task-grounded benchmark comprising 19,947 audited tasks grounded in 18 real-world knowledge sources across 7 domains, with source-native verification. Spanning Application, Repair, Organization, and Construction, AutoOntologyBench compares nine frontier-model agents under matched knowledge representations. Layered on retrieval, agent-built ontologies add 6.1 points of task success, yet trail an expert-derived reference by 13.0, identifying autonomous knowledge acquisition as the bottleneck to scalable scientific research. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/VinyehShaw/AutoOntologyBench.
中文翻译
基础模型智能体正逐步从软件工程扩展到自主科学研究领域，其中获取和整合领域知识仍是关键挑战。近期工业进展越来越多地采用知识图谱和基于本体的语义层，将异构知识组织为可复用的表示形式。我们提出问题：自主智能体能否构建有助于解决实际任务的本体？这种本体相较于其他知识表示形式是否具有可衡量的优势？现有基准仅孤立评估本体正确性或智能体任务成功率，未能揭示自主获取知识的实际价值。因此，我们提出了AutoOntologyBench，这是一个任务导向的基准，包含19,947个经审核的任务，覆盖7个领域的18个真实知识源，并支持源生验证。该基准涵盖应用、修复、组织与构建四类任务，对九种前沿模型智能体在相同知识表示下进行比较。结果显示，在检索基础上，由智能体构建的本体使任务成功率提升6.1分，但仍比专家构建的参考本体低13.0分，表明自主知识获取是实现可扩展科学研究的主要瓶颈。代码与资源已公开于https://github.com/VinyehShaw/AutoOntologyBench。
👤 Minye Shao, Chenghao Xiao, Xingyue Zhao, Yang Long 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

Tasklets: A Unit-Test Benchmark for Language Model Architectures
Tasklets：语言模型架构的单元测试基准
We introduce Tasklets, a benchmark of small synthetic tasks for evaluating the computational capabilities of language model sequence-mixing layers. These tasks probe foundational capabilities such as recall, sequence manipulation, and state tracking. Such tasks are widely used in architecture research, but differences in task selection, formulation, and evaluation make comparisons across studies difficult. Tasklets brings these tasks together, organizing them by the capabilities they test and providing a shared setup for evaluating how different architectural layer types learn fundamental computations. We compare a broad range of layer types through controlled experiments with small models. Our task implementations and evaluation code support reproducible, low-cost testing of new architectural proposals: https://github.com/zaydzuhri/flame/tree/tasklets.
中文翻译
我们提出了Tasklets，一个用于评估语言模型序列混合层计算能力的小型合成任务基准。这些任务探查记忆回溯、序列操作和状态跟踪等基础能力。这类任务广泛应用于架构研究中，但由于任务选择、表述和评估方式的差异，跨研究比较困难。Tasklets将这些任务统一整合，按其所测试的能力进行组织，并提供共享实验设置，以评估不同架构层类型如何学习基本计算。我们通过小模型的受控实验，对比了广泛的层类型。我们的任务实现和评估代码支持对新型架构提案进行可复现、低成本的测试：https://github.com/zaydzuhri/flame/tree/tasklets。
👤 Zayd Muhammad Kawakibi Zuhri, Munachiso Samuel Nwadike, Kentaro Inui, Alham Fikri Aji 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.27544]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Alliance Rupture and Repair in Simulating Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Dialogues
模拟认知行为疗法对话中的联盟破裂与修复
In cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT), routine work can strain the client-counselor working alliance, and the counselor must then notice the rupture, repair it, and adapt the work that follows. Existing CBT simulators model client resistance and follow structured treatment plans, but rarely track alliance state or allow counselors to repair ruptures over multiple turns and revise their plans accordingly. We present ALLY, a simulation framework that brings alliance rupture, repair, and replanning into ongoing CBT dialogue. Each client has a hidden alliance state that governs how openly the client engages. Like a real counselor, the counselor agent sees only the dialogue. It must notice when the client pulls back or pushes against the work, repair the rupture, and replan the rest of the session around what it has learned. Automatic evaluation shows that ALLY's counselors are closer to real CBT counselors than those of existing public synthetic datasets in strategy flow and emotional dynamics, and that ALLY is rated highest overall in counseling quality (CTRS) and interpersonal skill (FIS-T). An expert evaluation with 20 licensed therapists also finds the simulated ruptures plausible and the repairs and replanning largely appropriate.
中文翻译
在认知行为疗法（CBT）中，常规治疗过程可能对来访者与咨询师之间的协作联盟造成压力，咨询师必须察觉到这种破裂、进行修复，并调整后续工作。现有的CBT模拟器通常只建模来访者的抗拒并遵循结构化治疗方案，但很少追踪联盟状态，也难以让咨询师在多轮对话中修复破裂并相应地修改计划。我们提出了ALLY，一个将联盟破裂、修复和重新规划融入持续CBT对话的模拟框架。每位来访者都有一个隐藏的联盟状态，决定其参与的开放程度。如同真实咨询师，咨询代理只能看到对话内容，必须察觉到来访者退缩或对抗治疗时的情况，进行修复，并根据所获信息重新规划会话剩余部分。自动评估显示，ALLY的咨询师在策略流程和情感动态方面更接近真实CBT咨询师，且在咨询质量（CTRS）和人际技能（FIS-T）上整体评分最高。20位持证治疗师的专家评估也认为模拟的破裂合理，修复与重规划基本恰当。
👤 Jian Chai, Yitian Yang, Siqi Liu, Yi-Chia Wang, Yi-Chieh Lee 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.27536]

Open CDLM: A Benchmark and Library for Continuous Diffusion Language Models
Open CDLM：连续扩散语言模型的基准与库
Reliable comparisons of continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) require evaluating both model fit and the distribution of generated text. Two common practices leave this goal only partially addressed. Likelihood-based evaluation often reports a variational upper bound on discrete negative log-likelihood, whose slack can confound comparisons. Generated-sample-based evaluation commonly combines generative perplexity with within-sequence unigram entropy; both scores can remain favorable even when a sampler produces the same text on every draw. We introduce \textbf{Open CDLM}, a benchmark for evaluating CDLMs and their samplers, and describe the design of an accompanying open-source library to support reproducible comparisons. For likelihood-based evaluation, we clarify which comparisons of model fit are supported by reported perplexity bounds and how those conclusions depend on the evaluation procedure. For sample-based evaluation, we use \textit{Kernel Text Discrepancy} (\textbf{KTD}) to compare collections of generated and real texts, alongside diagnostics for diversity, validity, and coverage. In controlled OpenWebText experiments, replacing a collection of texts with copies of a single selected passage improves both conventional generation scores. MAUVE detects the resulting collapse, and its response to other perturbations depends on the text representation and the type of perturbation. This work marks the beginning of Open CDLM as an open-source project for CDLM research. We will maintain and extend the library over time and welcome contributions from the community.
中文翻译
对连续扩散语言模型（CDLMs）进行可靠比较需要同时评估模型拟合度和生成文本的分布。目前常见的两种做法仅部分满足这一目标。基于似然的评估通常报告离散负对数似然的变分上界，其松弛性可能导致比较混淆；基于生成样本的评估常结合生成困惑度与序列内一元语法熵，但即使采样器每次生成相同文本，这些分数仍可能保持良好。我们引入了 extbf{Open CDLM}，一个用于评估CDLM及其采样器的基准，并描述了一个配套的开源库以支持可复现的比较。针对基于似然的评估，我们明确了哪些模型拟合比较可通过报告的困惑度边界支持，以及结论如何依赖于评估过程。针对基于样本的评估，我们使用 extit{核文本差异}（ extbf{KTD}）来比较生成文本与真实文本集合，并辅以多样性、有效性与覆盖率的诊断工具。在受控的OpenWebText实验中，用单个选定段落的副本替换文本集合会提升传统生成得分，但MAUVE能检测到由此引发的崩溃现象，其对其他扰动的响应取决于文本表示方式和扰动类型。本工作标志着Open CDLM作为CDLM研究开源项目的开端，我们将持续维护和扩展该库，并欢迎社区贡献。
👤 Hao Jiang, Ruixi Chen, Jiayi Han, Yulei Sun, Yujian Chen et al. 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.27504]

The Assistant Persona Differs Across Languages in Activation Space
助手人格在激活空间中跨语言存在差异
Large language models behave differently across languages, and are noticeably less safe in lower-resource languages. This gap is well established behaviorally, but not mechanistically. Directions in activation space, including the assistant persona direction, are almost always extracted from English and assumed to generalize across languages. We extract the assistant persona direction in 31 languages in Qwen3-32B, and find that the resulting axes differ substantially: their cosine similarity to the English axis ranges from 0.378 to 0.911. Moreover, each language's assistant persona direction and its magnitude are correlated with the language's resource level. We also find that the model's default assistant state is farther from the helpful assistant pole in lower-resource languages. Finally, steering against a language's own axis amplifies persona-based jailbreak more than steering against the English axis. These results indicate that the model does not carry a single assistant representation across languages. More broadly, the assistant persona may need to be an explicit target of multilingual alignment.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在不同语言中的表现存在差异，且在低资源语言中明显更不安全。这一差距在行为层面已被广泛证实，但在机制层面尚不清楚。激活空间中的方向（包括助手人格方向）通常从英语中提取，并被假定可跨语言泛化。我们在Qwen3-32B中对31种语言提取了助手人格方向，发现所得轴线存在显著差异：其与英语轴线的余弦相似度介于0.378至0.911之间。此外，每种语言的助手人格方向及其幅度均与该语言的资源水平相关。我们还发现，模型默认的助手状态在低资源语言中距离“有益助手”极点更远。最后，沿语言自身轴线进行操控比沿英语轴线操控更能放大基于人格的越狱攻击。这些结果表明，模型并未在跨语言间携带单一的助手表征。更广泛而言，助手人格可能需要成为多语言对齐的显式目标。
👤 Daniel Yoo, Naama Rozen, Evan Harris 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

Typhoon OCR 2: From Thai Document Transcription to Multilingual, Multi-Task OCR
Typhoon OCR 2：从泰语文档转录到多语言、多任务OCR
Typhoon OCR 1.5 focused on reading Thai documents. However, in real-world workflows, users often need to extract specific information or preserve the structure of an entire page for further processing. Typhoon OCR 2 handles both tasks in a single model, with prompt-driven extraction and structured full-page parsing. Full-page parsing covers Thai, English, and Chinese. We release compact 2B and 0.8B models adapted from open document parsers through supervised fine-tuning on curated Thai and public multilingual data, followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task rewards and a rule penalizing unfinished responses. Among evaluated open models, Typhoon OCR 2 2B scores highest on Thai MDPBench (80.7 versus 68.9 for Typhoon OCR 1.5) and on ThaiOCRBench (59.91 versus 40.45), which covers recognition, extraction, and document understanding. The 2B model remains close to its base on English olmOCR-bench (84.7 versus 84.4) and on OmniDocBench, which evaluates English and Chinese together (90.97 versus 91.03). On MDPBench, GRPO reduces the mean share of pages with repetition loops for the 2B model from 3.2% to 0.7%, with a smaller and statistically uncertain score gain for the 0.8B model. We release the model weights, Python package, and evaluation suite.
中文翻译
Typhoon OCR 1.5专注于泰语文档阅读。然而，在实际工作流中，用户往往需要提取特定信息或保留整页结构以供进一步处理。Typhoon OCR 2在单一模型中同时完成这两项任务，采用提示驱动的信息提取和结构化全页解析。全页解析涵盖泰语、英语和中文。我们发布了通过在精选泰语文档和公开多语言数据上进行监督微调，再经组相对策略优化（GRPO）并施加任务奖励及未完成响应惩罚规则训练得到的紧凑型2B和0.8B模型。在评估的开源模型中，Typhoon OCR 2 2B在泰语MDPBench（80.7 vs. 68.9）和泰语OCRBench（59.91 vs. 40.45）上得分最高，后者涵盖识别、提取和文档理解。2B模型在英文olmOCR-bench（84.7 vs. 84.4）和OmniDocBench（90.97 vs. 91.03）上仍保持接近基础模型的表现。在MDPBench上，GRPO将2B模型的重复循环页面平均占比从3.2%降至0.7%，0.8B模型的得分提升较小且统计上不显著。我们发布了模型权重、Python包和评估套件。
👤 Surapon Nonesung, Natapong Nitarach, Sittipong Sripaisarnmongkol, Kunat Pipatanakul, Warit Sirichotedumrong 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.27444]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Silent Arbitration: How Large Language Models Resolve Co-Authoritative Conflicting Instructions
沉默仲裁：大语言模型如何解决共权威性冲突指令
Large language models increasingly receive high-level instructions about roles, values, interests, and communication style. Such instructions can conflict even when a prompt assigns them equal authority and provides no priority rule. We study co-authoritative persona conflict, in which a single prompt jointly assigns two opposing personas. Across eight conflict types grounded in published source material and five LLMs, we measure conflict acknowledgment, resolution strategy, and directional stance. Models acknowledge incompatibility in 72% of direct checks but explicitly surface it in only 22% of task responses, revealing silent arbitration. Dominance accounts for 45% of dual-persona responses, and 35% of compromise-labeled responses retain directional lean. Prompt framing further changes the visible form of arbitration substantially more than ordinary paraphrase variation. These findings show that evaluating instruction following should assess whether models disclose conflicts, how they resolve them, and which persona their responses favor.
中文翻译
大语言模型越来越多地接收关于角色、价值观、兴趣和沟通风格的高层指令。这些指令即使在提示中赋予同等权威且未提供优先规则的情况下也可能相互冲突。我们研究了共权威性人格冲突，即单个提示同时分配两个对立的人格。在基于已发表资料的八种冲突类型和五种LLM上，我们测量了冲突承认、解决策略和方向立场。模型在72%的直接检查中承认不相容性，但在任务响应中仅22%明确揭示冲突，显示出“沉默仲裁”现象。主导性解释了45%的双人格响应，35%的标为妥协的响应仍保留方向倾向。提示框架对仲裁可见形式的影响远大于普通改写变化。这些发现表明，评估指令遵循应考察模型是否披露冲突、如何解决以及其响应偏向哪个角色。
👤 Sihyeon Lee, Jiwoo Kum, Jihun Song, Chanwoo Kim, Chanjun Park 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.27421]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
ORBIT: Offset-based Activation Steering for Cross-lingual Multimodal Fact Verification
ORBIT：基于偏移量的激活引导用于跨语言多模态事实验证
Multimodal fact verification faces a fundamental accessibility bottleneck: while large proprietary models exhibit cross-lingual robustness, small vision-language models suffer a severe, disproportionate accuracy collapse on low-resource languages. Closing this gap traditionally requires prohibitive scale or extensive language-specific fine-tuning. In this work, we propose Offset Recovery via Bilingual Inference-time Technique (ORBIT), an algorithmic intervention that mitigates this collapse at inference time. ORBIT estimates span-specific offset vectors from parallel claim-evidence instances and applies them to claim and evidence representations, targeting the cross-lingual degradation induced by low-resource evidence. Experiments across 81 configurations reveal that ORBIT substantially recovers both accuracy and F1-score in low-resource settings, exposes calibration offset norms as a diagnostic proxy, and outperforms translation-based and steering baselines.
中文翻译

Observed, Curated, Reused: Observation Bias in Clinical AI Evaluation
观察、筛选、重用：临床AI评估中的观察偏差
Clinical AI evaluations are vulnerable to observation bias embedded in routine medical documentation, which captures only what clinicians notice, test, and record rather than a complete clinical picture. As this evidence travels into publications and reference knowledge graphs, crucial distinctions disappear: unexamined symptoms blur with confirmed negatives, and chronological disease progression is lost. Surviving evidence can also enter both the benchmark query and the reference knowledge base, creating a self-referential loop where a patient is evaluated against clues derived from their own published case report. To quantify this bias, we tracked 6,503 clinical cases across six historical Human Phenotype Ontology (HPO) releases (October 2021-June 2026) to identify when HPO first cited the publications describing them. This yielded a confirmatory cohort with an observable "source ingestion" event, where HPO cited a case's source publication as target-disease evidence. Diagnostic rankers depended heavily on these source publications: for our primary semantic ranker (Resnik), publication-weighted mean reciprocal rank rose from 0.26 before paper ingestion to 0.84 upon inclusion in HPO, but collapsed to 0.05 when source-linked assertions were counterfactually removed. Two additional rankers (IC-cosine and LIRICAL) reproduced this trajectory, and control experiments confirmed the diagnostic jump was driven by the patient's publication rather than background HPO growth. Clinical AI systems may therefore score well because their knowledge base contains evidence linked to the test case's source publication, rather than through generalization. Modern knowledge is not the problem; evaluation evidence must match the claim. Trustworthy benchmarks must separate patients, source-linked evidence, and time: historical tests should use only knowledge available at the intended decision time.
中文翻译
临床AI评估容易受到常规医疗记录中嵌入的观察偏差影响，这些记录仅反映临床医生注意到、检测和记录的内容，而非完整的临床图景。当这些证据进入论文和参考知识图谱后，关键区分消失：未检查的症状与确认的阴性结果混同，时间上的疾病进展也被丢失。幸存的证据还可能同时出现在基准查询和参考知识库中，形成自指循环——患者被其自身已发表病例报告中的线索所评估。为量化此偏差，我们追踪了六次历史人类表型本体（HPO）发布版本（2021年10月–2026年6月）中的6,503个临床病例，确定HPO首次引用其描述论文的时间点。这形成了一个可验证的“源数据摄入”事件队列，其中HPO将病例的源论文作为目标疾病的证据。诊断排序器高度依赖这些源论文：对于我们的主语义排序器（Resnik），加权倒数排名从论文摄入前的0.26上升至摄入后的0.84，但在反事实移除源关联断言后骤降至0.05。另外两个排序器（IC-cosine和LIRICAL）也重现了这一趋势，控制实验确认诊断跃升是由患者论文驱动，而非HPO背景增长所致。因此，临床AI系统可能因知识库包含与测试案例源论文相关的证据而得分较高，而非真正具备泛化能力。现代知识并非问题所在；评估证据必须匹配声明。可信的基准必须分离患者、源关联证据与时间：历史测试应仅使用在预期决策时间点可用的知识。

Do Companies Deliver on Their IPO Claims?
公司是否兑现了其IPO承诺？
Initial public offering (IPO) filings describe what a company does and what it intends to become. Yet whether individual forward-looking claims are subsequently realized is difficult to assess. We introduce Vision Follow-Through (VFT), a benchmark and agentic pipeline that separates claim extraction, category assignment, verifiability, and evidence-grounded follow-through assessment. \vftclaims{} contains 5,066 human-annotated claim spans from 50 IPO filings, while \vftverify{} adds verifiability tiers, follow-through outcomes, and cited later evidence for 1,000 claims. Across language models, classifying human-annotated claims is substantially easier than extracting claims from full filings. Extraction coverage also varies sharply by category, with Organizational/Culture claims recovered far less often than Market Opportunity claims. Follow-through assessment poses a separate challenge: model judgments diverge from human assessments in different ways rather than exhibiting a single consistent bias. These results show that reliable assessment of corporate follow-through requires both finding the relevant claims and grounding subsequent judgments in later evidence.
中文翻译
首次公开募股（IPO）文件描述了公司的业务内容及其未来愿景。然而，评估这些前瞻性声明是否被实际实现却十分困难。我们提出了Vision Follow-Through（VFT），一个基准测试和智能体流水线，用于分离声明提取、类别分配、可验证性判断以及基于证据的后续评估。\vftclaims{}包含来自50份IPO文件的5,066个由人工标注的声明片段，而\vftverify{}则为其中1,000个声明添加了可验证性层级、后续结果及引用的后续证据。在不同语言模型中，对人工标注声明进行分类比从完整文件中提取声明要容易得多。此外，不同类别的声明提取覆盖率差异显著，组织/文化类声明的恢复率远低于市场机会类声明。后续评估则面临另一挑战：模型判断与人类评估的偏差方式各异，并非表现出单一一致的偏见。这些结果表明，可靠评估企业后续执行情况需要既能准确识别相关声明，又能将后续判断建立在后期证据之上。

Correct Precedents Induce Policy Violations in On-Device LLM Agents
正确先例导致设备端大模型代理违反策略
Local-cloud agent systems pair a cloud model with a small on-device agent that acts on private data, often as the last check before an irreversible action. We identify, for the first time, a failure in which such an agent is led into a violation by its own correct decisions within a session. After it correctly releases an item whose restriction was lifted, it becomes more likely to release the next item that is still restricted. Because the trigger is a correct decision, a history with no faulty step can still end in a violation. We call this failure correct-precedent generalization and find it in 13 open-weight models from nine developers. A single correct precedent can cut the safe rate of the most affected agent roughly in half, and the risk compounds when a refused request is simply sent again in a new session. Interventions on the agent's history indicate that it carries its decision on the released item over to the restricted one instead of checking that item's status. Building on this finding, we propose Paired Decision Distillation (PDD), which distills the decisions of a weakly affected teacher into a LoRA adapter. PDD restores the safe rate to nearly 100% in every distilled model, without extra prompt text or model calls.
中文翻译
本地-云端代理系统将云端模型与小型设备端代理配对，后者在私有数据上执行操作，通常作为不可逆动作前的最后一道检查。我们首次识别出一种故障模式：代理在其会话中因自身正确的决策而陷入违规。在正确释放一个限制已解除的项目后，它更有可能释放下一个仍受限制的项目。由于触发因素是正确决策，即使历史记录中没有错误步骤，也会导致违规。我们称这种现象为“正确先例泛化”，并在九家开发者的13个开源权重模型中发现该现象。单个正确先例可使受影响最严重的代理的安全率大致减半，且当被拒绝请求在新会话中再次提交时，风险会叠加。对代理历史的干预表明，其将已释放项目的决策带到了受限项目上，而非检查该项目的状态。基于这一发现，我们提出配对决策蒸馏（PDD），将弱受影响教师模型的决策蒸馏为LoRA适配器。PDD在所有蒸馏模型中将安全率恢复至接近100%，且无需额外提示文本或模型调用。

How Much of a Calculation-Error Rate Is Not Arithmetic: Auditing the FinReason Cup 2026 Task 3 Metric with a Deterministic Rule System
计算错误率中有多少并非算术错误：使用确定性规则系统审计FinReason Cup 2026任务3的度量标准
Task 3 of the FinReason Cup 2026 scores a system on two values per case, the one an XBRL filing reports and the one its own calculation relationships imply, and labels each failure as structural, extraction or calculation. This report shows that the calculation-error rate so defined does not count arithmetic failures, and bounds how far it is from one using the published board alone. One of the three rules behind the benchmark, DQC_US_0015, has an answer that is the negation of the reported value (in all 110 published practice cases, against 1 of 120 and 6 of 102 for the other two), so a calculation label on such a case records a wrong fact or a wrong route, not a wrong sum. Subtracting the available structural and extraction labels from each entry's error count on that rule gives a floor: the bound is positive for 22 of the 23 ranked entries, and at the top it is sharpest, with at least 11 of 12 and 12 of 13 calculation errors of the two joint-first entries falling on cases where no arithmetic remained. The calculation-error rate of the best systems on this task is almost entirely not arithmetic. The vehicle for that result is an entry that reconstructs the three data-quality rules the benchmark is drawn from, DQC_US_0015, DQC_US_0117 and DQC_US_0126, as a deterministic system with no learned parameters, no training step and no external service, running all 680 cases in 1.4 seconds. It scores 96.62% under the offline judge on development and 95.29% on the published test set, and its residual error concentrates in cases where a concept carries several equally flagged facts and the question names no dimensional member. Reading those error classes against the per-rule accuracies is what exposed the metric, which is why both are reported here.
中文翻译
FinReason Cup 2026任务3对每个案例评估两个数值：一个来自XBRL文件报告，另一个由其自身的计算关系推导得出，并将每项失败标记为结构性、提取性或计算性。本报告表明，所定义的计算错误率并未包含算术错误，并仅通过公开榜单即可界定其与1之间的差距。基准测试背后的三个规则之一DQC_US_0015，其正确答案是报告值的否定（在全部110个已发布练习案例中，而其他两个规则分别为1/120和6/102），因此在该类案例中标记为计算错误，实际上记录的是错误事实或错误路径，而非错误求和。从每个条目的错误计数中减去可用的结构性和提取性标签，可得到下界：在23个排名条目中的22个中该下界为正，且在最高排名处最为显著——前两名联合第一的系统中，至少11/12和12/13的计算错误均发生在无需算术运算的案例上。该任务中最佳系统的计算错误率几乎完全不是算术错误。实现这一结果的工具是一个重建了基准测试所依据的三条数据质量规则（DQC_US_0015、DQC_US_0117和DQC_US_0126）的确定性系统，该系统无学习参数、无训练步骤、无外部服务，可在1.4秒内运行全部680个案例。它在开发集上的离线裁判得分达96.62%，在公开测试集上得分为95.29%；其残差错误集中在概念携带多个同等标记事实且问题未指定维度成员的案例中。通过将这些错误类别与各规则的准确率相对照，揭示了该度量标准的问题，因此两者均在此报告。

Co-Evolving Behavioral Agents and Detectors for Adaptive Information Operations
协同演化行为智能体与检测器以实现自适应信息操作
Detectors of state-sponsored information operations (IOs) are trained on campaigns that platforms have already taken down. They lose much of their accuracy on an actor they have not seen, even though no operator in the archived data is trying to avoid them. A real operator wants two things, to reach an audience and to avoid being caught. We present IO-AI, a framework that gives simulated agents these two capabilities, engagement and evasion, and tests what each one does to detection. The agents are built from the posting behavior of real campaigns and choose the emotion, type, and timing of each post. An agent and a detector then train against each other in turn. We rank the trained agents by how hard they are to detect and train one detector on them from easiest to hardest. On 20 Twitter IO campaigns, this detector reduces missed detections of adaptive agents by 65% relative to a detector trained only on the archived campaigns, at a similar false-alarm rate. On actors it has never seen it reaches an F1 of 0.81, against 0.62. We find that an agent trained to seek engagement becomes easier to catch, because it learns the fast, emotional retweeting that archived campaigns were caught producing. An agent that was trained for engagement before the game keeps its engagement lead after it learns to evade. We also introduce a behavioral distance metric that compares two groups of accounts using only their unlabeled posting histories. The larger the distance between two groups, the worse a detector trained on one does on the other (Spearman rho = -0.58), so the metric can be used to choose which new groups to test first.
中文翻译
针对国家支持的信息操作（IO）的检测器通常基于平台已下架的活动进行训练，因此在面对未曾见过的攻击者时准确性大幅下降，尽管档案数据中的操作员并未试图规避检测。真实操作者希望达成两个目标：触达受众并避免被发现。我们提出IO-AI框架，赋予模拟智能体“参与”和“规避”两种能力，并测试二者对检测的影响。智能体基于真实活动的发帖行为构建，自主选择每条帖子的情感、类型和发布时间。智能体与检测器交替进行对抗训练。我们根据智能体的检测难度对其进行排序，并训练一个检测器，从最易检测到最难检测的智能体逐步学习。在20个Twitter IO活动中，该检测器相对于仅在归档活动上训练的检测器，将自适应智能体的漏检率降低了65%，同时保持相似的误报率。对于从未见过的攻击者，其F1达到0.81，相较之下原检测器仅为0.62。研究发现，仅追求参与度的智能体会变得更容易被捕捉，因为它学会了档案中被发现的快速情绪化转发行为；而在博弈前即已训练参与度的智能体，在学会规避后仍能保持其参与优势。我们还引入一种行为距离度量，仅使用账户的未标注发帖历史来比较两组账户。两组间距离越大，一个在一组上训练的检测器在另一组上的表现越差（Spearman rho = -0.58），因此该度量可用于决定优先测试哪些新群体。

Do Senior Agents Do Their Job? Measuring Role Integrity in How LLM Agents Use Subordinate Material
高级智能体是否履行其职责？测量LLM智能体如何使用下属材料的角色完整性
Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly divide long tasks among subagents whose reports a senior agent turns into the final answer. Such a division of labor pays off only if each agent, and above all the senior agent, carries out the duties of its role. Current evaluations, however, mostly score the final answer, and a fluent, well-cited answer cannot show whether the senior agent weighed its subagents' reports or merely aggregated them. To assess whether a senior agent meets its assigned responsibilities, we define role integrity as the preservation of an agent's functional responsibilities when it acts on its subordinates' material. To measure it, we introduce RI-Bench, a behavioral benchmark grounded in the structures and public records of three institutional hierarchies: peer review, appellate adjudication, and parliamentary inquiry. We choose these hierarchies because their rules explicitly assign the senior role a duty toward subordinate material, and together they cover three basic duties: to weigh, to verify, and to relay. Rather than scoring output correctness, we measure how senior agents' outputs respond to controlled changes in subordinate material, using rule-based checks and a rubric-guided LLM judge calibrated against human annotation. Across eight LLMs and over 12,000 agentic episodes, senior agents rarely adopt a planted procedural error (2.9% of rulings) but often follow a majority of generic reviews (up to 71% of decisions) or drop witnesses' stated limitations (up to 65% of cited claims). Obligation prompts reduce these failures without removing them, and they also shift behavior in the control conditions. These findings motivate direct, obligation-specific evaluation of role integrity rather than inferring it from general capability or explicit role instructions. Code and data are available at https://github.com/jxtse/ri-bench.
中文翻译
多智能体LLM系统越来越多地将长期任务分配给子智能体，由高级智能体整合其报告形成最终答案。这种分工只有在每个智能体，尤其是高级智能体，切实履行其角色职责时才有效。然而，当前评估主要关注最终答案的正确性，而流畅且引用充分的答案无法反映高级智能体是否真正权衡了下属报告，还是仅简单聚合。为评估高级智能体是否履行其责任，我们定义“角色完整性”为智能体在处理下属材料时对其功能职责的保留程度。为此，我们提出了RI-Bench，一个基于三个制度层级结构和公开记录的行为基准：同行评审、上诉裁决和议会质询。选择这些层级是因为它们明确赋予高级角色对下属材料的责任，且共同涵盖三种基本职责：权衡、验证和转述。不同于仅评估输出正确性，我们通过规则检查和基于评分标准的LLM评判（经人类标注校准），测量高级智能体输出对下属材料受控变化的响应。在八种LLM和超过12,000次智能体事件中，高级智能体很少采纳预设的程序性错误（仅2.9%的裁决），但经常遵循多数通用评审意见（高达71%的决策）或忽略证人陈述的限制（高达65%的引用主张）。义务提示虽能减少这些失误但无法根除，且在控制条件下也改变了行为模式。这些发现强调应直接、针对性地评估角色完整性，而非通过通用能力或显式角色指令间接推断。代码与数据可在https://github.com/jxtse/ri-bench获取。

Cross-Platform Production Signatures of State-Linked Information Operations
国家关联信息操作在跨平台上的生产签名
An information operation active on several platforms presents different names, personas, and topics on each. Its working routine is harder to change. We call this routine the operation's production signature: the regularities in when and how its accounts post, reply, and repost. We measure it from timestamps and coarse action types only, using no content, profile metadata, or network ties, as timing and action profiles for accounts and learned action-gap policies for populations. We then ask which parts of a signature carry over to another platform and which belong to a single operation. We train models on Twitter archives of four state-linked operations and apply them to Internet Research Agency (IRA) accounts on Reddit, with no Reddit training labels. They rank the IRA accounts at AUC 0.90-0.94. Content detectors on the same route fall from 0.98 within a platform to 0.81. Daily phase accounts for most of the transfer. Posting volume reverses direction between platforms, and action mix follows the role accounts hold on each platform. What transfers is not unique to the IRA: a second operation shares the same working-hours window, and action-gap policies separate every operation from organic accounts (AUC 0.96-1.0) without consistently ranking their own operation first. On 938 Telegram channels, groups that forward the same sources keep ordinary hours and pace but publish original posts in the same ten-minute windows more often than chance. This pattern is invisible when accounts are examined one at a time. Our results suggest that timing and action traces learned on one platform can help flag likely operations on another.
中文翻译
在多个平台上活跃的信息操作会呈现不同的名称、身份和话题。其运作流程难以改变。我们将此流程称为操作的“生产签名”：账户在何时何地发帖、回复和转发的规律性特征。我们仅基于时间戳和粗粒度动作类型进行测量，不依赖内容、个人资料元数据或网络关系，而是利用账户的时间与动作特征以及人群的已学习动作间隙策略。随后我们探讨哪些签名部分可跨平台迁移，哪些属于单一操作特有。我们在四个国家关联操作的Twitter档案上训练模型，并将其应用于Reddit上的互联网研究机构（IRA）账户，未使用任何Reddit训练标签。模型对IRA账户的排名AUC为0.90–0.94。同一路径的内容检测器在同一平台内的AUC为0.98，跨平台则降至0.81。每日周期是迁移的主要部分。发帖量在不同平台间方向反转，动作组合则遵循账户在各平台所扮演的角色。转移的并非仅限于IRA：第二个操作共享相同的办公时段窗口，且动作间隙策略能有效区分所有操作与自然账户（AUC 0.96–1.0），但不会一致地将自身操作排在首位。在938个Telegram频道中，转发相同来源的群组保持常规作息和节奏，但在同一十分钟窗口内发布原创内容的频率远高于随机水平。这一模式在单独分析账户时不可见。结果表明，从一个平台学习到的时间与动作痕迹可用于识别另一平台上的潜在操作。

Separating Automation Gains from Agent Contributions in AI-Assisted Model Validation
在AI辅助模型验证中分离自动化收益与智能体贡献
LLM agents are being proposed for bank model validation, yet evaluations that compare agent-assisted work with manual practice credit the agent with gains that come from digitized evidence and deterministic tests. We propose a staged architecture in which an LLM-free controlled workflow (P1) is both the fallback and the control arm for individually authorized agent tasks (P2), and an attribution protocol that credits an agent only for faults P1 does not already report. The protocol charges decoy false alarms, unnecessary actions and endorsed developer assertions, counts invalid outputs as misses, folds newly automatable findings back into P1 with discovery credit, and fixes safety bounds and a break-even cost gate in advance. On a synthetic credit-scorecard harness, five scripted reference policies show why this matters: naive case accuracy (4/6) and pooled detection (3/5) tie four flawed policies, whereas the protocol separates all five and credits only the reference policy with detection beyond P1. One fold-back round raises a P1-parroting policy's naive scores without any change to the policy. A regression matrix of sixteen mutants covers nine ways in which earlier versions of the evaluation passed misleadingly. No LLM agent has been evaluated yet; we specify a falsifiable pilot.
中文翻译
LLM智能体正被提议用于银行模型验证，但现有评估将代理协助工作与人工实践对比时，将数字化证据和确定性测试带来的收益归功于智能体。我们提出一种分阶段架构：无LLM的受控工作流（P1）既是备用方案，也是对每个独立授权的智能体任务（P2）的对照组；并设计一种归因协议，仅将P1未报告的故障归功于智能体。该协议扣除虚假警报、不必要的操作和被认可的开发者断言，将无效输出计为遗漏，将新发现的可自动化成果重新纳入P1并给予发现奖励，预先设定安全边界和盈亏平衡成本阈值。在一个合成的信用评分卡测试环境中，五个脚本化的参考策略说明了其重要性：朴素案例准确率（4/6）和合并检测率（3/5）使四个有缺陷的策略表现持平，而该协议则成功区分所有五者，并仅将超出P1检测能力的贡献归于参考策略。一次回滚循环提升了P1模仿型策略的朴素得分，而策略本身未作任何修改。对十六个变异体的回归矩阵揭示了早期评估存在九种误导性方式。目前尚未有LLM智能体接受此类评估；我们提出了一个可验证的试点方案。

NightmareDiscourseBench: A Benchmark Dataset for Fictional Direct Discourse Identification Pipelines
NightmareDiscourseBench：虚构直接话语识别流水线的基准数据集
In this paper we introduce a new benchmark dataset, NightmareDiscourseBench, for testing NLP pipelines that identify forms of direct discourse in English-language fiction. We first review existing publicly available benchmarks (providing summary statistics and reviewing available metadata) for this task, identifying theoretical inconsistency and exclusive focus on speech as primary issues in this domain. We then overview our narrative theory-based framework for classifying and evaluating different forms of direct discourse, and finally describe NightmareDiscourseBench, a new benchmark test set of 12 text samples accompanied by metadata pertinent to the task of direct discourse identification.

Modeling Attributional Style at Scale: A Dataset and Analysis for Psychological Attribution Assessment and Reframing
大规模归因风格建模：用于心理归因评估与重构的数据集与分析
According to the reformulated Learned Helplessness theory, repeated exposure to uncontrollable negative events can foster a depressogenic attributional style, increasing susceptibility to depression yet remaining a tractable target for cognitive therapy. Computational research on attributional cognition, however, is hampered by the lack of large-scale datasets and robust evaluation protocols. In this work, we introduce the Attributional Style Transfer Dataset (ASTD) along with dedicated evaluation metrics, the first benchmark designed to model, assess, and reframe attributional explanations at scale. Constructed via a Prevent-Filter-Validate pipeline that integrates LLM-based generation with specialist validation, ASTD contains 42,000 real-world events paired with psychologically grounded attributions spanning seven styles. Using this dataset, we address two key challenges: (1) scalable assessment of attributional style via both supervised classifiers and zero/few-shot LLMs; and (2) attributional reframing and evaluation, where we propose automatic evaluation metrics to quantify psychological validity. Furthermore, we leverage our proposed metrics to construct a preference dataset, fine-tuning LLMs with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and achieving substantial gains in reframing quality. Together, our dataset, metrics, and methodology offer a new paradigm for understanding and modeling attributional style, with direct implications for scalable and adaptive mental health interventions. Code and data: https://github.com/qzhou711/ASTD
中文翻译

ProbeAgent: Principled Diagnosis of Agent Failures in Verifiable Environments
ProbeAgent：可验证环境中的智能体失败原则性诊断
Improving agents requires discovering where they fail and understanding why. An agent acts over multiple steps in a changing environment, so diagnosing its failures requires more than inspecting the final response of a large language model (LLM). The same unsuccessful outcome can arise from task difficulty or from an error the agent repeats across tasks. We introduce ProbeAgent, an automated framework that discovers agent failures, tests their proposed causes, and identifies shared failure modes. Starting from benchmark tasks, ProbeAgent builds executable tests and validates them using reference solutions, automated checks, and review of the task requirements. For each failed trajectory, it explains the error and constructs new tasks that preserve the suspected cause while changing the surrounding context. Running the agent on these tasks tests whether the same error recurs. ProbeAgent then groups failures with similar causes and generates descriptions that distinguish their errors. This process connects explanations of individual failures to executable tests and descriptions of shared failure modes. We evaluate ProbeAgent across 22 agent configurations using tasks from 10 benchmarks, collecting 1,244 failures and producing 536 failure-mode descriptions. These descriptions distinguish specific errors within broad outcomes such as incomplete work, including planning without acting and stopping before delivering a file. In human evaluation, annotators accept 90.0% of assigned descriptions and correctly reject 83.3% of similar alternatives, supporting the accuracy and specificity of the discovered failure modes.
中文翻译
改进智能体需要发现其失败之处并理解原因。智能体在动态环境中多步行动，因此诊断其失败不能仅依赖大型语言模型（LLM）的最终输出。相同的失败结果可能源于任务难度，也可能源于智能体在多个任务中重复出现的错误。我们提出ProbeAgent，一个自动化框架，用于发现智能体失败、测试其假设原因，并识别共有的失败模式。从基准任务出发，ProbeAgent构建可执行测试，并通过参考解答、自动化检查和任务需求审查进行验证。针对每个失败轨迹，它解释错误并构造新任务，在保留疑似原因的同时改变上下文。在这些任务上运行智能体以检验相同错误是否重现。随后，ProbeAgent将具有相似原因的失败归类，并生成可区分其错误的描述。该过程将个别失败的解释与可执行测试及共享失败模式的描述相连接。我们在10个基准的22种智能体配置上评估ProbeAgent，收集了1,244个失败案例，并生成536个失败模式描述。这些描述能区分广义结果（如工作不完整）中的具体错误，包括只规划不执行、提前停止文件交付等。人工评估显示，标注者接受90.0%的分配描述，正确拒绝83.3%的类似替代项，支持所发现失败模式的准确性和特异性。

Your Judge Inherits Your Prompt: In-Context Label Bias in an LLM-as-Judge Is Directional, Not Symmetric
你的裁判继承了你的提示：在LLM作为裁判中的上下文标签偏差具有方向性而非对称性
LLM-as-judge has become a standard way to score open-ended model outputs, yet the judge is itself a frozen language model steered by a few in-context examples, and such models are known to absorb the label balance of their demonstrations rather than treat them as neutral illustrations. We ask whether this label bias reaches an LLM judge used inside a multimodal (video question answering) evaluation pipeline, and what form it takes. Using a fully frozen generator-judge pipeline, we hold the generated answers fixed and vary only the Yes/No balance of the judge's few-shot examples across four demonstration sets. Each verdict is validated against a two-annotator human consensus on 200 examples per dataset. The judge's acceptance rate stays close to human consensus when its examples are balanced or skewed toward rejection, but jumps sharply (by about 30 points on NExT-QA and 20 on MSVD-QA, with a strongly one-sided error profile) when the examples are skewed toward acceptance; the opposite skew produces no matching drop. The bias is therefore one-directional within the tested range of label ratios. A separate five-draw analysis shows that results from a single example draw are unreliable, varying by up to 18 points, more than any difference between prompting conditions. We conclude that demonstration label bias in an LLM judge is a directional, inflation-only failure mode that can fabricate apparent benchmark gains but not losses, and that it is cheaply avoided by balancing the judge's examples and reporting results averaged over several draws.
中文翻译
LLM作为裁判已成为评分开放式模型输出的标准方式，但裁判本身是一个冻结的语言模型，受少量上下文示例引导，而此类模型已知会吸收演示示例中的标签分布，而非将其视为中立的说明。我们探讨这种标签偏差是否会出现在多模态（视频问答）评估流程中使用的LLM裁判身上，以及其表现形式如何。使用完全冻结的生成-裁判流水线，我们固定生成答案，仅在四个演示集之间改变裁判的少量示例中的“是/否”比例。每个判决均通过两名标注员的人工共识在每组数据集的200个样本上验证。当示例偏向接受时，裁判的接受率急剧上升（在NExT-QA上约30个百分点，在MSVD-QA上约20个百分点，且误差呈强烈单边分布），而相反的偏斜并未导致相应下降。因此，在测试的标签比例范围内，偏差具有单向性。另一次五次抽样分析表明，单次示例抽取的结果不可靠，波动幅度高达18个百分点，超过任何提示条件之间的差异。我们得出结论：LLM裁判中的演示标签偏差是一种具有方向性的、仅导致高估的失效模式，可能制造虚假的基准提升，但无法制造损失，且通过平衡裁判示例并报告多次抽取的平均结果即可低成本避免。

How Long Will My Language Model Run? A Benchmark for Pre-Execution Runtime Prediction
我的语言模型会运行多久？一项预执行运行时间预测基准
Researchers submitting deep learning jobs to shared GPU clusters need runtime estimates to request time limits, choose hardware, and budget compute. Large language models (LLMs) offer a tempting shortcut: describe the job and ask. We introduce PreRun, a benchmark for predicting deep learning training and inference runtime from information available before execution. PreRun contains 801 training and 1,884 inference records of language and vision models collected on a GPU cluster, paired with workload configurations and explicitly defined timing targets. It also includes 64 text-generation configurations measured under standardized conditions, each in three separate program launches. We compare three LLMs (Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Claude Opus 5.5) with regression baselines and evaluate analytical estimates on a supported forward-pass subset. On 16 held-out generation configurations, the open-weight LLMs underestimate latency by 90-99% on average without examples. Claude's zero-shot mean absolute error is 4.5 times that of a Random Forest fit on 32 configurations. Five labeled examples substantially reduce Claude's generation error, but their benefit varies across timing targets. We will open-source our data and evaluation pipeline upon acceptance.
中文翻译
研究人员向共享GPU集群提交深度学习任务时，需要运行时间估计以请求时间限制、选择硬件和预算计算资源。大语言模型（LLMs）提供了一条诱人的捷径：描述任务并询问。我们引入PreRun，一项基于执行前可用信息预测深度学习训练与推理运行时间的基准。PreRun包含在GPU集群上收集的801条训练记录和1,884条推理记录，涵盖语言与视觉模型，附带工作负载配置和明确的定时目标。还包含64种文本生成配置，在标准化条件下测量，每种配置独立启动三次。我们对比三种LLM（Qwen3.5-9B、GPT-OSS-120B、Claude Opus 5.5）与回归基线，并在支持的前向传播子集上评估解析估计。在16个预留的生成配置上，开源权重LLM在无示例情况下平均低估延迟达90%-99%。Claude的零样本平均绝对误差是基于32个配置拟合的随机森林的4.5倍。五个标注示例显著降低了Claude的生成误差，但其收益在不同定时目标间存在差异。我们将在论文被接受后开源数据与评估流水线。

Weight-Space Programming Writes In-Context Learning and Matches Fine-Tuning in Language Models
权重空间编程实现上下文学习并媲美微调
Weight-space programming is a frontier language model's recently reported ability to read and rewrite another network's weights without gradient descent. We investigate how examples can provide a weight update so that a weaker language model performs the task with nothing in the prompt, and study what it edits. Our experiments cover six tasks, four frontier editors and three families of weaker language models, without the editors being told anything about in-context learning (ICL). In most runs, the few-shot minus zero-shot residual is measured at the final prompt token, the ICL task vector, and write it into the weights, at its sparsest into near-silent MLP units keyed to the prompt format. Results reach the accuracy of ICL and of fine-tuning on the same examples, LoRA included, usually leaving perplexity untouched, with worse performance under changes of wording. We also qualify the comparison by counting unfinished runs, observing that one editor falls below fine-tuning; held only to the seen examples, without task pairs of their own, only the strongest editor has similar performance. Given a task and unlabelled inputs, that editor writes its own examples and does nearly as well on unseen items as with labels.
中文翻译
权重空间编程是前沿语言模型最近展示出的一种能力，即无需梯度下降即可读取并重写另一网络的权重。我们研究示例如何提供权重更新，使较弱的语言模型在提示中不包含任何内容的情况下完成任务，并探究其修改的内容。实验涵盖六个任务、四个前沿编辑器和三个较弱语言模型家族，且编辑器未被告知任何关于上下文学习（ICL）的信息。大多数实验中，在最终提示词元处测量少样本减去零样本的残差，作为ICL任务向量，并将其写入权重，通常以最稀疏的方式写入与提示格式相关的近静默MLP单元。结果在相同示例上达到了ICL和微调（包括LoRA）的准确率，通常保持困惑度不变，但在措辞变化时性能下降。我们通过统计未完成运行的情况进一步评估对比，发现其中一个编辑器表现低于微调；仅在仅使用见过的示例、无自身任务对的情况下，最强编辑器才具有类似表现。给定一个任务和未标注输入，该编辑器能自行生成示例，并在未见样本上的表现几乎与有标签时相当。

Search From the Start: Search-Native Pretraining for Language Models
从开始就搜索：面向语言模型的搜索原生预训练
Modern language models (LMs) are increasingly deployed with access to search tools at inference. Yet pretraining remains predominantly focused on predicting static text, with search capabilities typically introduced during post-training. We propose Search-Native Pretraining as a practical route to teach search capability during pretraining from ordinary text corpora. Specifically, we augment pretraining corpus at scale with web-search queries and retrieve web response using a local index. Training on this augmented corpus teaches models to generate search calls and incorporate retrieved evidence during language modeling. Across Wikipedia and DCLM-Edu corpora and 360M to 3.3B parameter scales, search-pretrained models ("QueryLM") show improved performance on knowledge-intensive tasks and higher steerability toward the retrieved evidence. Crucially, their advantage persists after supervised fine-tuning of both search-pretrained and conventionally pretrained models, indicating that the tested post-training procedures do not fully recover the capabilities developed during pretraining. Throughout our experiments, we find that a 1.4B QueryLM consistently beats or matches the performance of a FLOP-matched 3.3B baseline model across all knowledge intensive tasks. These results establish search-pretraining as a practical way to develop foundational search capabilities during pretraining. We release the training artifacts, including model checkpoints and data.
中文翻译
现代语言模型在推理时越来越多地接入搜索工具。然而，预训练仍主要聚焦于静态文本的预测，搜索能力通常在后训练阶段引入。我们提出“搜索原生预训练”作为一种实用路径，旨在从普通文本语料库中在预训练阶段教授搜索能力。具体而言，我们在大规模预训练语料库中加入网络搜索查询，并使用本地索引检索网页响应。在这一增强语料库上训练，使模型学会生成搜索调用并整合检索到的证据进行语言建模。在Wikipedia和DCLM-Edu语料库以及360M至3.3B参数规模下，经过搜索预训练的模型（“QueryLM”）在知识密集型任务上表现更优，且对检索证据的可引导性更高。关键的是，即使在对搜索预训练和传统预训练模型进行监督微调后，其优势依然存在，表明所测试的后训练流程无法完全恢复预训练期间发展出的能力。在整个实验中，一个1.4B的QueryLM模型在所有知识密集型任务上始终优于或匹配FLOP相当的3.3B基线模型。这些结果确立了搜索预训练作为在预训练阶段发展基础搜索能力的一种实用方法。我们发布了训练资源，包括模型检查点和数据。

What Helps Language-Model Agents Review Violent Video? A Controlled Comparison with a Human Evidence Reference
什么有助于语言模型代理审查暴力视频？与人类证据参考的受控比较
Systems that flag violent video for a human operator can now be built from language models without training, by combining perception models and letting a language model decide. We ask how the observations of the perception models should reach the decision, and which evidence matters. On the XD-Violence test set (799 clips) we fix one set of perception agents (video description, object detection, sound tagging, speech recognition and speech tagging) and one reasoning model, and compare three interfaces: messages between agents, a typed evidence graph explored with tools, and flat text read in one pass; and a single audio-visual model given the raw clip. Two annotators provide a human reference that records the decision, the incident segments and whether they relied on sight, sound or speech. Flat text matches the single model (F1 0.967 against 0.968), while the graph agent and message passing are less accurate; a variant of the graph agent developed on a separate set did not close the gap, and both graph summaries and multi-turn tool use lowered accuracy. These results hold with reworded prompts and with a larger reasoning model, except that the larger model weakens message passing enough for the graph agent to beat it. Sound events improve F1 by +0.012 [+0.004, +0.021], in line with the annotators, who used sound or speech on most violent clips. Citations of stored observations are faithful, changing about half of the decisions when removed, but no more so with the graph than with flat text, and the agent pipeline costs about 3 times the GPU time of the single model. We also report two artefacts of vision-language describers that mislead pipelines built on them.
中文翻译
如今，无需训练即可通过结合感知模型并让语言模型做出判断，构建用于向人类操作员标记暴力视频的系统。我们探讨感知模型的观察应如何传递给决策模块，以及哪些证据最为重要。在XD-Violence测试集（799个片段）上，我们固定一组感知代理（视频描述、目标检测、声音标签、语音识别和语音标签）和一个推理模型，比较三种接口：代理间的消息传递、通过工具探索的键入证据图，以及一次性读取的扁平文本；以及一个接收原始视频片段的单体音视频模型。两名标注者提供人类参考，记录决策、事件片段及是否依赖视觉、听觉或语音。扁平文本与单体模型表现接近（F1分别为0.967和0.968），而图代理和消息传递的准确性较低；在另一组数据上开发的图代理变体未能缩小差距，且图摘要和多轮工具使用均降低了准确率。这些结果在改写提示和使用更大推理模型时依然成立，但更大的模型削弱了消息传递效果，使图代理反超。声音事件使F1提升+0.012 [+0.004, +0.021]，与标注者一致，后者在多数暴力片段中依赖声音或语音。引用存储的观察结果忠实可靠，移除时约一半决策发生变化，但在图与扁平文本之间并无显著差异；而代理流水线的GPU时间约为单体模型的三倍。我们还报告了视觉-语言描述器的两个导致管道误导的缺陷。

Know Your Context Bank: Repairing Inconsistency and Measuring Generalization in Context-Aware Text-to-SQL
了解你的上下文银行：修复上下文感知文本到SQL中的不一致并衡量泛化能力
Text-to-SQL systems increasingly rely on context banks: curated collections of example queries, metric definitions, and domain knowledge that tell a model how an organization writes its queries. In the enterprise, a bank grows over years as many analysts add entries, checked one at a time for correctness (does the SQL answer its own question?) and rarely for consistency with one another. Yet correctness is often loosely defined: a question seldom fixes how an entity is counted or which table defines it, so two entries can each pass review yet resolve the same decision in opposite ways. We call this condition context bank rot. A second question follows: does a system that uses a bank generalize from it, or only reproduce it? Existing benchmarks cannot tell, as they do not control how far a test question sits from the context. We answer both questions by comparing queries decision by decision, with a fast scorer distilled from human-calibrated LLM judges. Turned inward, the comparison diagnoses and repairs a bank. Fine-tuning on a cleaned BIRD-train bank beats a same-size random subset at every epoch (+2.6 points on average), and 92.4% of our removals had their SQL corrected by an independent expert audit, against 45.6% of audited entries. In-context examples that follow one convention beat those following the opposite one by 13.3 points, while a mix is barely better than none. Turned outward, the comparison generates test questions at a controlled distance from a bank, a pipeline we call CAT (Context-Aware Testing). On CAT-BIRD, for retrieval and fine-tuning alike, the benefit of a bank falls step by step with distance and is largely gone once about three quarters of a question's logic is new, and on CAT-Spider the benefit of retrieval likewise shrinks with distance.
中文翻译
文本到SQL系统越来越多地依赖于上下文银行：由示例查询、度量定义和领域知识组成的精心整理集合，指导模型如何编写组织的查询。在企业中，随着众多分析师逐年添加条目，银行不断增长，每个条目都经过逐一检查以确保正确性（SQL是否回答了自身的问题），但很少检查彼此之间的一致性。然而，正确性的定义往往模糊：一个问题很少明确实体的计数方式或其定义表，因此两个条目可能各自通过审查，却对同一决策采取相反的方式。我们称这种现象为上下文银行腐坏。第二个问题是：使用银行的系统是真正泛化，还是仅重复复制？现有基准无法回答，因为它们未控制测试问题与上下文之间的距离。我们通过逐决策比较，利用从人类校准的大语言模型裁判中提炼出的快速评分器来回答这两个问题。向内看，该比较可诊断并修复银行。在清理后的BIRD-train银行上微调的表现优于相同大小的随机子集（平均提升2.6分），且92.4%的被移除条目经独立专家审计后得到修正，而被审计条目的修正率仅为45.6%。遵循统一规范的上下文示例比遵循相反规范的高出13.3分，混合情况仅略优于无上下文示例。向外看，该比较生成了与银行保持可控距离的测试问题，我们称之为CAT（上下文感知测试）流程。在CAT-BIRD上，无论检索还是微调，银行带来的优势随距离逐步下降，一旦问题逻辑中有约四分之三为新内容，优势基本消失；在CAT-Spider上，检索收益也随距离减小。
👤 Hailong Li, Mohammadreza Pourreza, Tal Neiman, Reza Sherkat, Per Jacobsson et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

SkillPrivus: Uncovering PRIVacy of USe Harms in Agentic Skills
SkillPrivus：揭示智能体技能中用户隐私滥用的危害
Agent skills extend AI assistants through installable instructions and code that can access users' files, accounts, and data. Even when performing their advertised tasks, skills may infer sensitive attributes, retain unnecessary information, or disclose data to unexpected recipients. We study these potential privacy-of-use harms through SkillPrivus, a static audit framework that analyzes skills before installation without executing them. SkillPrivus extracts data operations from code and natural-language instructions, grounds them in source evidence, and adapts Contextual Integrity to assess their appropriateness. Applied to 726 real skills from public marketplaces, 563 of which handle personal data, SkillPrivus flags at least one potential violation in 307 of these skills (54.5%), and its judgments agree with three blind human annotators on 83.2% of resolvable moves. The flagged behaviors include sensitive inferences, information use beyond the stated task, and disclosures to unexpected recipients, suggesting that privacy harms can arise from ordinary skill functionality.
中文翻译
智能体技能通过可安装的指令和代码扩展AI助手的功能，使其能够访问用户的文件、账户和数据。即使执行其宣传的任务，这些技能也可能推断敏感属性、保留不必要的信息，或向意外接收方披露数据。我们通过SkillPrivus——一种无需执行即可在安装前分析技能的静态审计框架——研究这些潜在的使用隐私危害。SkillPrivus从代码和自然语言指令中提取数据操作，将其与源证据关联，并应用情境完整性理论评估其适当性。在公开市场中726个真实技能的应用中，其中563个处理个人数据，SkillPrivus在307个技能中至少标记出一个潜在违规行为（占54.5%），其判断与三位盲评人类标注者在83.2%的可解决动作上达成一致。被标记的行为包括敏感推断、超出声明任务范围的信息使用，以及向意外接收方披露，表明隐私危害可能源于普通技能功能本身。

TEA-Bench-BR: Can Coding Agents Turn a Branching Clinical Specification into Correct Code?
TEA-Bench-BR：编码智能体能否将分支临床规范转化为正确代码？
Coding agents increasingly implement rules written by domain experts, and bugs in such code are silent: it runs and returns a plausible answer. We introduce TEA-Bench-BR, a benchmark built on the Brazilian pathway for early autism (TEA) screening: three fictitious instruments with the structure of real ones, a multi-professional referral flow, and export to FHIR R4 with the national RNDS profiles, all specified in Portuguese. A generator samples each task's parameters (144 tasks, 6,798 hidden cases), and the hidden cases kill all 1,955 non-equivalent mutants of the reference. With Gemma 4 as the agent, the 12B solves 38.0% of single-instrument tasks and none at the FHIR level; the 31B solves 77.8%, 85.2% and 78.7% at levels 1 to 3 and passes over 99% of the hidden cases, its remaining failures mostly in input validation rather than clinical decisions. With the same tasks in English, the 31B solves 57.4% at level 3, as its code validates an identifier less often; the 12B's, more often. The 12B misses 10.10% of the cases that call for referral; the 31B, 0.02%. A contamination probe on a modified PHQ-9 finds no error that follows the original rules.
中文翻译
编码智能体越来越多地实现领域专家编写的规则，而此类代码中的错误往往是沉默的：程序运行并返回看似合理的答案。我们引入TEA-Bench-BR，这是一个基于巴西早期自闭症（TEA）筛查路径构建的基准：包含三个虚构工具（结构与真实工具一致）、多专业转诊流程，以及符合国家RNDS档案的FHIR R4导出，所有内容均以葡萄牙语描述。生成器为每个任务采样参数（共144个任务，6,798个隐藏案例），隐藏案例可杀死参考代码的所有1,955个非等价变异体。使用Gemma 4作为智能体，12B模型解决了38.0%的单工具任务，但在FHIR级别未能解决任何任务；31B模型在第1至第3级分别解决了77.8%、85.2%和78.7%的任务，并通过超过99%的隐藏案例，其剩余失败主要出现在输入验证而非临床决策方面。在相同任务的英文版本中，31B在第3级解决了57.4%的任务，因其较少验证标识符；而12B则更频繁地进行验证。12B遗漏了10.10%需要转诊的案例；31B仅遗漏0.02%。对修改版PHQ-9的污染探测未发现任何违反原始规则的错误。

agent-benchmark
DistDNA: Language Model Fingerprinting under Stochastic Decoding
DistDNA：在随机解码下的语言模型指纹识别
Behavioral fingerprints identify a large language model (LLM) from its responses to a shared set of prompts, enabling applications such as verifying whether a deployed model corresponds to the checkpoint it claims to be. Deployed models, however, are commonly served with stochastic decoding, under which the same model may produce different responses to an identical prompt, depending on an unknown temperature and top-$p$ setting. Existing representations such as LLM DNA provide limited treatment of this variation. A natural remedy is to characterize models by their response distributions, which, however, raises two challenges. First, each distribution must be estimated from only a small number of sampled responses. Second, a change in decoding setting can alter responses to the same extent as a change of checkpoint. Consequently, fingerprint distances alone provide an incomplete basis for model identity, and identification typically relies on a classifier trained jointly on fingerprints from many checkpoints. We propose DistDNA, which represents a model by its prompt-conditional response distributions and compares models by promptwise maximum mean discrepancy (MMD). The model under examination is assigned to the known checkpoint whose reference fingerprints, collected at several decoding settings, lie nearest to its own, without training a classifier. We show that finite sampling adds variance-dependent terms to the expected squared fingerprint distance, with these terms decreasing as $O(1/R)$ for equal response counts, where $R$ is the number of responses per prompt. Experiments across 115 model checkpoint entries demonstrate that DistDNA improves identification accuracy over the strongest baseline, LLM DNA, by 10.36%.
中文翻译
行为指纹可通过共享提示集响应识别大型语言模型（LLM），用于验证部署模型是否与其声称的检查点一致。然而，部署模型通常采用随机解码，同一模型在相同提示下可能因未知的温度和top-$p$设置产生不同响应。现有表示方法如LLM DNA对这种变化处理有限。自然的解决方案是通过响应分布来表征模型，但这带来两个挑战：第一，每个分布必须仅从少量采样响应中估计；第二，解码设置的变化可能与检查点变化造成同样程度的响应差异。因此，仅靠指纹距离不足以完整支持模型身份识别，通常需联合多个检查点的指纹训练分类器。我们提出DistDNA，通过提示条件响应分布表示模型，并使用逐提示最大均值差异（MMD）比较模型。被检测模型被分配到其参考指纹（在多种解码设置下收集）最接近的已知检查点，无需训练分类器。我们证明，有限采样会为期望平方指纹距离引入与方差相关的项，这些项随$O(1/R)$衰减，其中$R$为每提示的响应数量。在115个模型检查点的实验中，DistDNA相比最强基线LLM DNA提升了10.36%的识别准确率。

SLP-Bench: Evaluating and Improving Acoustic Claims of Reasoning Audio Language Models on Clinical Speech
SLP-Bench：在临床语音上评估和改进推理型语音语言模型的声学主张
Audio language models (ALMs) can answer clinical questions from speech, outperforming baselines built on handcrafted acoustic features. Reasoning ALMs generate a chain of thought that refers to properties of the speaker's voice before predicting. Each statement the model makes about the voice needs to be checked against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. On clinical speech, existing evaluations score only the final answer. We introduce Speech Language Pathologist Bench (SLP-Bench), a benchmark grading each acoustic claim in the chain of thought against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. It covers five clinician-rated datasets and 25 perceptual dimensions, and assesses claims for factuality, stability under resampling, and consistency across reworded and different questions. Across ten open-weight reasoning ALMs evaluated zero-shot, claim balanced accuracy ranges from 0.52 to 0.58, claims change under resampling and rewording, and the most consistent models are not the most factual. Fine-tuning a 7B ALM with GRPO on a reward for claims that agree with clinicians raises its claim balanced accuracy from 0.54 to 0.60 and its mean kappa from 0.39 to 0.61, while preserving answer accuracy on per-dimension questions. SLP-Bench is available upon request, subject to approval by the providers of each dataset.

PaMuk-Bench: A Parallel Multilingual Multitask Benchmark for Vision-Language Tasks
PaMuk-Bench：面向视觉-语言任务的并行多语言多任务基准
While vision-language models (VLMs) currently obtain notable results for highly-studied languages, e.g. English, they exhibit significant performance drops in certain low-resource languages, especially due to the lack of annotated datasets. To this end, we propose PaMuk-Bench, a novel parallel multilingual multitask benchmark for vision-language tasks. Our benchmark includes a set of over 10K curated English prompts that are translated into six languages: Arabic, Danish, French, Hindi, Italian, Romanian. Parallel translations, which are obtained via a semi-automatic process, along with accompanying generated and retrieved images enable the comparative development and evaluation of VLMs across a wide range of tasks: text-to-image generation, image captioning, content-based image retrieval, image generation difficulty estimation, image retrieval difficulty estimation. We empirically show that zero-shot English and multilingual pre-trained VLMs compare consistently worse than their fine-tuned counterparts, confirming that PaMuk-Bench is a valuable resource that can drastically reduce the language gap across a variety of VLM architectures and tasks. PaMuk-Bench is available at https://github.com/ghesil/pamuk_bench.

Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation of RL Experts: Toward Continual Post-Training
可组合的软提示蒸馏：迈向持续后训练
RL post-training typically produces domain experts that must then be consolidated into one model, and as new experts keep arriving, this consolidation becomes a continual process. Weight merging and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) must re-consolidate the whole model whenever an expert is added, and can erode capabilities outside the target domains. We propose Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation (CSPD), which keeps the pre-RL model frozen, distills each RL expert independently into a soft prompt, and composes experts by concatenating their prompts without joint training. A single soft prompt suffices to distill an RL expert, recovering at least 90% of the teacher's gain in 22 of 23 math, code, and agent settings. Independently trained prompts compose well. In a multi-expert setting, the average rises with every expert added, and the full composition matches MOPD (67.83 vs. 67.79). The composition generalizes best across six held-out benchmarks. Input-space adaptation adds little serving overhead, keeping throughput within 1.5% of the unadapted backbone and reaching 2.40x that of rank-4 LoRA. These results point toward continual post-training on a fixed backbone.
中文翻译
强化学习后训练通常生成特定领域的专家模型，随后需将其整合为单一模型；随着新专家不断出现，这一整合过程演变为持续性任务。现有的权重融合与多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）方法在新增专家时必须重新整合整个模型，且可能损害目标领域以外的能力。我们提出可组合软提示蒸馏（CSPD），保持预强化学习模型冻结，独立地将每个强化学习专家蒸馏为一个软提示，并通过拼接提示实现专家组合，无需联合训练。单个软提示即可有效蒸馏一个RL专家，在23个数学、代码和智能体场景中的22个中恢复至少90%的教师性能增益。独立训练的提示具有良好的可组合性。在多专家设置中，平均性能随专家数量增加而提升，完整组合表现接近MOPD（67.83 vs. 67.79）。该组合在六个保留基准上的泛化能力最强。输入空间适配带来的推理开销极小，吞吐量保持在未适配主干的1.5%以内，达到rank-4 LoRA的2.40倍。这些结果表明，基于固定主干的持续后训练是可行路径。
👤 Zhen Zhang, Changyi Yang, Xuehai He, Chengzhi Liu, Yutong Yin et al. 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

Belief-State Management Meets Rubric Rewards for Agentic Forecasting
信念状态管理与评分奖励结合用于智能体预测
Current large language model (LLM) agents still struggle to forecast real-world events. This stems from two primary limitations: i) standard ReAct harness accumulates reasoning and evidence within one growing context, and ii) outcome-based rewards in forecasting lead to a slow and noisy signal that cannot distinguish sound reasoning from lucky guesses. To address these, we introduce a forecasting training framework including harness design and post-training. For the harness side, we propose Proactive Belief-State Management (PBM), equipping agents with a fold tool to compress lengthy context into compact belief states and reset the context for subsequent exploration. For the training side, we develop SEIPH, a five-dimensional, outcome-blind rubric, providing process-quality rewards. Using the rubric-based reward, we post-train a 35B model with PPO and GRPO, yielding DeepForecaster-PPO and DeepForecaster-GRPO. Theoretically, we prove that idealized fold-based stopping converges and calibrated local rewards reduce segment-level gradient variance. Empirically, DeepForecaster-PPO (35B) achieves the best Brier score on both ForecastBench and Polymarket-Sim, outperforming frontier agents based on DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T), Kimi-K2.6 (1T), and GPT-5.5. DeepForecaster-GRPO ranks second on both FutureX metrics. Ablations reveal that PBM also improves the untrained base model without any training. Calibration analysis shows that DeepForecaster-PPO makes more accurate high-confidence predictions than Qwen3.6 and GPT-5.5. Trajectory analysis further shows that forecasts generally improve from the first to the final belief state as agents gather evidence and revise their judgments.
中文翻译
当前大型语言模型（LLM）代理在预测现实世界事件方面仍面临挑战。这主要源于两个限制：i）标准ReAct框架将推理和证据累积在一个不断增长的上下文中；ii）基于结果的奖励在预测任务中导致信号缓慢且嘈杂，无法区分合理的推理与侥幸猜测。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种包含框架设计和后训练的预测训练框架。在框架方面，我们引入了主动信念状态管理（PBM），赋予代理一个折叠工具，可将长上下文压缩为紧凑的信念状态，并重置上下文以进行后续探索。在训练方面，我们开发了SEIPH，一种五维、与结果无关的评分体系，提供过程质量奖励。利用基于评分的奖励，我们使用PPO和GRPO对35B模型进行后训练，得到DeepForecaster-PPO和DeepForecaster-GRPO。理论上，我们证明了理想化的基于折叠的停止机制具有收敛性，且校准的局部奖励能降低分段级别的梯度方差。实证结果显示，DeepForecaster-PPO（35B）在ForecastBench和Polymarket-Sim上均取得最佳Brier分数，优于基于DeepSeek-V4-Pro（1.6T）、Kimi-K2.6（1T）和GPT-5.5的前沿代理。DeepForecaster-GRPO在FutureX指标上排名第二。消融实验表明，PBM即使在无训练情况下也能提升未训练的基础模型性能。校准分析显示，DeepForecaster-PPO在高置信度预测上比Qwen3.6和GPT-5.5更准确。轨迹分析进一步表明，随着代理收集证据并修正判断，预测质量从初始到最终信念状态普遍提升。

What Does Matching a Human Intervention Effect Establish? Benchmark Checks for LLM Simulations
匹配人类干预效果意味着什么？LLM模拟的基准检验
LLM simulations are validated by equivalence with human effects, yet a zero reference passes whenever the human 90 percent interval lies inside the margin, as the human benchmark shows before simulation. When the zero reference fails, planned precision gives independent simulations the margin up to which passing is a 5 percent test of response. In an exploratory 70-experiment reanalysis, 72 percent of GPT-4's $\pm$0.20 SD passes were zero-reference passes; GPT-4 passed 16 contrasts the zero reference failed (1.58 expected without response). In a preregistered audit, Claude Sonnet 5.5 passed three intention targets, all zero-reference passes, and the registered belief-gradient control; norm beliefs changed 0.065 SD against humans' 0.351 (zero-reference check added after unblinding; norm beliefs emphasized after inspection). Registered tests detected no overestimation; average-effect intervals excluded overestimation above 0.08 SD. A group audit's precision floor is 1.33 facts against a 2.9-fact effect. Report the zero-reference threshold beside every equivalence decision.
中文翻译
LLM模拟通过与人类效果等价来验证，但当人类90%置信区间位于容差范围内时，零参考检验总是通过，因为人类基准在模拟前已显示该范围。当零参考检验失败时，计划精度为独立模拟提供了上限，即通过测试仅需达到5%响应水平。在一项探索性70次实验的重新分析中，72%的GPT-4的±0.20标准差通过情况属于零参考通过；GPT-4在16个对比中通过而零参考未通过（无响应下预期为1.58）。在预注册审计中，Claude Sonnet 5.5通过了三个意图目标，均为零参考通过，并通过了注册的信念梯度控制；规范信念变化为0.065标准差，而人类为0.351（零参考检查在解盲后添加；规范信念在检查后强调）。注册测试未检测到高估；平均效应区间排除了高于0.08标准差的高估。群体审计的精度下限为1.33个事实，而实际效应为2.9个事实。报告每个等价决策时应附带零参考阈值。

The Vulnerability Window: Decision Instability in Quantized Reward Models
脆弱窗口：量化奖励模型中的决策不稳定性
Reward models and LLM judges are routinely quantized to 4 bits so that scoring many candidates stays affordable, and the usual check is that benchmark accuracy barely moves. That check is close to blind. Across eight reward models from two families (0.6B-8B), three generative judges and four 4-bit quantizers, quantization rewrites 1.1-12.3% of pairwise preference decisions on RewardBench while aggregate accuracy moves by as little as 0.03 points (p = 1.00), because the errors it fixes and introduces cancel. Flips concentrate where the full-precision margin is small relative to the quantization noise scale sigma: a Gaussian approximation Phi(-|m|/sigma) describes the aggregate rate, over-predicting it in all 30 scalar settings and failing for generative judges, and occupancy of the resulting vulnerability window falls from 25% to 7% with model size, which is what makes small judges fragile. The churn stays within noise under benign best-of-16 and becomes clear under adversarial pressure for some quantizers (146:54 harmful:helpful flips on one family, reversing sign on another). Downstream it changes what ships: across six (model, quantizer) pairs from two families, 14.6-65.7% of problems get a different best-of-64 selection while only 2.6-9.6% get a different final answer, with selections changing 5-8x more often than answers in every pair, and the pair the benchmark calls identical (0.03 points, p = 1.00) still changes 34.5% of them. Benchmark churn ranks that downstream change (rho = 0.89); benchmark accuracy does not order it reliably. Held-out audits then locate the useful diagnostic in the margin rather than the Gaussian: every margin-based predictor orders flips identically (AUC 0.927), and on the level a directly measured churn rate wins in-distribution. As a routing signal it is matched by plain top-k. The contribution is the characterization, not the model.
中文翻译
奖励模型和LLM裁判通常被量化至4比特以保持大量候选评分的可负担性，常规检查是基准准确性几乎不变。然而这种检查近乎盲视。在来自两个系列（0.6B-8B）的八个奖励模型、三个生成式裁判和四个4比特量化器中，量化在RewardBench上重写了1.1%-12.3%的成对偏好决策，而聚合准确性仅下降0.03点（p=1.00），因为其修复和引入的错误相互抵消。错误集中出现在全精度边际相对于量化噪声尺度σ较小时：高斯近似Phi(-|m|/sigma)描述了总体翻转率，在所有30个标量设置中均高估，对生成式裁判则失效；随着模型规模增大，由此产生的脆弱窗口占用率从25%降至7%，这正是小模型脆弱的原因。在良性条件下（best-of-16），波动保持在噪声范围内；但在对抗压力下，某些量化器出现明显波动（某一类中146:54有害:有益翻转，另一类中符号反转）。下游影响显著：在六个（模型, 量化器）组合中，14.6%-65.7%的问题获得不同的best-of-64选择，而仅有2.6%-9.6%获得不同最终答案，选择变化频率是答案变化的5-8倍，即使基准称其相同（0.03点，p=1.00），仍有34.5%的选择发生变化。基准波动与下游变化相关（rho=0.89）；基准准确性则无法可靠排序。保留审计发现，边际而非高斯分布才是有用的诊断信号：每个基于边际的预测器均一致排序翻转（AUC 0.927），且直接测量的波动率在分布内表现最优。作为路由信号，其性能与简单top-k相当。贡献在于表征，而非模型本身。
👤 Omkar Shewale, Deepak Kumar, Divakar Kumar Yadav 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

ORION: Exact Canonical-State Supervision for Systematic Generalization in Transformers
ORION：Transformer系统泛化中的精确规范状态监督
Autoregressive Transformers generally fail on out-of-distribution reasoning due to two primary reasons: positional corruption over long sequences and structural state drift across deep execution steps. We introduce ORION, a framework that supervises exact canonical execution states via auxiliary heads to anchor latent trajectories to valid computational manifolds. Across Folding, GridShift, and CLRS-30, a pre-registered 2 x 2 factorial shows that RoPE and ORION address orthogonal axes. RoPE recovers length extrapolation (59.8%), while ORION recovers depth extrapolation (66.7%). Moreover, their combination complementarily achieves strict additive gain in joint length-depth extrapolation, while either method alone offers near satisfactory results. Negative controls confirm that the gain depends strictly on exact canonical alignment; hashed state statistics fail to constrain depth extrapolation.
中文翻译
自回归Transformer在分布外推理中通常表现不佳，主要原因有两个：长序列中的位置信息污染以及深层执行步骤中的结构状态漂移。我们提出ORION框架，通过辅助头监督精确的规范执行状态，以锚定潜在轨迹至有效的计算流形。在Folding、GridShift和CLRS-30任务中，一个预先注册的2×2因子设计表明，RoPE和ORION分别解决正交维度：RoPE恢复长度外推能力（59.8%），而ORION恢复深度外推能力（66.7%）。此外，二者结合实现了联合长度-深度外推的严格加性增益，而单独使用任一方法已能提供接近满意的结果。负向对照实验确认，该增益严格依赖于精确的规范对齐；哈希状态统计无法约束深度外推。

From Letter-of-Credit Requirements to Executable Specifications: Evaluating Verification Accuracy and Completeness
从信用证要求到可执行规范：评估验证准确性和完整性
In letter-of-credit (LC) document examination, a correct final decision does not show that every requirement was examined. We therefore evaluate examination requirement by requirement and make each requirement executable. A language model converts the bill of lading (BL) and commercial invoice (IV) requirements of a credit into atomic conditions and logical structures, and a deterministic verifier retrieves the named evidence and computes each outcome, so every check can be compared with a gold encoding and traced to logged evidence. Because LC records are confidential, our on-premises 9B parser is fine-tuned only on synthetic pairs built from templates and human-reviewed public document values. On 345 real LC cases, final-decision accuracy proves misleading: a constant PASS rule has the highest raw accuracy while examining nothing. Although it never sees a real record during training, the fine-tuned parser detects 93% of the atomic requirements its ontology covers and reconstructs 86% of the covered requirements exactly. It verifies 82% of the evaluable requirements correctly, against 54% for a tool-using agent, and leads the agent on both satisfied requirements and discrepancies. It also completely examines 1.8 times as many cases as few-shot prompting of the same model, while direct prediction and the agent complete none. Executing the gold encodings with the same verifier shows that some of the remaining errors, missed discrepancies included, come from the operators and normalization, which a better parser alone cannot fix. Every rejection comes with a trace of the units and evidence behind it, which suits examiner-in-the-loop review.
中文翻译
在信用证（LC）单据审核中，最终决策正确并不意味着每一项要求都已被检查。因此，我们逐项评估审查要求，并使每项要求可执行。语言模型将信用证下的提单（BL）和商业发票（IV）要求转化为原子条件和逻辑结构，确定性验证器检索命名证据并计算每项结果，使得每一项检查均可与黄金标注对比并追溯至日志证据。由于LC记录具有保密性，我们的本地9B解析器仅在基于模板构建的合成数据对和人工审核的公开文档值上进行微调。在345个真实LC案例中，最终决策准确率具有误导性：恒定PASS规则具有最高原始准确率，却未检查任何内容。尽管训练中从未见过真实记录，微调后的解析器检测到了其本体覆盖的93%原子要求，并精确重构了86%的覆盖要求。它正确验证了82%的可评估要求，优于使用工具的代理（54%），并在满足要求和差异发现方面领先于代理。其检查案例数是同模型少样本提示的1.8倍，而直接预测和代理均未完成任何案例。使用相同验证器执行黄金标注显示，部分剩余错误（包括遗漏的差异）源于操作者和归一化问题，仅靠更好解析器无法解决。每次拒绝均附带相关单元和证据溯源，适合审查员参与式复核。

Run One, Get Two: Near-Free Streaming Safety Checks for Local LLMs
运行一次，获得两次：面向本地大模型的近零成本流式安全检查
Local LLM assistants decode for one user at a time, so decoding is bound by memory bandwidth and leaves compute idle. Streaming safety checks today trade accuracy against cost. A dedicated guard model is accurate but nearly doubles the latency per token, while a hidden-state probe is cheap but less accurate. We present ToRA, an adapter that runs a second model as an extra row in the generator's decode step, sharing its weights and KV cache. Dual-row kernels read each weight once for both rows. On Qwen3-0.6B, ToRA matches or beats a dedicated streaming guard, with higher F1 on 12 of 17 benchmarks. It detects 11.8 points more unsafe responses than the probe at a similar cost, under 4% added latency per token.
中文翻译
本地大模型助手一次仅服务一个用户，解码过程受限于内存带宽，导致计算资源闲置。当前的流式安全检查在准确性和成本之间权衡。专用守护模型准确但几乎使每token延迟翻倍，而隐藏状态探测器成本低但准确性较差。我们提出ToRA，一种适配器，在生成器解码步骤中以额外行运行第二个模型，共享权重和KV缓存。双行核每次读取权重一次即可服务于两行。在Qwen3-0.6B模型上，ToRA的表现匹配或超越专用流式守护模型，在17个基准测试中的12个上取得更高F1值。它在相似成本下比探测器多检测出11.8个百分点的不安全响应，每token增加延迟低于4%。

Right Numbers, Wrong Story: When LLMs Narrate Financial Changes Beyond Their Inputs

A financial report drafted by an LLM can state every number correctly but still go beyond its input, adding judgments and causes that no one supplied. We call these additions unsupported framing and find them far more often in LLM drafts than in reports written by proInput facts Revenue +3% Higher prices 10-K filings Revenue increased 3% on higher prices. 10-K writer Revenue surged 3% on higher prices, due to strong demand. LLM Fits the size? Surged: Unusual for 3% In the input? In the input? Strong demand: Not in input Higher prices: Kept fessionals, and attempts to fix them by prompting with how each word is used instead make such judgments more frequent. No extra model call Judgment word Unsupported cause Supported cause tween the LLM and human review to keep each draft within its input. Specifically, FrameCheck 1) guides the draft with professional examples that report a change of similar magnitude, 2) flags judgments that professionals would not use and causes the input does not give, and 3) removes them by rule, without a model call. In experiments on 17 open LLMs and 3M drafts, FrameCheck removes almost all unsupported framing from drafts and brings the judgment rate close to that of the writers of 10-K filings, the annual reports that US firms file with regulators.
