A financial report drafted by an LLM can state every number correctly but still go beyond its input, adding judgments and causes that no one supplied. We call these additions unsupported framing and find them far more often in LLM drafts than in reports written by proInput facts Revenue +3% Higher prices 10-K filings Revenue increased 3% on higher prices. 10-K writer Revenue surged 3% on higher prices, due to strong demand. LLM Fits the size? Surged: Unusual for 3% In the input? In the input? Strong demand: Not in input Higher prices: Kept fessionals, and attempts to fix them by prompting with how each word is used instead make such judgments more frequent. No extra model call Judgment word Unsupported cause Supported cause tween the LLM and human review to keep each draft within its input. Specifically, FrameCheck 1) guides the draft with professional examples that report a change of similar magnitude, 2) flags judgments that professionals would not use and causes the input does not give, and 3) removes them by rule, without a model call. AstronautBench: Measuring Agent Self-Improvement Across Episodes Through Knowledge, Evidence, and Progress
AstronautBench：通过知识、证据与进展衡量智能体在多轮中的自我改进
LLM agents are expected to improve themselves from their own experience across episodes, with fixed weights. Self-improvement in an embodied world cannot be read from one measure. Existing benchmarks measure task progress, world-knowledge question answering (QA), and episodic memory, but a correct answer does not show that the agent ever observed the evidence for it, and progress does not show what the agent learned. We introduce AstronautBench, which measures one agent's self-improvement through three views on the same run: what it knows, whether its own evidence supports that knowledge, and how far it gets in the world. It is built on the game Outer Wilds, where each episode is a 22-minute loop after which the world resets, only the agent's knowledge carries over, and progress is gated by knowledge. After every episode, we measure the places reached and ship-log rumor facts revealed, the correctness of answers to 164 questions about the world, and whether each answer is supported by the frames and position the agent cites from its own recorded play. Two LLM judges from different model families check every answer. In eight-episode baselines with GPT-6.1 Sol and Gemini 4 Argon planners, which act through a learned controller or their own gamepad inputs, the three views do not move together, so any one of them alone gives an incomplete picture of whether the agent is improving. The GPT-6.1 Sol agent's correct answers grow from 14.6% to 26.8% of all questions, but at episode 8 only 59 to 68% of them are supported by its cited evidence. It reveals no new rumor facts after episode 3, while the places it reaches and its memory keep growing. More than half of the answers that become correct after episode 3 cite only evidence from earlier episodes.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）代理应在固定权重下通过自身经历在多轮中实现自我改进。在具身世界中，无法仅通过单一指标判断自我改进情况。现有基准测试主要衡量任务进展、世界知识问答（QA）和回合记忆，但正确答案并不表明代理曾观察到支持它的证据，而进展也无法反映代理真正学到了什么。我们引入AstronautBench，通过三个视角在同一运行中衡量一个代理的自我改进：它知道什么、其自身证据是否支持该知识、以及它在世界中取得了多大进展。该基准基于游戏《Outer Wilds》，其中每轮为22分钟循环，之后世界重置，仅代理的知识保留，而进展受知识限制。每轮结束后，我们测量到达的位置和船载日志谣言事实的揭示情况、对164个关于世界的问题的回答正确性，以及每个回答是否由代理引用的自身录制回放中的帧和位置所支持。两名来自不同模型家族的LLM裁判检查每个答案。在八轮基线实验中，使用GPT-6.1 Sol和Gemini 4 Argon规划器（分别通过学习控制器或自身游戏手柄输入行动），这三个视角并未同步变化，因此任一单独视角都无法完整反映代理是否在改进。GPT-6.1 Sol代理的正确答案比例从14.6%提升至26.8%，但在第8轮仅有59%至68%的答案得到其引用证据的支持。它在第3轮后未揭示任何新谣言事实，而其到达位置和记忆仍在增长。超过一半在第3轮后变正确的答案仅引用了早期轮次的证据。

Perceive Once, Escalate on Demand: Handing Off Visual KV Memory from Small to Large VLMs
感知一次，按需升级：将小型VLM的视觉KV记忆传递给大型VLM
An embodied agent cannot afford a large vision-language model (VLM) at every step of a long video, so a cascade runs a small VLM continuously and escalates only uncertain decisions to a large one. Each escalation, however, forces the large model to re-encode a history the small model has already perceived, and this dominates its cost. We present Baton, which hands the large model the small model's visual memory, its key-value (KV) cache over the history. Baton rests on one finding: the large model needs a usable memory, not a faithful copy of its own. A map trained to reproduce the large model's cache matches it most closely, yet brings no gain over the small model alone. Baton instead distills a per-layer linear map, without labels, from the large model's own answers, with the large model encoding only the current frame. Interventions on the map trace the gain to one direction, carried by the middle third of the large model's layers. On RoboMME, Baton beats the small model alone, a pruned or shortened re-watch, and a text memory; as the escalation channel of a cascade it matches re-watching to within about one point at 28-47% less compute, and 8x less per escalation at 64 frames. Its gain grows with the large model, from 8B to 32B, tracks the large model's headroom across eight model pairs from four families, and transfers zero-shot to OpenEQA.
中文翻译
一个具身智能体无法在长视频的每一步都使用大型视觉语言模型（VLM），因此采用级联架构持续运行小型VLM，并仅在决策不确定时升级到大型模型。然而，每次升级都会迫使大型模型重新编码小型模型已感知的历史内容，这成为其主要开销。我们提出Baton，将小型模型的视觉记忆（即对历史的键值缓存）直接传递给大型模型。Baton基于一个发现：大型模型需要的是可用的记忆，而非自身完整忠实的副本。训练一个映射网络以复现大型模型的缓存，尽管匹配度最高，但并未带来性能提升。Baton则通过无标签的方式，从大型模型自身的回答中蒸馏出每层的线性映射，且大型模型仅需编码当前帧。对映射的干预分析表明，性能增益来自大型模型中间三分之一层所携带的方向信息。在RoboMME基准上，Baton优于仅使用小型模型、剪枝或缩短重看、以及文本记忆的方法；作为级联中的升级通道，其在计算量减少28%-47%的情况下，性能与重看相当，且每次升级的计算量降低8倍（在64帧场景下）。该方法的增益随大型模型规模增长（从8B到32B），在四个模型家族的八组对比中均与大型模型的潜力保持一致，并可零样本迁移至OpenEQA。
👤 Qiang Zhou, Jianing Deng, Siyan Dong, Jingxian Chen, Han-Yi Lin et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator
TOUMAII：一个450万对工具调用偏好数据集及其源无关生成器
To autonomously execute multi-step tasks, enterprise agents must reliably interact with tools, call APIs, or query databases, making tool-calling a fundamental capability of AI models. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) optimises for producing the correct call, but never contrasts it with a plausible incorrect one. In an environment where even a minor formatting error can invalidate a call and an incorrect decision can derail the interaction, direct preference optimisation (DPO) targets exactly this contrast. DPO, therefore, requires preference pairs rather than plain demonstrations. For tool-calling, such data, especially in realistic multi-turn settings, remains extremely scarce. To address this scarcity, we introduce TOUMAII, a large-scale tool-calling preference dataset containing ~4.5M validated preference pairs, derived from Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M (apache-2.0) conversations. The dataset consists of multi- and single-turn interactions and includes parallel and sequential function calls. Additionally, we release TOUMAII-Gen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that consumes tool-calling SFT datasets and generates a preference dataset with naturally occurring model-generated tool-calling failure modes. To validate the effectiveness of our DPO data, we fine-tune a 3-billion-parameter model, achieving performance parity with other high-quality tool-calling preference datasets. Furthermore, we release a second small preference dataset derived from ToolACE (apache-2.0), showcasing the source-agnostic nature of our pipeline. We release the complete code and datasets: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen and https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets
中文翻译
为了自主执行多步骤任务，企业级智能体必须可靠地与工具交互、调用API或查询数据库，因此工具调用是AI模型的核心能力。监督微调（SFT）优化的是生成正确调用，但从不将其与可能的错误调用进行对比。在环境中，即使微小的格式错误也可能使调用失效，错误决策可能导致交互失败，因此直接偏好优化（DPO）恰好针对这种对比。DPO需要偏好对而非普通示范数据。然而，尤其是在真实多轮场景下，工具调用的偏好数据仍极度稀缺。为解决这一问题，我们引入TOUMAII，一个大规模工具调用偏好数据集，包含约450万条经验证的偏好对，源自Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M（Apache-2.0）对话。该数据集涵盖单轮与多轮交互，包括并行与串行函数调用。此外，我们发布了TOUMAII-Gen，一个合成数据生成管道，可接收工具调用SFT数据集，生成包含自然发生的模型调用失败模式的偏好数据集。为验证DPO数据的有效性，我们微调了一个30亿参数模型，性能达到与其他高质量工具调用偏好数据集相当的水平。同时，我们还发布了一个从ToolACE（Apache-2.0）衍生的较小偏好数据集，展示了我们管道的源无关特性。完整代码与数据集已开源：https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen 和 https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets。
👤 Moritz Pötzsch, Andrea Gentilini, Lisa Rossgoderer, Tom Osterfeld, Kesava Prasad Sanjivi Arul et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

Adding One More Agent: How AI Team Size and Formation Shape Productivity and Diversity in Human-AI Brainstorming Teams
再多一个智能体：AI团队规模与构成如何塑造人机头脑风暴团队的生产力与多样性
Generative AI has improved individual productivity, but whether adding a plurality of AI partners helps or hurts remains open. We crossed AI partnership (one versus two) with behavioral formation (calibrated to average human behavior versus customized by the participant) in a brainstorming experiment ($N$=168) with human-human dyads as reference. Two-AI rooms posted more ideas to the shared board, but human-authored ideas dropped substantially. With one AI partner, formation mattered: humans paired with an average-calibrated AI posted more than any other condition. With two, this effect vanished. Humans continued talking but converted fewer ideas into board posts. AI social talk predicted more human posts, while AI posting volume predicted fewer. AI presence increased global diversity of human-authored ideas, but only a second AI increased within-session differentiation. The number of AI partners and their formation can reshape what the human contributes; single-agent results do not generalize to multi-agent teams.
中文翻译
生成式AI提升了个体生产力，但增加多个AI伙伴是否有助于或损害效率仍存在争议。我们在一项头脑风暴实验中（N=168），交叉考察了AI合作模式（一人 vs 两人）与行为形成方式（基于平均人类行为校准 vs 由参与者自定义）。结果显示，双AI房间提交的想法更多，但人类原创想法显著下降。当只有一个AI伙伴时，形成方式有影响：人类与平均校准的AI配对时产生的想法最多；但在双AI情况下，该效应消失。人类继续交流，但将想法转化为共享板上的数量减少。AI社交性话语预测更多人类投稿，而AI发帖量则预测更少。AI的存在增加了人类原创想法的全局多样性，但只有第二个AI才能提升会话内的差异化程度。AI伙伴的数量及其形成方式会重塑人类贡献的内容；单智能体的结果无法推广至多智能体团队。
👤 Rong-Ching Chang, Hao-Chuan Wang 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

MemOnce: A Scalable Memory Architecture for Large Language Models
MemOnce：一种适用于大语言模型的可扩展记忆架构
Deploying large language models (LLMs) in long-horizon and continual interactions requires maintaining reusable memory states for future inference, posing challenges for existing architectures. Standard Transformers accumulate layer-wise, token-level KV caches, incurring linear GPU memory growth and quadratic computational complexity as the context expands. In contrast, linear-time architectures compress history into fixed-size states, but struggle to retain fine-grained details. We introduce MemOnce, a foundation architecture that learns scalable memory states for language models. MemOnce is based on two core designs: (a) Memory encoder-decoder: it caches only a small subset of final-layer hidden states as compact memory representations and dynamically decodes them back into KV caches for attention layers; (b) Memory sparse attention: for each continual input, MemOnce adaptively retrieves relevant memory representations, with the sparse indices computed only once across layers. Coupling these two designs enables efficient on-the-fly memory loading and KV cache decoding, drastically reducing the GPU memory footprint and achieving linear-time complexity. We conduct extensive experiments to show that MemOnce outperforms Transformers and other architectures across varying model sizes, context lengths, and training paradigms (pretraining and post-training adaptation). In particular, adapting Qwen3.5-4B with MemOnce reduces memory state cache by 1313$\times$ and prefill latency by 6.9$\times$ at 1M context, and improves throughput by 3.5$\times$ and overall memory performance by +5.77%. Finally, MemOnce generalizes zero-shot to downstream representation tasks, achieving competitive results against specialized models.
中文翻译
在长时程和持续交互场景中部署大语言模型（LLMs）需要维持可复用的记忆状态以供未来推理，这对现有架构提出了挑战。标准Transformer通过逐层、逐token地累积KV缓存，导致GPU内存随上下文增长呈线性增加，计算复杂度呈二次增长。相比之下，线性时间架构将历史信息压缩为固定大小的状态，但难以保留细粒度细节。我们提出MemOnce，一种为语言模型学习可扩展记忆状态的基础架构。MemOnce基于两个核心设计：(a) 记忆编码器-解码器：仅缓存最终层隐藏状态的一个小子集作为紧凑的记忆表示，并动态将其解码回注意力层的KV缓存；(b) 记忆稀疏注意力：对于每个持续输入，MemOnce自适应地检索相关记忆表示，且稀疏索引在整个层间仅计算一次。结合这两项设计，实现了高效的即时记忆加载与KV缓存解码，显著降低GPU内存占用并实现线性时间复杂度。我们进行了大量实验，结果表明MemOnce在不同模型规模、上下文长度和训练范式（预训练与后训练适配）下均优于Transformer及其他架构。特别地，使用MemOnce适配Qwen3.5-4B模型，在1M上下文下使记忆状态缓存减少1313倍，预填充延迟降低6.9倍，吞吐量提升3.5倍，整体内存性能提高5.77%。最后，MemOnce在零样本迁移下游表征任务中表现优异，达到与专用模型相当的水平。
👤 Chang Nie, Chaoyou Fu, Songlin Liu, Yiqing Hu, Caifeng Shan 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

Enhancing Attention with Higher-Order Attention
通过高阶注意力增强注意力机制
Current decoder large language models rely on a combination of attention, feed forward and residual layers. The attention used is autoregressive (AR) though this is not strictly correct, as we note. That motivates the introduction of other attention methods that, while also not strictly AR, do enhance the AR character. One is based on a decomposition of mutual information (MI) into a set of Conditional MIs (CMIs) that correlate key tokens with pairs of query tokens. A second, higher-order attention, introduces correlations between contiguous key and query tokens. A third introduces correlations between a set of key and query tokens and uses a Generalized Maximum Entropy Principle to obtain an attention expression spanning a sequence of tokens. All these methods may enhance the information content of the autoregressive probabilities that are used in inference/generation. Applications to contrastive decoding and a systematic analysis of autoregressive probabilities are presented.
中文翻译
当前解码器型大语言模型依赖于注意力、前馈和残差层的组合。所使用的注意力机制虽称为自回归（AR），但严格来说并不准确，这促使我们引入其他非严格自回归但能增强自回归特性的注意力方法。第一种方法基于互信息（MI）分解为一组条件互信息（CMIs），将关键标记与查询标记对相关联；第二种高阶注意力引入连续关键标记与查询标记之间的相关性；第三种方法则引入一组关键与查询标记间的关联，并利用广义最大熵原理推导出跨越序列标记的注意力表达式。这些方法均可增强用于推理/生成的自回归概率的信息含量。文中还展示了其在对比解码中的应用以及对自回归概率的系统性分析。

AutoOntologyBench: A Task-Grounded Benchmark for Agentic Knowledge Engineering
AutoOntologyBench：面向智能体知识工程的任务导向基准测试
Foundation-model agents are increasingly extending beyond software engineering (SWE) into autonomous scientific research, where acquiring and integrating domain knowledge remains a critical challenge. Recent industrial advances have increasingly adopted knowledge graphs and ontology-based semantic layers to organize heterogeneous knowledge into reusable representations. We ask: Can autonomous agents construct ontologies that help them solve real tasks, and do they offer measurable benefits over alternative knowledge representations? Existing benchmarks evaluate ontology correctness or agent task success in isolation, leaving the value of autonomously acquired knowledge unresolved. We therefore introduce AutoOntologyBench, a task-grounded benchmark comprising 19,947 audited tasks grounded in 18 real-world knowledge sources across 7 domains, with source-native verification. Spanning Application, Repair, Organization, and Construction, AutoOntologyBench compares nine frontier-model agents under matched knowledge representations. Layered on retrieval, agent-built ontologies add 6.1 points of task success, yet trail an expert-derived reference by 13.0, identifying autonomous knowledge acquisition as the bottleneck to scalable scientific research. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/VinyehShaw/AutoOntologyBench.
中文翻译
基础模型智能体正逐步从软件工程扩展到自主科学研究领域，其中获取和整合领域知识仍是关键挑战。近期工业进展越来越多地采用知识图谱和基于本体的语义层，将异构知识组织为可复用的表示形式。我们提出问题：自主智能体能否构建有助于解决实际任务的本体？这种本体相较于其他知识表示形式是否具有可衡量的优势？现有基准仅孤立评估本体正确性或智能体任务成功率，未能揭示自主获取知识的实际价值。因此，我们提出了AutoOntologyBench，这是一个任务导向的基准，包含19,947个经审核的任务，覆盖7个领域的18个真实知识源，并支持源生验证。该基准涵盖应用、修复、组织与构建四类任务，对九种前沿模型智能体在相同知识表示下进行比较。结果显示，在检索基础上，由智能体构建的本体使任务成功率提升6.1分，但仍比专家构建的参考本体低13.0分，表明自主知识获取是实现可扩展科学研究的主要瓶颈。代码与资源已公开于https://github.com/VinyehShaw/AutoOntologyBench。
👤 Minye Shao, Chenghao Xiao, Xingyue Zhao, Yang Long 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv

Alliance Rupture and Repair in Simulating Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Dialogues
模拟认知行为疗法对话中的联盟破裂与修复
In cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT), routine work can strain the client-counselor working alliance, and the counselor must then notice the rupture, repair it, and adapt the work that follows. Existing CBT simulators model client resistance and follow structured treatment plans, but rarely track alliance state or allow counselors to repair ruptures over multiple turns and revise their plans accordingly. We present ALLY, a simulation framework that brings alliance rupture, repair, and replanning into ongoing CBT dialogue. Each client has a hidden alliance state that governs how openly the client engages. Like a real counselor, the counselor agent sees only the dialogue. It must notice when the client pulls back or pushes against the work, repair the rupture, and replan the rest of the session around what it has learned. Automatic evaluation shows that ALLY's counselors are closer to real CBT counselors than those of existing public synthetic datasets in strategy flow and emotional dynamics, and that ALLY is rated highest overall in counseling quality (CTRS) and interpersonal skill (FIS-T). An expert evaluation with 20 licensed therapists also finds the simulated ruptures plausible and the repairs and replanning largely appropriate.
中文翻译
在认知行为疗法（CBT）中，常规治疗过程可能对来访者与咨询师之间的协作联盟造成压力，咨询师必须察觉到这种破裂、进行修复，并调整后续工作。现有的CBT模拟器通常只建模来访者的抗拒并遵循结构化治疗方案，但很少追踪联盟状态，也难以让咨询师在多轮对话中修复破裂并相应地修改计划。我们提出了ALLY，一个将联盟破裂、修复和重新规划融入持续CBT对话的模拟框架。每位来访者都有一个隐藏的联盟状态，决定其参与的开放程度。如同真实咨询师，咨询代理只能看到对话内容，必须察觉到来访者退缩或对抗治疗时的情况，进行修复，并根据所获信息重新规划会话剩余部分。自动评估显示，ALLY的咨询师在策略流程和情感动态方面更接近真实CBT咨询师，且在咨询质量（CTRS）和人际技能（FIS-T）上整体评分最高。20位持证治疗师的专家评估也认为模拟的破裂合理，修复与重规划基本恰当。

Typhoon OCR 2: From Thai Document Transcription to Multilingual, Multi-Task OCR
Typhoon OCR 2：从泰语文档转录到多语言、多任务OCR
Typhoon OCR 1.5 focused on reading Thai documents. However, in real-world workflows, users often need to extract specific information or preserve the structure of an entire page for further processing. Typhoon OCR 2 handles both tasks in a single model, with prompt-driven extraction and structured full-page parsing. Full-page parsing covers Thai, English, and Chinese. We release compact 2B and 0.8B models adapted from open document parsers through supervised fine-tuning on curated Thai and public multilingual data, followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task rewards and a rule penalizing unfinished responses. Among evaluated open models, Typhoon OCR 2 2B scores highest on Thai MDPBench (80.7 versus 68.9 for Typhoon OCR 1.5) and on ThaiOCRBench (59.91 versus 40.45), which covers recognition, extraction, and document understanding. The 2B model remains close to its base on English olmOCR-bench (84.7 versus 84.4) and on OmniDocBench, which evaluates English and Chinese together (90.97 versus 91.03). On MDPBench, GRPO reduces the mean share of pages with repetition loops for the 2B model from 3.2% to 0.7%, with a smaller and statistically uncertain score gain for the 0.8B model. We release the model weights, Python package, and evaluation suite.
中文翻译
Typhoon OCR 1.5专注于泰语文档阅读。然而，在实际工作流中，用户往往需要提取特定信息或保留整页结构以供进一步处理。Typhoon OCR 2在单一模型中同时完成这两项任务，采用提示驱动的信息提取和结构化全页解析。全页解析涵盖泰语、英语和中文。我们发布了通过在精选泰语文档和公开多语言数据上进行监督微调，再经组相对策略优化（GRPO）并施加任务奖励及未完成响应惩罚规则训练得到的紧凑型2B和0.8B模型。在评估的开源模型中，Typhoon OCR 2 2B在泰语MDPBench（80.7 vs. 68.9）和泰语OCRBench（59.91 vs. 40.45）上得分最高，后者涵盖识别、提取和文档理解。2B模型在英文olmOCR-bench（84.7 vs. 84.4）和OmniDocBench（90.97 vs. 91.03）上仍保持接近基础模型的表现。在MDPBench上，GRPO将2B模型的重复循环页面平均占比从3.2%降至0.7%，0.8B模型的得分提升较小且统计上不显著。我们发布了模型权重、Python包和评估套件。

Silent Arbitration: How Large Language Models Resolve Co-Authoritative Conflicting Instructions
沉默仲裁：大语言模型如何解决共权威性冲突指令
Large language models increasingly receive high-level instructions about roles, values, interests, and communication style. Such instructions can conflict even when a prompt assigns them equal authority and provides no priority rule. We study co-authoritative persona conflict, in which a single prompt jointly assigns two opposing personas. Across eight conflict types grounded in published source material and five LLMs, we measure conflict acknowledgment, resolution strategy, and directional stance. Models acknowledge incompatibility in 72% of direct checks but explicitly surface it in only 22% of task responses, revealing silent arbitration. Dominance accounts for 45% of dual-persona responses, and 35% of compromise-labeled responses retain directional lean. Prompt framing further changes the visible form of arbitration substantially more than ordinary paraphrase variation. These findings show that evaluating instruction following should assess whether models disclose conflicts, how they resolve them, and which persona their responses favor.

大语言模型越来越多地接收关于角色、价值观、兴趣和沟通风格的高层指令。这些指令即使在提示中赋予同等权威且未提供优先规则的情况下也可能相互冲突。我们研究了共权威性人格冲突，即单个提示同时分配两个对立的人格。在基于已发表资料的八种冲突类型和五种LLM上，我们测量了冲突承认、解决策略和方向立场。模型在72%的直接检查中承认不相容性，但在任务响应中仅22%明确揭示冲突，显示出“沉默仲裁”现象。主导性解释了45%的双人格响应，35%的标为妥协的响应仍保留方向倾向。提示框架对仲裁可见形式的影响远大于普通改写变化。这些发现表明，评估指令遵循应考察模型是否披露冲突、如何解决以及其响应偏向哪个角色。

WaXup: Weight Averaging of Experts as Data Augmentation for Robust Introspection
WaXup：通过专家权重平均实现数据增强以提升鲁棒性自省
As the capabilities of large language models keep improving, traditional mechanistic interpretability methods struggle to keep pace. In contrast, trainable introspection methods emerge as a promising alternative; they repurpose the model itself as a self-diagnostic tool, trained to verbalize what it has learned during finetuning in natural language. While training on datasets of finetuned experts achieves encouraging results, it currently lacks robustness and generalization. Building on the data augmentation framework of MiXup, we introduce WaXup, a method that augments the introspection training set through linear interpolation in the weight space. This is motivated by the linear mode connectivity property: weight averaged experts interpolate the behaviors of the initial experts. We evaluate WaXup on three benchmarks: hidden-behavior models, a meta-dataset of real-world NLP tasks and AuditBench, a challenging alignment auditing suite of models adversarially trained to conceal their behaviors. WaXup improves the accuracy of generated descriptions as well as robustness to various distribution shifts. We hope to improve the reliability of trainable introspection methods towards better and safer models.

Breaking the Long Horizon: Short-Horizon Process Supervision for Long-Form Generation in LLMs
打破长时程限制：面向大语言模型长文本生成的短时程过程监督
Long-form generation is increasingly important for agentic LLM systems, yet current reinforcement learning methods typically optimize an entire document with a single outcome-level reward. This delayed and coarse-grained supervision makes it difficult to localize errors and assign credit across semantically distinct parts of the output. To address this, we propose a short-horizon framework that reformulates long-form generation through semantic decomposition into segment-level decisions. Each segment is generated with prefix-conditioned context, allowing cross-segment coherence to be preserved while keeping supervision local. The generated segments are then scored with rubric-based rewards for adherence, coherence, and relevance, enabling explicit local supervision at the segment level. These local rewards are used to compute step-wise advantages, yielding localized policy updates. To isolate the effect of supervision granularity, we instantiate both outcome-level supervision and our short-horizon process reward method (SH-PRM) under the same GRPO framework. Across multiple LLMs and two long-form generation benchmarks, SH-PRM consistently outperforms outcome-level supervision and strong inference-time baselines under the same optimizer and comparable budgets. Further analyses suggest that these gains primarily arise from improved credit assignment. Overall, our results suggest that aligning supervision granularity with the semantic structure of long-form generation is an effective design principle for long-form LLM training.
中文翻译
长文本生成对于智能体式大语言模型系统日益重要，但当前的强化学习方法通常仅使用单一结果级奖励来优化整个文档。这种延迟且粗粒度的监督使得难以定位错误并为输出中语义不同的部分分配责任。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种短时程框架，通过语义分解将长文本生成重构为分段级决策。每个段落均在前缀条件上下文下生成，从而在保持跨段落连贯性的同时使监督局部化。生成的各段落随后通过基于评分标准的奖励（遵循性、连贯性和相关性）进行评分，实现在分段层级上的显式局部监督。这些局部奖励用于计算逐步优势，进而实现局部策略更新。为隔离监督粒度的影响，我们在相同的GRPO框架下同时实例化了结果级监督与我们的短时程过程奖励方法（SH-PRM）。在多个大语言模型和两个长文本生成基准测试中，SH-PRM始终优于结果级监督及强推理时基线，且在相同优化器和相近预算下表现更佳。进一步分析表明，这些提升主要源于改进的责任分配。总体而言，我们的结果表明，将监督粒度与长文本生成的语义结构对齐，是长文本大语言模型训练的有效设计原则。

Reasoning in Fewer Tokens: Concept-Normalized Multilingual Scratchpads and Reversible Model-Adaptive Reasoning Codes
用更少的标记实现推理：概念归一化的多语言草稿板与可逆的模型自适应推理编码
Chain-of-thought prompting significantly improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) but incurs high inference costs due to lengthy autoregressive decoding. While previous efficiency approaches reduce costs by discarding tokens or utilizing continuous latent representations, these lossy methods fundamentally sacrifice interpretability and auditability. In this work, we introduce a novel compression framework for intermediate reasoning. We apply Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) to reasoning corpora to learn a compressed vocabulary of Private Use Area (PUA) tokens, each representing frequent semantic spans. After expanding the LLM's vocabulary, we fine-tune the model via strictly 1:1, our method is lossless. We evaluate our approach on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-14B, and Qwen2.5-32B across four diverse benchmarks. Our method reduces total generated tokens by 14.7% on average with reasoning trace lengths dropping by up to 27.1%, while incurring a negligible average accuracy cost of 1.6 percentage points. Crucially, this approach is orthogonal to existing lossy methods, allowing practitioners to stack lossless and irreversible compression techniques. We demonstrate that a compressed intermediate language provides a highly efficient computational resource that accelerates inference while fully preserving the transparency of LLM reasoning.

Do Language Models Know Which Words Steer Their Reasoning?
语言模型是否知道哪些词引导了其推理？
While chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting elicits intermediate reasoning steps from language models, it is unclear which words in the context actually steer the CoT. We identify Phantom Context Influence (PCI): language models are disproportionately steered by semantically irrelevant context words, even as the same models deny those words' relevance when asked. To localize PCI, we introduce Leave-One-Out Word JSD analysis, which removes one context word at a time from the otherwise intact context to isolate its marginal effect on the next-token distribution; words whose removal flips the prediction (pivot words) are passed to the same LLM to self-judge whether it recognizes this predictive dependence. Across four models and three benchmarks, language models largely do not know which words steer their reasoning, a mismatch we call the Generation-Judgment Contradiction. Inspired by this phenomenon, we propose Online KV Suppression: it scores every prompt word's influence over the whole chain of thought in a single pass, then zeroes the KV-cache entries of the highest-influence word the model itself judges inert and redecodes. This single-word intervention improves CoT accuracy across four models and five reasoning benchmarks, by up to 8.9 percentage points on math reasoning benchmark.
中文翻译
尽管思维链（CoT）提示能从语言模型中引出中间推理步骤，但尚不清楚上下文中的哪些词语实际上主导了CoT。我们识别出“幻觉上下文影响”（PCI）现象：语言模型在语义无关的上下文词影响下表现出显著偏差，即使在被询问时，同一模型也否认这些词的相关性。为定位PCI，我们引入“逐词剔除词JSD分析”，即每次从完整上下文中移除一个词，以孤立其对下一个词分布的边际影响；若移除某词导致预测反转（称为“枢纽词”），则将其传递给同一模型，由其自我判断是否意识到这种预测依赖性。在四个模型和三个基准测试中，语言模型大多不知道哪些词引导了其推理，这种不一致我们称之为“生成-判断矛盾”。受此现象启发，我们提出在线KV抑制：在单次遍历中评估每个提示词对整个思维链的影响，然后将模型自身判断为无影响的最高影响力词对应的KV缓存置零，并重新解码。这一单词干预在四个模型和五个推理基准测试中均提升了CoT准确率，数学推理基准测试最高提升达8.9个百分点。

Do Companies Deliver on Their IPO Claims?
公司是否兑现了其IPO承诺？
Initial public offering (IPO) filings describe what a company does and what it intends to become. Yet whether individual forward-looking claims are subsequently realized is difficult to assess. We introduce Vision Follow-Through (VFT), a benchmark and agentic pipeline that separates claim extraction, category assignment, verifiability, and evidence-grounded follow-through assessment. \vftclaims{} contains 5,066 human-annotated claim spans from 50 IPO filings, while \vftverify{} adds verifiability tiers, follow-through outcomes, and cited later evidence for 1,000 claims. Across language models, classifying human-annotated claims is substantially easier than extracting claims from full filings. Extraction coverage also varies sharply by category, with Organizational/Culture claims recovered far less often than Market Opportunity claims. Follow-through assessment poses a separate challenge: model judgments diverge from human assessments in different ways rather than exhibiting a single consistent bias. These results show that reliable assessment of corporate follow-through requires both finding the relevant claims and grounding subsequent judgments in later evidence.

首次公开募股（IPO）文件描述了公司的业务内容及其未来愿景。然而，评估这些前瞻性声明是否被实际实现却十分困难。我们提出了Vision Follow-Through（VFT），一个基准测试和智能体流水线，用于分离声明提取、类别分配、可验证性判断以及基于证据的后续评估。\vftclaims{}包含来自50份IPO文件的5,066个由人工标注的声明片段，而\vftverify{}则为其中1,000个声明添加了可验证性层级、后续结果及引用的后续证据。在不同语言模型中，对人工标注声明进行分类比从完整文件中提取声明要容易得多。此外，不同类别的声明提取覆盖率差异显著，组织/文化类声明的恢复率远低于市场机会类声明。后续评估则面临另一挑战：模型判断与人类评估的偏差方式各异，并非表现出单一一致的偏见。这些结果表明，可靠评估企业后续执行情况需要既能准确识别相关声明，又能将后续判断建立在后期证据之上。

LongCat Recall Sparse Attention: Co-Designing Key Organization and Retrieval for Long-Context Inference
LongCat召回稀疏注意力：针对长上下文推理的键组织与检索协同设计
Retrieval-based sparse attention, exemplified by DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA), reduces attention computation by selecting a small subset of key-value entries. However, selecting these entries can still require searching the entire visible history. For long sequences, retrieval becomes a major bottleneck, limiting the efficiency gains from sparse attention. To address this problem, we propose LongCat Recall Sparse Attention (LRSA), which combines fine-grained representatives with paged retrieval in an incrementally maintained index. LRSA partitions the sequence into time buckets that can be indexed independently as new keys arrive, without regrouping existing history. Within each bucket, it organizes indexing keys into capacity-balanced directional groups and packs a fixed number of complete groups into each page, retaining a separate center for every group. These centers provide fine-grained representatives for page selection, after which refinement scores members of the selected pages together with sink, sliding-window, and visible unindexed candidates to select the final Top-$K$ logical positions. To execute this retrieval process efficiently, we store members in reordered pages, fuse construction stages according to group size, and share storage for overlapping fixed candidates across queries during chunked prefill. These choices support regular member access while reducing kernel launches during construction and repeated candidate transfers. Moreover, we integrate LRSA into SGLang to maintain the index during inference, overlapping asynchronous index construction for new buckets with subsequent computation and reusing compatible bucket indices and member pages after a prefix match in the original key-value pool. With a 1024K-token context, LRSA accelerates key selection by 4.70$\times$ over DSA and combined selection and attention by 35.60$\times$ over full attention.
中文翻译
基于检索的稀疏注意力，如DeepSeek稀疏注意力（DSA），通过选择少量键值条目来减少注意力计算量。然而，这些条目的选择仍可能需要搜索整个可见历史。对于长序列而言，检索成为主要瓶颈，限制了稀疏注意力带来的效率提升。为解决此问题，我们提出LongCat召回稀疏注意力（LRSA），该方法结合细粒度代表与分页检索，并在增量维护的索引中实现。LRSA将序列划分为时间桶，新键到达时可独立索引，无需重新分组现有历史。每个桶内，将索引键组织成容量均衡的方向性组，并将固定数量的完整组打包到每一页，同时为每组保留单独的中心。这些中心作为页面选择的细粒度代表，随后对选定页面中的成员以及sink、滑动窗口和未索引的可见候选进行精炼评分，以选出最终的Top-$K$逻辑位置。为高效执行检索过程，我们将成员存储在重排的页面中，根据组大小融合构建阶段，并在分块预填充期间共享跨查询的重叠固定候选的存储空间。这些设计支持常规成员访问，同时减少了构建过程中的内核调用次数和重复候选传输。此外，我们将LRSA集成到SGLang中，在推理过程中维持索引，使新桶的异步索引构建与后续计算重叠，并在原始键值池前缀匹配后复用兼容的桶索引和成员页面。在1024K令牌上下文下，LRSA相比DSA将键选择加速4.70倍，相比全注意力将选择与注意力合并过程加速35.60倍。

CoPyro: Feeding Copy Proposals to Parallel Drafters
CoPyro：将复制建议输入并行草稿生成器
Speculative decoding accelerates language generation by letting a target model verify several draft tokens in one forward pass. Earlier neural drafters such as EAGLE extend each candidate sequence one token at a time. Recent work has turned to parallel drafters such as DFlash, which predict a whole block at once. Another approach uses n-gram retrieval to find an earlier occurrence of the current context suffix and copy the tokens that followed it directly into the draft. We introduce CoPyro, which feeds these copied tokens into a parallel drafter in place of masks. The drafter learns from context which tokens to keep and which to revise as it predicts the block in its usual forward pass. Across eight benchmarks on Qwen3-4B, CoPyro improves overall acceptance length for all four parallel drafters tested, with gains of 2.19-5.08% when trained on 100K samples. With DFlash trained on 710K samples, overall gains range from 1.08% to 2.00% across the tested model sizes and temperatures. Code generation benefits most in both settings. On Qwen3-8B, processing the proposals adds an average of 0.24 ms per decoding round in Transformers.

Explicit Style Extraction for Personalized Long-Form Text Generation
显式风格提取用于个性化长篇文本生成
Personalized long-form generation asks a language model to write new text the way a specific user would. The standard recipe, retrieval-augmented generation, places a few of the user's past texts in the prompt but leaves the model to decide what to take from them. We introduce StylePrism, a taxonomy of 28 stylistic aspects in seven categories, and SP-Refine, an inference-time method that makes style extraction an explicit step: the model first fills StylePrism with the user's patterns, quoted evidence, and habits to avoid, then drafts from the user's samples and revises the draft twice against this profile, guided by its own style scores. On the three long-form tasks of LongLaMP with three LLMs, SP-Refine outperforms LongLaMP's retrieval-augmented baseline in 25 of 27 ROUGE and METEOR comparisons, by 10.2% on average and by up to 35.8%. Annotating users' own texts with the extracted profiles shows that StylePrism captures concrete, checkable habits, from the order of rhetorical moves to a reviewer's sign-off. Ablations show that the profile helps as revision guidance, not as a replacement for the samples: writing from the profile alone is worse in all 27 comparisons, while a single implicit-inference call is a cheaper alternative that comes close. Oracle variants that let the reviser see the reference, together with reference-free controls, show that larger refinement gains require information unavailable at inference time.
中文翻译
个性化长篇生成要求语言模型以特定用户的方式撰写新文本。标准方法——检索增强生成，仅在提示中放入用户几篇过往文本，让模型自行决定从中提取什么。我们引入StylePrism，一个涵盖七个类别共28种风格特征的分类体系，以及SP-Refine，一种推理时的方法，将风格提取明确化为一个独立步骤：模型首先用用户的模式、引用证据和应避免的习惯填充StylePrism，然后基于用户样本起草文本，并两次依据该风格档案修订草稿，由模型自身的风格得分引导。在LongLaMP的三个长篇任务上，使用三种LLM测试，SP-Refine在27次ROUGE和METEOR比较中有25次优于LongLaMP的检索增强基线，平均提升10.2%，最高达35.8%。对用户自身文本进行标注并与提取的风格档案对比显示，StylePrism捕捉到了具体可验证的习惯，从修辞手段的顺序到审稿人的签退方式。消融实验表明，风格档案的作用在于作为修订指导，而非替代样本本身：仅凭档案写作在全部27次比较中表现更差；而一次隐式推理调用是一种更低成本的替代方案，效果接近。Oracle变体允许修订者看到参考文本，结合无参考控制实验表明，更大的改进需要推理时无法获取的信息。

How Much of a Calculation-Error Rate Is Not Arithmetic: Auditing the FinReason Cup 2026 Task 3 Metric with a Deterministic Rule System
计算错误率中有多少并非算术错误：使用确定性规则系统审计FinReason Cup 2026任务3的度量标准
Task 3 of the FinReason Cup 2026 scores a system on two values per case, the one an XBRL filing reports and the one its own calculation relationships imply, and labels each failure as structural, extraction or calculation. This report shows that the calculation-error rate so defined does not count arithmetic failures, and bounds how far it is from one using the published board alone. One of the three rules behind the benchmark, DQC_US_0015, has an answer that is the negation of the reported value (in all 110 published practice cases, against 1 of 120 and 6 of 102 for the other two), so a calculation label on such a case records a wrong fact or a wrong route, not a wrong sum. Subtracting the available structural and extraction labels from each entry's error count on that rule gives a floor: the bound is positive for 22 of the 23 ranked entries, and at the top it is sharpest, with at least 11 of 12 and 12 of 13 calculation errors of the two joint-first entries falling on cases where no arithmetic remained. The calculation-error rate of the best systems on this task is almost entirely not arithmetic. The vehicle for that result is an entry that reconstructs the three data-quality rules the benchmark is drawn from, DQC_US_0015, DQC_US_0117 and DQC_US_0126, as a deterministic system with no learned parameters, no training step and no external service, running all 680 cases in 1.4 seconds. It scores 96.62% under the offline judge on development and 95.29% on the published test set, and its residual error concentrates in cases where a concept carries several equally flagged facts and the question names no dimensional member. Reading those error classes against the per-rule accuracies is what exposed the metric, which is why both are reported here.
中文翻译
FinReason Cup 2026任务3对每个案例评估两个数值：一个来自XBRL文件报告，另一个由其自身的计算关系推导得出，并将每项失败标记为结构性、提取性或计算性。本报告表明，所定义的计算错误率并未包含算术错误，并仅通过公开榜单即可界定其与1之间的差距。基准测试背后的三个规则之一DQC_US_0015，其正确答案是报告值的否定（在全部110个已发布练习案例中，而其他两个规则分别为1/120和6/102），因此在该类案例中标记为计算错误，实际上记录的是错误事实或错误路径，而非错误求和。从每个条目的错误计数中减去可用的结构性和提取性标签，可得到下界：在23个排名条目中的22个中该下界为正，且在最高排名处最为显著——前两名联合第一的系统中，至少11/12和12/13的计算错误均发生在无需算术运算的案例上。该任务中最佳系统的计算错误率几乎完全不是算术错误。实现这一结果的工具是一个重建了基准测试所依据的三条数据质量规则（DQC_US_0015、DQC_US_0117和DQC_US_0126）的确定性系统，该系统无学习参数、无训练步骤、无外部服务，可在1.4秒内运行全部680个案例。它在开发集上的离线裁判得分达96.62%，在公开测试集上得分为95.29%；其残差错误集中在概念携带多个同等标记事实且问题未指定维度成员的案例中。通过将这些错误类别与各规则的准确率相对照，揭示了该度量标准的问题，因此两者均在此报告。

Co-Evolving Behavioral Agents and Detectors for Adaptive Information Operations
协同演化行为智能体与检测器以实现自适应信息操作
Detectors of state-sponsored information operations (IOs) are trained on campaigns that platforms have already taken down. They lose much of their accuracy on an actor they have not seen, even though no operator in the archived data is trying to avoid them. A real operator wants two things, to reach an audience and to avoid being caught. We present IO-AI, a framework that gives simulated agents these two capabilities, engagement and evasion, and tests what each one does to detection. The agents are built from the posting behavior of real campaigns and choose the emotion, type, and timing of each post. An agent and a detector then train against each other in turn. We rank the trained agents by how hard they are to detect and train one detector on them from easiest to hardest. On 20 Twitter IO campaigns, this detector reduces missed detections of adaptive agents by 65% relative to a detector trained only on the archived campaigns, at a similar false-alarm rate. On actors it has never seen it reaches an F1 of 0.81, against 0.62. We find that an agent trained to seek engagement becomes easier to catch, because it learns the fast, emotional retweeting that archived campaigns were caught producing. An agent that was trained for engagement before the game keeps its engagement lead after it learns to evade. We also introduce a behavioral distance metric that compares two groups of accounts using only their unlabeled posting histories. The larger the distance between two groups, the worse a detector trained on one does on the other (Spearman rho = -0.58), so the metric can be used to choose which new groups to test first.
中文翻译
针对国家支持的信息操作（IO）的检测器通常基于平台已下架的活动进行训练，因此在面对未曾见过的攻击者时准确性大幅下降，尽管档案数据中的操作员并未试图规避检测。真实操作者希望达成两个目标：触达受众并避免被发现。我们提出IO-AI框架，赋予模拟智能体“参与”和“规避”两种能力，并测试二者对检测的影响。智能体基于真实活动的发帖行为构建，自主选择每条帖子的情感、类型和发布时间。智能体与检测器交替进行对抗训练。我们根据智能体的检测难度对其进行排序，并训练一个检测器，从最易检测到最难检测的智能体逐步学习。在20个Twitter IO活动中，该检测器相对于仅在归档活动上训练的检测器，将自适应智能体的漏检率降低了65%，同时保持相似的误报率。对于从未见过的攻击者，其F1达到0.81，相较之下原检测器仅为0.62。研究发现，仅追求参与度的智能体会变得更容易被捕捉，因为它学会了档案中被发现的快速情绪化转发行为；而在博弈前即已训练参与度的智能体，在学会规避后仍能保持其参与优势。我们还引入一种行为距离度量，仅使用账户的未标注发帖历史来比较两组账户。两组间距离越大，一个在一组上训练的检测器在另一组上的表现越差（Spearman rho = -0.58），因此该度量可用于决定优先测试哪些新群体。

Separating Automation Gains from Agent Contributions in AI-Assisted Model Validation
在AI辅助模型验证中分离自动化收益与智能体贡献
LLM agents are being proposed for bank model validation, yet evaluations that compare agent-assisted work with manual practice credit the agent with gains that come from digitized evidence and deterministic tests. We propose a staged architecture in which an LLM-free controlled workflow (P1) is both the fallback and the control arm for individually authorized agent tasks (P2), and an attribution protocol that credits an agent only for faults P1 does not already report. The protocol charges decoy false alarms, unnecessary actions and endorsed developer assertions, counts invalid outputs as misses, folds newly automatable findings back into P1 with discovery credit, and fixes safety bounds and a break-even cost gate in advance. On a synthetic credit-scorecard harness, five scripted reference policies show why this matters: naive case accuracy (4/6) and pooled detection (3/5) tie four flawed policies, whereas the protocol separates all five and credits only the reference policy with detection beyond P1. One fold-back round raises a P1-parroting policy's naive scores without any change to the policy. A regression matrix of sixteen mutants covers nine ways in which earlier versions of the evaluation passed misleadingly. No LLM agent has been evaluated yet; we specify a falsifiable pilot.

How Far Does Lossless Re-tokenization Move an RLVR Update?
无损重分词对强化学习从可验证奖励更新的影响有多大？
Language models assign different probabilities to token sequences that decode to the same string. In reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR), a trainer that re-encodes sampled text may train on a different token sequence with the same reward. We ask how far this moves a training update, and whether the movement has a consistent direction. From one checkpoint, we compare updates on the same rollouts, canonical and with selected tokens split into two pieces, against an update on a different batch. On Qwen2.5-0.5B with three-digit addition, splitting about one token in 25 moves the update 0.556 times as far as replacing the batch. The ratio rises with the fraction split in four models and depends on which tokens are split; within a checkpoint, the displacements share a direction across batches. In a tabular model with one table per tokenization and a single sampled string, we prove that whenever two strings below an unsampled prefix differ in probability, only the zero full-gradient step agrees across tokenizations, however it is weighted. In an audit that decodes and re-encodes resampled rollouts, re-encoding touches up to 5.2% of rollouts but at most 0.08% of tokens, far below the split fractions we test. Training on fixed rollouts, the canonical arm finishes ahead in 14 of 18 pre-registered pairs; the interval for the mean accuracy difference includes zero over all pairs and excludes it only under the registered exclusion rule, which favours the canonical arm.
中文翻译
语言模型会对解码为相同字符串的不同词元序列分配不同的概率。在可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，重新编码采样文本的训练者可能基于具有相同奖励但不同词元序列进行训练。我们探讨这种变化对训练更新的影响程度及其方向是否一致。从一个检查点出发，我们比较了同一轮次上的更新：标准形式与选定词元拆分为两部分的形式，与另一批数据上的更新。在Qwen2.5-0.5B模型上进行三位数加法任务时，每25个词元拆分约1个，其更新移动距离相当于更换批次的0.556倍。该比例随拆分比例增加而上升，并且取决于被拆分的词元类型；在同一检查点内，各批次间的位移方向一致。在一个每个分词方式对应一张表、仅采样一个字符串的表格模型中，我们证明：只要两个低于未采样前缀的字符串在概率上存在差异，只有零梯度步长的全梯度更新在不同分词间保持一致，尽管其权重不同。在一次对重采样轮次进行解码和重编码的审计中，重编码影响最多达5.2%的轮次，但不超过0.08%的词元，远低于我们测试的拆分比例。在固定轮次训练下，标准分支在18组预注册配对中有14组领先；所有配对的均值准确率差异区间包含零，仅在注册排除规则下才不包含零，而该规则有利于标准分支。

ProbeAgent: Principled Diagnosis of Agent Failures in Verifiable Environments
ProbeAgent：可验证环境中的智能体失败原则性诊断
Improving agents requires discovering where they fail and understanding why. An agent acts over multiple steps in a changing environment, so diagnosing its failures requires more than inspecting the final response of a large language model (LLM). The same unsuccessful outcome can arise from task difficulty or from an error the agent repeats across tasks. We introduce ProbeAgent, an automated framework that discovers agent failures, tests their proposed causes, and identifies shared failure modes. Starting from benchmark tasks, ProbeAgent builds executable tests and validates them using reference solutions, automated checks, and review of the task requirements. For each failed trajectory, it explains the error and constructs new tasks that preserve the suspected cause while changing the surrounding context. Running the agent on these tasks tests whether the same error recurs. ProbeAgent then groups failures with similar causes and generates descriptions that distinguish their errors. This process connects explanations of individual failures to executable tests and descriptions of shared failure modes. We evaluate ProbeAgent across 22 agent configurations using tasks from 10 benchmarks, collecting 1,244 failures and producing 536 failure-mode descriptions. These descriptions distinguish specific errors within broad outcomes such as incomplete work, including planning without acting and stopping before delivering a file. In human evaluation, annotators accept 90.0% of assigned descriptions and correctly reject 83.3% of similar alternatives, supporting the accuracy and specificity of the discovered failure modes.
中文翻译
改进智能体需要发现其失败之处并理解原因。智能体在动态环境中多步行动，因此诊断其失败不能仅依赖大型语言模型（LLM）的最终输出。相同的失败结果可能源于任务难度，也可能源于智能体在多个任务中重复出现的错误。我们提出ProbeAgent，一个自动化框架，用于发现智能体失败、测试其假设原因，并识别共有的失败模式。从基准任务出发，ProbeAgent构建可执行测试，并通过参考解答、自动化检查和任务需求审查进行验证。针对每个失败轨迹，它解释错误并构造新任务，在保留疑似原因的同时改变上下文。在这些任务上运行智能体以检验相同错误是否重现。随后，ProbeAgent将具有相似原因的失败归类，并生成可区分其错误的描述。该过程将个别失败的解释与可执行测试及共享失败模式的描述相连接。我们在10个基准的22种智能体配置上评估ProbeAgent，收集了1,244个失败案例，并生成536个失败模式描述。这些描述能区分广义结果（如工作不完整）中的具体错误，包括只规划不执行、提前停止文件交付等。人工评估显示，标注者接受90.0%的分配描述，正确拒绝83.3%的类似替代项，支持所发现失败模式的准确性和特异性。

How Language Models Compress and Compare: Understanding Selection with Token Covariance Maps
语言模型如何压缩与比较：通过词元协方差图理解选择机制
Many language-model tasks reduce to selecting among structured alternatives, yet we still lack a simple picture of how a decoder-only transformer organizes an entire selection prompt across depth. We introduce token covariance maps, a prompt-level geometric view that tracks how token positions co-vary in hidden-state space at each layer. This turns a structured prompt into a depth-indexed sequence of maps whose spectral and block structure can be aligned with instructions, context, and candidate answers. Across 14 decoder-only models from 4 families and 12 structured-selection benchmarks, token covariance maps reveal a robust compress-then-compare organization. Early layers represent prompts diffusely, middle layers compress them into a shared low-rank bottleneck, and late layers re-expand into context-option and option-option geometry. This organization recurs across larger fixed-template samples, deterministic prompt rewrites, pairwise reranking reformulations, and controlled answer-set manipulations, while also emerging during training from an initially flat depth profile. Its timing aligns with behavior: useful answer discrimination appears well after the bottleneck, and low-rank intervention around the bottleneck preferentially disrupts late comparison geometry. We additionally show that the same geometric profile appears in instruction-tuned models, across several languages, and during open-ended generation. We further show that changing the internal representation of one candidate around the compressed region can shift which candidate the model later prefers. These results show that token covariance maps are a diagnostic tool for comparing how models organize information across prompts, model families, and stages of training.
中文翻译
许多语言模型任务可归结为在结构化选项中进行选择，但我们仍缺乏对仅解码器Transformer如何在深度维度上组织整个选择提示的直观理解。我们引入了词元协方差图，这是一种提示级别的几何视角，用于追踪每一层隐藏状态空间中词元位置的共变关系。这将一个结构化提示转化为一系列按深度索引的地图，其谱结构和块结构可与指令、上下文及候选答案对齐。在来自4个模型家族的14个仅解码器模型以及12个结构化选择基准上的实验表明，词元协方差图揭示了一种稳健的‘先压缩后比较’组织模式：早期层以弥散方式表示提示，中间层将其压缩至共享的低秩瓶颈，晚期层则重新展开为上下文-选项与选项-选项的几何结构。该组织模式在更大固定模板样本、确定性提示重写、成对重排序重构以及受控答案集操作中反复出现，并且在训练过程中从初始平坦的深度分布中自然涌现。其时间动态与行为一致：有用的答案区分出现在瓶颈之后，而在瓶颈附近进行低秩干预会优先破坏晚期比较几何结构。我们还发现，相同的几何特征也出现在指令微调模型中，跨越多种语言，并在开放式生成中显现。此外，改变压缩区域中某一候选项的内部表示，可导致模型后期偏好发生变化。这些结果表明，词元协方差图是一种诊断模型如何在不同提示、模型族和训练阶段组织信息的有效工具。
👤 Moshe Eliasof 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26863]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
LLMs Memorize Their Own Hallucinations During Post-Training
大语言模型在后训练期间记忆自身幻觉
Reasoning domains have shaped the post-training recipes of frontier models even though they are trained and deployed in domains beyond math and code. In this paper, we study the consequences of directly adopting reasoning-centric training recipes, e.g., the use of outcome-based rewards for reinforcement learning (RL), injection of "cognitive" behaviors such as proposing and backtracking from hypotheses in reasoning chains, etc., for knowledge tasks. We hypothesize that these decisions, while useful for reasoning, inadvertently encourage models to memorize hallucinations they themselves generate in their long reasoning chains when trained on knowledge tasks. To study this, we train models with standard post-training, extract their self-generated hallucinations during RL, and track memorization throughout the rest of training. Our experiments on six open models across two model families and two architectures confirm our hypothesis. We find that models' recall of hallucinations reinforced during training increases by 4.83% on average, compared to just 0.17% for self-hallucinations that receive no training signal. In fact, models even memorize false facts that they immediately retract in their reasoning; a backtracking template inherited from reasoning-centric training that has no benefit for knowledge recall. Furthermore, we find that memorization is strongest on the hardest training questions, and rewarding a hallucination makes the model less likely to abstain from it later (by 7.23% on average). Overall, our work identifies and systematically studies a previously unexamined pathway through which models learn hallucinations during training.
中文翻译
尽管前沿模型在数学和代码之外的领域进行训练和部署，但推理领域已深刻影响其后训练策略。本文研究直接采用以推理为中心的训练方法（如基于结果的强化学习奖励、在推理链中注入‘认知’行为如提出并回溯假设等）应用于知识任务的后果。我们假设，这些看似有益于推理的设计，却无意中促使模型在知识任务训练中记忆自身在长推理链中生成的幻觉。为验证此假设，我们在标准后训练流程下训练模型，提取强化学习阶段自动生成的幻觉，并在整个训练过程中追踪其记忆程度。在两个模型家族、两种架构的六款开源模型上的实验验证了我们的假设：与未获得训练信号的自幻觉相比，模型对训练中强化的幻觉回忆率平均提升了4.83%，而后者仅提升0.17%。事实上，模型甚至记住了它们在推理中立即否定的错误事实——这一回溯模板源自以推理为中心的训练，对知识召回无益。此外，我们发现记忆最强的是最难的训练问题，且对幻觉进行奖励会显著降低模型后续回避该幻觉的可能性（平均下降7.23%）。总体而言，本工作首次系统识别并研究了模型在训练过程中学习幻觉的一个此前未被关注的路径。

Weight-Space Programming Writes In-Context Learning and Matches Fine-Tuning in Language Models
权重空间编程实现上下文学习并媲美微调
Weight-space programming is a frontier language model's recently reported ability to read and rewrite another network's weights without gradient descent. We investigate how examples can provide a weight update so that a weaker language model performs the task with nothing in the prompt, and study what it edits. Our experiments cover six tasks, four frontier editors and three families of weaker language models, without the editors being told anything about in-context learning (ICL). In most runs, the few-shot minus zero-shot residual is measured at the final prompt token, the ICL task vector, and write it into the weights, at its sparsest into near-silent MLP units keyed to the prompt format. Results reach the accuracy of ICL and of fine-tuning on the same examples, LoRA included, usually leaving perplexity untouched, with worse performance under changes of wording. We also qualify the comparison by counting unfinished runs, observing that one editor falls below fine-tuning; held only to the seen examples, without task pairs of their own, only the strongest editor has similar performance. Given a task and unlabelled inputs, that editor writes its own examples and does nearly as well on unseen items as with labels.

权重空间编程是前沿语言模型最近展示出的一种能力，即无需梯度下降即可读取并重写另一网络的权重。我们研究示例如何提供权重更新，使较弱的语言模型在提示中不包含任何内容的情况下完成任务，并探究其修改的内容。实验涵盖六个任务、四个前沿编辑器和三个较弱语言模型家族，且编辑器未被告知任何关于上下文学习（ICL）的信息。大多数实验中，在最终提示词元处测量少样本减去零样本的残差，作为ICL任务向量，并将其写入权重，通常以最稀疏的方式写入与提示格式相关的近静默MLP单元。结果在相同示例上达到了ICL和微调（包括LoRA）的准确率，通常保持困惑度不变，但在措辞变化时性能下降。我们通过统计未完成运行的情况进一步评估对比，发现其中一个编辑器表现低于微调；仅在仅使用见过的示例、无自身任务对的情况下，最强编辑器才具有类似表现。给定一个任务和未标注输入，该编辑器能自行生成示例，并在未见样本上的表现几乎与有标签时相当。

Non-Expanding Gated Continued Fraction Architecture for Feedforward Layers in Language Models
前馈层中非扩展门控连分数架构
Recent work has shown that continued fraction inspired neural architectures (CoFrGeNets) can be highly parameter efficient yet performant for language modeling. In this work, we revisit continued fraction-based channel mixing with the goal of improving its performance and simplifying integration into standard pre-training protocols for language models. We achieve this by two simple yet critical contributions: i) we propose a novel non-expanding gated continued fraction inspired architectural component to replace feedforward layers in established language model architectures that may use vanilla multilayer perceptrons or gated linear units or (vanilla/gated) experts in mixture-of-experts (MoE) type architectures. ii) We propose a way to re-implement divisions -- the non-linearity in these architectures -- in terms of logarithms and exponentials. By experimenting on different language modeling architectures such as GPT-2, Llama-3, Mamba-2 and (nano) Llama-MoE we find that our proposed architecture performs similarly or significantly better than the continued fraction architecture proposed in prior work along with having higher throughput. In addition, our re-implementation of divisions makes training of deeper CoFrGeNets stable without the need for incremental training which was proposed in prior work as a means to stabilize training, but added notable complexity to existing large language model training implementations. Code is available at http://github.com/IBM/CoFrNets

近期研究表明，受连分数启发的神经架构（CoFrGeNets）可在语言建模中实现高度参数效率且性能优异。本文重新审视基于连分数的通道混合机制，旨在提升其性能并简化集成到标准语言模型预训练协议中的难度。我们通过两项简单但关键的贡献实现目标：i）提出一种新颖的非扩展门控连分数启发式架构组件，用于替代现有语言模型架构中的前馈层，这些架构可能使用原始多层感知机、门控线性单元或（原始/门控）专家的混合专家（MoE）型架构；ii）提出一种将此类架构中的除法运算重新实现为对数与指数形式的方法。通过对GPT-2、Llama-3、Mamba-2和（纳米）Llama-MoE等不同语言建模范式进行实验，我们发现所提出的架构在性能上与先前工作的连分数架构相当或显著更优，同时具备更高的吞吐量。此外，我们对除法的重新实现使得更深的CoFrGeNets训练稳定，无需像之前工作所建议的增量训练，从而避免了为现有大语言模型训练流程增加显著复杂性。代码已公开于 http://github.com/IBM/CoFrNets。

Search From the Start: Search-Native Pretraining for Language Models
从开始就搜索：面向语言模型的搜索原生预训练
Modern language models (LMs) are increasingly deployed with access to search tools at inference. Yet pretraining remains predominantly focused on predicting static text, with search capabilities typically introduced during post-training. We propose Search-Native Pretraining as a practical route to teach search capability during pretraining from ordinary text corpora. Specifically, we augment pretraining corpus at scale with web-search queries and retrieve web response using a local index. Training on this augmented corpus teaches models to generate search calls and incorporate retrieved evidence during language modeling. Across Wikipedia and DCLM-Edu corpora and 360M to 3.3B parameter scales, search-pretrained models ("QueryLM") show improved performance on knowledge-intensive tasks and higher steerability toward the retrieved evidence. Crucially, their advantage persists after supervised fine-tuning of both search-pretrained and conventionally pretrained models, indicating that the tested post-training procedures do not fully recover the capabilities developed during pretraining. Throughout our experiments, we find that a 1.4B QueryLM consistently beats or matches the performance of a FLOP-matched 3.3B baseline model across all knowledge intensive tasks. These results establish search-pretraining as a practical way to develop foundational search capabilities during pretraining. We release the training artifacts, including model checkpoints and data.

现代语言模型在推理时越来越多地接入搜索工具。然而，预训练仍主要聚焦于静态文本的预测，搜索能力通常在后训练阶段引入。我们提出“搜索原生预训练”作为一种实用路径，旨在从普通文本语料库中在预训练阶段教授搜索能力。具体而言，我们在大规模预训练语料库中加入网络搜索查询，并使用本地索引检索网页响应。在这一增强语料库上训练，使模型学会生成搜索调用并整合检索到的证据进行语言建模。在Wikipedia和DCLM-Edu语料库以及360M至3.3B参数规模下，经过搜索预训练的模型（“QueryLM”）在知识密集型任务上表现更优，且对检索证据的可引导性更高。关键的是，即使在对搜索预训练和传统预训练模型进行监督微调后，其优势依然存在，表明所测试的后训练流程无法完全恢复预训练期间发展出的能力。在整个实验中，一个1.4B的QueryLM模型在所有知识密集型任务上始终优于或匹配FLOP相当的3.3B基线模型。这些结果确立了搜索预训练作为在预训练阶段发展基础搜索能力的一种实用方法。我们发布了训练资源，包括模型检查点和数据。

MedicalRSI: Medical Recursive Self-Improving Agents via Clinical Model-Harness Co-Evolution
MedicalRSI：通过临床模型- harness 共进化实现医疗递归自我改进代理
Medical agents built on large language models (LLMs) have advanced rapidly, yet most stay fixed once deployed and do not keep using their experience to improve later attempts. As a result, the same kind of error can recur across patients. To address this, existing self-improvement methods update an agent's weights or its medical harness from its own experience, but their improving strategy stays fixed and never learns which changes actually help, which caps how much the agent can improve. Recursive self-improvement (RSI) instead improves both the agent and its improving strategy. However, achieving RSI for medical agents is challenging, because clinical workflows rarely come with a reference harness, and a change that fixes early errors may not fix the problems the agent meets later. We propose MedicalRSI, an RSI framework built on Clinical Model-Harness Co-Evolution, in which one model plays two roles. The Solver is the medical agent itself, and the Improver is its learnable improving strategy, which writes a medical harness from the agent's failures. Each harness is rewarded by its gain over acting without it, and both roles are trained together, so the Improver keeps adapting to the problems the Solver has not yet solved. On multiple benchmarks and LLM backbones, MedicalRSI outperforms existing context-level and weight-level self-improvement methods and transfers well to unseen workflows, new LLMs and new databases, a step toward medical agents that keep improving.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLM）的医疗代理发展迅速，但大多数在部署后保持不变，不会利用经验持续改进后续尝试。因此，相同类型的错误可能在不同患者间重复出现。为解决此问题，现有自我改进方法通过自身经验更新代理权重或其医疗harness，但其改进策略固定不变，从未学习哪些更改真正有效，这限制了代理的改进潜力。递归自我改进（RSI）则同时改进代理及其改进策略。然而，实现医疗代理的RSI极具挑战性，因为临床工作流程很少提供参考harness，且早期修复错误的更改未必能解决后期遇到的问题。我们提出MedicalRSI，一种基于临床模型-harness共进化的RSI框架，其中单一模型扮演双重角色：求解器（即医疗代理本身）和改进者（其可学习的改进策略），后者根据代理的失败编写医疗harness。每个harness通过其相对于无harness行为的收益获得奖励，两者协同训练，使改进者持续适应求解器尚未解决的问题。在多个基准测试和LLM骨干网络上，MedicalRSI优于现有的上下文级和权重级自我改进方法，并能良好迁移到未见过的工作流程、新LLM和新数据库，朝着持续改进的医疗代理迈出重要一步。
👤 Ziqing Wang, Hamid Palangi, Lili Zhao, Kaize Ding 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

Know Your Context Bank: Repairing Inconsistency and Measuring Generalization in Context-Aware Text-to-SQL
了解你的上下文银行：修复上下文感知文本到SQL中的不一致并衡量泛化能力
Text-to-SQL systems increasingly rely on context banks: curated collections of example queries, metric definitions, and domain knowledge that tell a model how an organization writes its queries. In the enterprise, a bank grows over years as many analysts add entries, checked one at a time for correctness (does the SQL answer its own question?) and rarely for consistency with one another. Yet correctness is often loosely defined: a question seldom fixes how an entity is counted or which table defines it, so two entries can each pass review yet resolve the same decision in opposite ways. We call this condition context bank rot. A second question follows: does a system that uses a bank generalize from it, or only reproduce it? Existing benchmarks cannot tell, as they do not control how far a test question sits from the context. We answer both questions by comparing queries decision by decision, with a fast scorer distilled from human-calibrated LLM judges. Turned inward, the comparison diagnoses and repairs a bank. Fine-tuning on a cleaned BIRD-train bank beats a same-size random subset at every epoch (+2.6 points on average), and 92.4% of our removals had their SQL corrected by an independent expert audit, against 45.6% of audited entries. In-context examples that follow one convention beat those following the opposite one by 13.3 points, while a mix is barely better than none. Turned outward, the comparison generates test questions at a controlled distance from a bank, a pipeline we call CAT (Context-Aware Testing). On CAT-BIRD, for retrieval and fine-tuning alike, the benefit of a bank falls step by step with distance and is largely gone once about three quarters of a question's logic is new, and on CAT-Spider the benefit of retrieval likewise shrinks with distance.
中文翻译
文本到SQL系统越来越多地依赖于上下文银行：由示例查询、度量定义和领域知识组成的精心整理集合，指导模型如何编写组织的查询。在企业中，随着众多分析师逐年添加条目，银行不断增长，每个条目都经过逐一检查以确保正确性（SQL是否回答了自身的问题），但很少检查彼此之间的一致性。然而，正确性的定义往往模糊：一个问题很少明确实体的计数方式或其定义表，因此两个条目可能各自通过审查，却对同一决策采取相反的方式。我们称这种现象为上下文银行腐坏。第二个问题是：使用银行的系统是真正泛化，还是仅重复复制？现有基准无法回答，因为它们未控制测试问题与上下文之间的距离。我们通过逐决策比较，利用从人类校准的大语言模型裁判中提炼出的快速评分器来回答这两个问题。向内看，该比较可诊断并修复银行。在清理后的BIRD-train银行上微调的表现优于相同大小的随机子集（平均提升2.6分），且92.4%的被移除条目经独立专家审计后得到修正，而被审计条目的修正率仅为45.6%。遵循统一规范的上下文示例比遵循相反规范的高出13.3分，混合情况仅略优于无上下文示例。向外看，该比较生成了与银行保持可控距离的测试问题，我们称之为CAT（上下文感知测试）流程。在CAT-BIRD上，无论检索还是微调，银行带来的优势随距离逐步下降，一旦问题逻辑中有约四分之三为新内容，优势基本消失；在CAT-Spider上，检索收益也随距离减小。
👤 Hailong Li, Mohammadreza Pourreza, Tal Neiman, Reza Sherkat, Per Jacobsson et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26756]
agentic-rl
Reinforcement Learning Unlearns Corrupted SFT Reasoning
强化学习能够消除被污染的监督微调推理
We show that reinforcement learning (RL) in reasoning tasks can succeed even when the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data is severely corrupted. In a mathematical reasoning dataset (GSM8K), we systematically corrupt training trajectories across 361 configurations by injecting realistic error types, varying error locations, trace-answer consistency, and corruption ratios up to 100%. While aggregated SFT-only performance steadily deteriorates under corruption (dropping from 43.61% to 13.80%), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) post-training consistently restores model accuracy back to gold-baseline levels (approximately 60%). Crucially, initializing RL with as few as N = 8 minimal, incorrect, three-line "form-only" traces boosts post-RL performance to 69.30%, outperforming standard gold SFT initialization (60.71%) and GRPO without SFT (35.00%). Further, we show that SFT accuracy is largely uncorrelated with post-RL accuracy (r approximately 0), demonstrating a fundamental asymmetry in post-training: SFT primarily establishes structural layout (form), while RL independently discovers functional reasoning (function).
中文翻译
我们表明，在推理任务中，即使监督微调（SFT）数据严重受损，强化学习（RL）仍能取得成功。在数学推理数据集（GSM8K）中，我们通过注入真实错误类型，系统性地对361种配置的训练轨迹进行污染，涵盖错误位置、轨迹-答案一致性以及污染比例（最高达100%）。尽管聚合的SFT性能随污染程度持续恶化（从43.61%降至13.80%），但后训练阶段的组相对策略优化（GRPO）始终能将模型准确率恢复至黄金基准水平（约60%）。关键的是，仅用N=8个最小的、错误的、三行“仅形式”轨迹作为RL初始化，即可使后RL性能达到69.30%，优于标准黄金SFT初始化（60.71%）和无SFT的GRPO（35.00%）。此外，我们发现SFT准确率与后RL准确率几乎无关（相关系数r≈0），揭示了后训练阶段的根本不对称性：SFT主要建立结构布局（形式），而RL则独立发现功能推理（功能）。
👤 Anika Misra, Shreyas Vinod, Huteng Dai 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

TEA-Bench-BR: Can Coding Agents Turn a Branching Clinical Specification into Correct Code?
TEA-Bench-BR：编码智能体能否将分支临床规范转化为正确代码？
Coding agents increasingly implement rules written by domain experts, and bugs in such code are silent: it runs and returns a plausible answer. We introduce TEA-Bench-BR, a benchmark built on the Brazilian pathway for early autism (TEA) screening: three fictitious instruments with the structure of real ones, a multi-professional referral flow, and export to FHIR R4 with the national RNDS profiles, all specified in Portuguese. A generator samples each task's parameters (144 tasks, 6,798 hidden cases), and the hidden cases kill all 1,955 non-equivalent mutants of the reference. With Gemma 4 as the agent, the 12B solves 38.0% of single-instrument tasks and none at the FHIR level; the 31B solves 77.8%, 85.2% and 78.7% at levels 1 to 3 and passes over 99% of the hidden cases, its remaining failures mostly in input validation rather than clinical decisions. With the same tasks in English, the 31B solves 57.4% at level 3, as its code validates an identifier less often; the 12B's, more often. 编码智能体越来越多地实现领域专家编写的规则，而此类代码中的错误往往是沉默的：程序运行并返回看似合理的答案。我们引入TEA-Bench-BR，这是一个基于巴西早期自闭症（TEA）筛查路径构建的基准：包含三个虚构工具（结构与真实工具一致）、多专业转诊流程，以及符合国家RNDS档案的FHIR R4导出，所有内容均以葡萄牙语描述。生成器为每个任务采样参数（共144个任务，6,798个隐藏案例），隐藏案例可杀死参考代码的所有1,955个非等价变异体。使用Gemma 4作为智能体，12B模型解决了38.0%的单工具任务，但在FHIR级别未能解决任何任务；31B模型在第1至第3级分别解决了77.8%、85.2%和78.7%的任务，并通过超过99%的隐藏案例，其剩余失败主要出现在输入验证而非临床决策方面。在相同任务的英文版本中，31B在第3级解决了57.4%的任务，因其较少验证标识符；而12B则更频繁地进行验证。12B遗漏了10.10%需要转诊的案例；31B仅遗漏0.02%。对修改版PHQ-9的污染探测未发现任何违反原始规则的错误。

SLP-Bench: Evaluating and Improving Acoustic Claims of Reasoning Audio Language Models on Clinical Speech
SLP-Bench：在临床语音上评估和改进推理型语音语言模型的声学主张
Audio language models (ALMs) can answer clinical questions from speech, outperforming baselines built on handcrafted acoustic features. Reasoning ALMs generate a chain of thought that refers to properties of the speaker's voice before predicting. Each statement the model makes about the voice needs to be checked against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. On clinical speech, existing evaluations score only the final answer. We introduce Speech Language Pathologist Bench (SLP-Bench), a benchmark grading each acoustic claim in the chain of thought against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. It covers five clinician-rated datasets and 25 perceptual dimensions, and assesses claims for factuality, stability under resampling, and consistency across reworded and different questions. Across ten open-weight reasoning ALMs evaluated zero-shot, claim balanced accuracy ranges from 0.52 to 0.58, claims change under resampling and rewording, and the most consistent models are not the most factual. Fine-tuning a 7B ALM with GRPO on a reward for claims that agree with clinicians raises its claim balanced accuracy from 0.54 to 0.60 and its mean kappa from 0.39 to 0.61, while preserving answer accuracy on per-dimension questions. SLP-Bench is available upon request, subject to approval by the providers of each dataset.

语音语言模型（ALMs）能够从语音中回答临床问题，其表现优于基于手工设计声学特征的基线模型。推理型ALMs会生成一个思维链，其中包含对说话人声音特性的描述，然后据此进行预测。模型关于声音的每一项陈述都需要与临床医生对同一录音的感知评分进行比对。目前针对临床语音的评估仅关注最终答案。我们提出了语音语言病理学家基准（SLP-Bench），该基准将思维链中的每个声学主张与临床医生的感知评分进行逐项评分。该基准涵盖五个由临床医生评分的数据集和25个感知维度，评估主张的真实性、重采样下的稳定性以及重述问题后的连贯性。在对十种开放权重推理型ALMs进行零样本评估时，主张平衡准确率在0.52至0.58之间，主张在重采样和重述后发生变化，且最一致的模型并非最真实的模型。通过使用GRPO在与临床医生意见一致的主张上进行奖励信号微调一个7B的ALM，其主张平衡准确率从0.54提升至0.60，平均kappa值从0.39提升至0.61，同时保持了在各维度问题上的答案准确率。SLP-Bench可应请求获取，需经各数据集提供方批准。

Beyond Math, Code, and Logic: Do Reasoning Models Transfer to Scientific Argumentation?
超越数学、代码与逻辑：推理模型能否迁移至科学论证？
Scientific discourse depends on argumentative reasoning: identifying claims, evaluating evidence, and constructing coherent responses. While recent advances in reasoning-capable language models have demonstrated strong performance on mathematical, coding, and logical benchmarks, their ability to engage in scientific argumentation remains unclear. We present the first systematic evaluation of language models across seven tasks spanning argument mining, rebuttal generation, and discourse-level reasoning using research papers, peer reviews, and grant proposals. Our study reveals that even frontier models with strong general reasoning skills struggle with domain-specific argumentative tasks, highlighting a fundamental capability gap. To address this, we introduce a training framework that explicitly scaffolds the argumentative reasoning process in language models, substantially improving their competence in scientific discourse. The resulting compact models approach or exceed the performance of much larger proprietary systems and generalize to unseen conversational settings, demonstrating reasoning transfer beyond task-specific supervision. These findings underscore that effective scientific argumentation is not an emergent property of scale, but requires explicit reasoning-aware training, and they point toward practical pathways for building AI systems that can contribute meaningfully to scientific discourse.

Potential emergence in LLM agents: a statistical physics perspective
大语言模型智能体中的潜在涌现现象：统计物理视角
There has been significant recent interest in understanding the behaviour of LLM agents from the perspective of human preferences or corpus statistics. In this paper we propose agent states as intermediate variables through which post-training can be measured and predicted. From a statistical physics perspective, we study the emergence of a global potential which governs the transition of state in LLM agent reasoning. We introduce a partition function to model the ideal KL-regularised transition distribution and track potential structure, mean reward and state entropy. We demonstrate our approach in two Qwen3.5-9B tasks: safety-test selection and physics-reasoning auditing. We observe that the potential structure strengthens during post-training as predicted from our framework, while joint changes in reward and state entropy provide consistency evidence. States thus provide a quantitative description of how post-training organises agent behaviour.

Efficient Natural Language to Temporal Logic Translation for Real-Time Physical AI
面向实时物理人工智能的高效自然语言到时序逻辑翻译
Robots can be controlled to perform tasks with guaranteed correctness when the task instructions are stated in a formal language. Large language models (LLMs) have been widely used to allow users to provide such instructions in natural language (NL), by translating NL into formal language. When deploying such translators on a robot, however, we face limited computing and energy resources, while offloading computation or data to the cloud may be infeasible or undesirable. Yet an LLM is a general-purpose text generator, whereas a formula in a fixed formal language fragment follows a well-defined syntax. We therefore ask whether text generation is needed at all. For the formal language of linear temporal logic (LTL), we show that this translation can instead be broken down into answering a set of questions with pre-defined answers. We then construct a System-One model, a recent class of NL-based classifier, to provide such answers and enable us to translate a natural language instruction into an LTL instruction. On three benchmarks, our translator matches or exceeds the accuracy of the best local LLMs available, at up to one order of magnitude lower runtime and energy consumption. We show that it generalizes to unseen atomic propositions and temporal patterns.

HR-Merge: Model Merging with Headroom-Weighted Residual Refinement
HR-Merge：基于余量加权残差优化的模型融合
Model merging combines experts fine-tuned from a shared backbone, such as LoRA adapters of a large language model, into one multi-task model without further training. Existing methods apply one rule to every task, yet the accuracy they lose is uneven across tasks. We show that the lost accuracy remains recoverable from each task's residual, its task vector minus the merged update, and that a task's validation headroom, the share of its expert validation accuracy the merge loses, indicates where recovery pays off. Residuals from different tasks interfere, so they must be merged rather than summed. We propose HR-Merge, a training-free wrapper around any merging method. It weights each residual by its task's headroom, re-merges the weighted residuals with the base operator, and adds the result back at a strength selected on validation, falling back to the base merge when no strength helps. On six NLI adapters of LLaMA-3 8B, HR-Merge lifts TIES from 93.29 to 94.29 normalized accuracy, above all tested bases, mainly by recovering its weakest task. In vision, on three CLIP backbones, up to 20 tasks, and LoRA merging, it generally improves or preserves base accuracy and yields the best model in every setting.
中文翻译
模型融合将从共享主干网络微调得到的专家模型（如大型语言模型的LoRA适配器）合并为一个无需进一步训练的多任务模型。现有方法对所有任务采用统一规则，但不同任务损失的准确率并不均衡。我们发现，每个任务的残差（即任务向量减去融合更新量）仍保留可恢复的准确率，而任务的验证余量（即融合过程中损失的专家验证准确率比例）可指示恢复收益是否值得。由于不同任务的残差会相互干扰，必须进行融合而非简单相加。我们提出HR-Merge，一种适用于任何融合方法的无训练封装框架。它根据任务的余量权重化每个残差，使用基础算子重新融合加权残差，并在验证集上选择最优强度将结果回加；若无强度提升则退回到基础融合。在LLaMA-3 8B的六个NLI适配器上，HR-Merge将TIES得分从93.29提升至94.29的归一化准确率，优于所有测试基线，主要得益于对最弱任务的性能恢复。在视觉领域，针对三个CLIP主干网络、最多20个任务及LoRA融合，HR-Merge通常保持或提升基线准确率，并在每种设置下均取得最佳模型表现。

Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation of RL Experts: Toward Continual Post-Training
可组合的软提示蒸馏：迈向持续后训练
RL post-training typically produces domain experts that must then be consolidated into one model, and as new experts keep arriving, this consolidation becomes a continual process. Weight merging and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) must re-consolidate the whole model whenever an expert is added, and can erode capabilities outside the target domains. We propose Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation (CSPD), which keeps the pre-RL model frozen, distills each RL expert independently into a soft prompt, and composes experts by concatenating their prompts without joint training. A single soft prompt suffices to distill an RL expert, recovering at least 90% of the teacher's gain in 22 of 23 math, code, and agent settings. Independently trained prompts compose well. In a multi-expert setting, the average rises with every expert added, and the full composition matches MOPD (67.83 vs. 67.79). The composition generalizes best across six held-out benchmarks. Input-space adaptation adds little serving overhead, keeping throughput within 1.5% of the unadapted backbone and reaching 2.40x that of rank-4 LoRA. These results point toward continual post-training on a fixed backbone.
中文翻译
强化学习后训练通常生成特定领域的专家模型，随后需将其整合为单一模型；随着新专家不断出现，这一整合过程演变为持续性任务。现有的权重融合与多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）方法在新增专家时必须重新整合整个模型，且可能损害目标领域以外的能力。我们提出可组合软提示蒸馏（CSPD），保持预强化学习模型冻结，独立地将每个强化学习专家蒸馏为一个软提示，并通过拼接提示实现专家组合，无需联合训练。单个软提示即可有效蒸馏一个RL专家，在23个数学、代码和智能体场景中的22个中恢复至少90%的教师性能增益。独立训练的提示具有良好的可组合性。在多专家设置中，平均性能随专家数量增加而提升，完整组合表现接近MOPD（67.83 vs. 67.79）。该组合在六个保留基准上的泛化能力最强。输入空间适配带来的推理开销极小，吞吐量保持在未适配主干的1.5%以内，达到rank-4 LoRA的2.40倍。这些结果表明，基于固定主干的持续后训练是可行路径。

AMARIS: Merging Generalist and Specialist LLMs via Adaptive Subspace Inheritance
AMARIS：通过自适应子空间继承融合通用型与专业型大语言模型
Domain specialization of large language models (LLMs) often improves target-domain performance but degrades broad capabilities. Model merging is an attractive training-free alternative, yet common parameter-space strategies (for example, interpolation and sparsified averaging) provide limited control over task-specific interference. We introduce Adaptive Model Alignment via Ranked Inherited Subspaces (AMARIS), a covariance-driven framework that transfers specialist updates only along selected activation directions. AMARIS formulates merging as budgeted subspace inheritance: it seeks high specialist activation capture under a regularized base-output perturbation budget, then builds a low-rank projection gate from ranked generalized eigendirections. This yields a single static merged checkpoint that concentrates transfer in high-utility, low-interference directions. We evaluate AMARIS on Qwen3-based merges (8B and 32B) across finance, instruction-following, and multilingual SEA specialization. In our evaluated settings, AMARIS reaches Pareto operating points that are competitive with strong training-free baselines, including 98.37% specialist retention with 95.16% core retention for the 8B finance setting, 103.55% core retention with 90.28% specialist retention for the 8B instruction-following setting, and 99.20% core retention with 94.80% specialist retention for the 32B SEA setting. These results support subspace-constrained merging as a practical route to compositional capability integration without additional training.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）的领域专业化通常能提升目标领域性能，但会削弱其通用能力。模型融合是一种有吸引力的免训练替代方案，但常见的参数空间策略（如插值和稀疏平均）对任务间干扰的控制能力有限。我们提出自适应模型对齐通过排序继承子空间（AMARIS），一种基于协方差驱动的框架，仅沿选定的激活方向传递专业模型更新。AMARIS将融合建模为预算约束的子空间继承：在正则化的基础输出扰动预算下，寻找高专业激活捕获率，并基于排序的广义特征方向构建低秩投影门。这生成一个静态合并检查点，使知识迁移集中于高价值、低干扰的方向。我们在基于Qwen3的融合（8B和32B）上评估AMARIS，涵盖金融、指令遵循和多语言东南亚（SEA）专业化场景。在所评估设置中，AMARIS达到了与强训练自由基线相当的帕累托最优点，包括8B金融设置中98.37%的专业保留率与95.16%的核心保留率，8B指令遵循设置中103.55%的核心保留率与90.28%的专业保留率，以及32B SEA设置中99.20%的核心保留率与94.80%的专业保留率。这些结果支持子空间约束融合作为无需额外训练的组合能力集成的实用路径。

Belief-State Management Meets Rubric Rewards for Agentic Forecasting
信念状态管理与评分奖励结合用于智能体预测
Current large language model (LLM) agents still struggle to forecast real-world events. This stems from two primary limitations: i) standard ReAct harness accumulates reasoning and evidence within one growing context, and ii) outcome-based rewards in forecasting lead to a slow and noisy signal that cannot distinguish sound reasoning from lucky guesses. To address these, we introduce a forecasting training framework including harness design and post-training. For the harness side, we propose Proactive Belief-State Management (PBM), equipping agents with a fold tool to compress lengthy context into compact belief states and reset the context for subsequent exploration. For the training side, we develop SEIPH, a five-dimensional, outcome-blind rubric, providing process-quality rewards. Using the rubric-based reward, we post-train a 35B model with PPO and GRPO, yielding DeepForecaster-PPO and DeepForecaster-GRPO. Theoretically, we prove that idealized fold-based stopping converges and calibrated local rewards reduce segment-level gradient variance. Empirically, DeepForecaster-PPO (35B) achieves the best Brier score on both ForecastBench and Polymarket-Sim, outperforming frontier agents based on DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T), Kimi-K2.6 (1T), and GPT-5.5. DeepForecaster-GRPO ranks second on both FutureX metrics. Ablations reveal that PBM also improves the untrained base model without any training. Calibration analysis shows that DeepForecaster-PPO makes more accurate high-confidence predictions than Qwen3.6 and GPT-5.5. Trajectory analysis further shows that forecasts generally improve from the first to the final belief state as agents gather evidence and revise their judgments.

当前大型语言模型（LLM）代理在预测现实世界事件方面仍面临挑战。这主要源于两个限制：i）标准ReAct框架将推理和证据累积在一个不断增长的上下文中；ii）基于结果的奖励在预测任务中导致信号缓慢且嘈杂，无法区分合理的推理与侥幸猜测。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种包含框架设计和后训练的预测训练框架。在框架方面，我们引入了主动信念状态管理（PBM），赋予代理一个折叠工具，可将长上下文压缩为紧凑的信念状态，并重置上下文以进行后续探索。在训练方面，我们开发了SEIPH，一种五维、与结果无关的评分体系，提供过程质量奖励。利用基于评分的奖励，我们使用PPO和GRPO对35B模型进行后训练，得到DeepForecaster-PPO和DeepForecaster-GRPO。理论上，我们证明了理想化的基于折叠的停止机制具有收敛性，且校准的局部奖励能降低分段级别的梯度方差。实证结果显示，DeepForecaster-PPO（35B）在ForecastBench和Polymarket-Sim上均取得最佳Brier分数，优于基于DeepSeek-V4-Pro（1.6T）、Kimi-K2.6（1T）和GPT-5.5的前沿代理。DeepForecaster-GRPO在FutureX指标上排名第二。消融实验表明，PBM即使在无训练情况下也能提升未训练的基础模型性能。校准分析显示，DeepForecaster-PPO在高置信度预测上比Qwen3.6和GPT-5.5更准确。轨迹分析进一步表明，随着代理收集证据并修正判断，预测质量从初始到最终信念状态普遍提升。
