Double counting in LLM evidence fusion: exact posteriors separate rule compliance from posterior gain
LLM证据融合中的重复计数：精确后验分离规则合规性与后验增益
Language models fuse reports in retrieval, multi-agent consensus and agent memory, where treating shared-source reports as independent can double count evidence. A common remedy is an instruction to count each source once. Its value depends on the receiver's raw baseline. Pricing it needs an exact reference. We build the graded-dependence exact reference: binary worlds with single-report reliability 0.65 and four dependence levels, whose disclosed parameters give every world an exact posterior and every rule a target risk. The paired gain protocol scores raw and instructed answers of four configurations on 2,976 worlds. Agreement and gain separate in both directions. At $δ$ = 0, claude-opus-5-5 and gpt-6.1-sol agreed with the rule within 0.001 in 744 of 744, and the instruction raised their posterior mismatch by 0.044; gpt-6-luna agreed in 165 of 744 and gained 0.0140 (95% interval 0.0064 to 0.0219); claude-haiku-5-5's gain moved from -0.0295 ($δ$ = 0) to +0.0302 ($δ$ = 1) (descriptive). The prespecified first-child endpoint contrast, gain at $δ$ = 1 minus $δ$ = 0, was 0.0342 (0.0303 to 0.0382). In the preregistered tests, the raw source-weight contrast was positive (0.259, 95% interval 0.182 to 0.337; Holm rejected zero) and the grouping endpoint difference was not detected (0.0004, -0.0023 to 0.0031). This interval excludes differences larger than 0.0031 in magnitude and lies within the registered precision bound of $\pm$0.0046. The reference prices the rule before any model call. The paired gain, estimable as a Brier difference on labelled deployment items, decides adoption per receiver and prompt condition.
中文翻译
语言模型在检索、多智能体共识和代理记忆中融合报告，若将共享来源的报告视为独立，可能导致证据重复计数。常见补救方法是指令要求每源只计一次。其价值取决于接收者的原始基线。定价需要一个精确参考。我们构建了分级依赖的精确参考：在二元世界中，单报告可靠性为0.65，四种依赖程度，其披露参数使每个世界都有精确后验，每个规则都有目标风险。配对增益协议在2,976个世界中对四种配置的原始和指令回答进行评分。一致性与增益在两个方向上均分离。当δ=0时，claude-opus-5-5和gpt-6.1-sol在744个世界中均符合规则（误差<0.001），但指令使其后验偏差增加0.044；gpt-6-luna符合165/744，增益0.0140（95%区间0.0064至0.0219）；claude-haiku-5-5的增益从δ=0时的-0.0295升至δ=1时的+0.0302（描述性）。预设首子节点终点对比（δ=1减δ=0）为0.0342（0.0303至0.0382）。在预注册测试中，原始源权重对比为正（0.259，95%区间0.182至0.337；Holm拒绝零假设），而分组终点差异未被检测到（0.0004，-0.0023至0.0031）。该区间排除了超过0.0031的差异，且落在注册精度界限±0.0046内。参考价格在任何模型调用前就确定了规则价值。配对增益可通过标注部署项上的Brier差异估算，决定根据接收者和提示条件是否采纳。

Reasoning in Two Dimensions: Quantifying Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness and Efficiency
二维推理：量化思维链的忠实性与效率
Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning exposes intermediate steps, but a correct answer does not establish that an LLM relies on the dependence expressed by those steps or reasons efficiently. We operationalize CoT faithfulness and efficiency within a common framework. Specifically, we represent a CoT as a semantic reasoning graph and decompose the model's predictive dependence along two complementary axes: dependence explained by semantic support versus additional reasoning history, and dependence allocated to answer-reaching versus off-path reasoning. This decomposition yields global and step-level measures of unfaithfulness and inefficiency. Controlled interventions show that the proposed metrics respond to hidden dependence and induced off-path reasoning as intended. Across mathematical and multi-hop reasoning tasks, answer-level unfaithfulness is consistently associated with greater predictive uncertainty, whereas both unfaithfulness and inefficiency exhibit weak or inconsistent associations with answer correctness. We further derive theoretically grounded, graph-aware fine-tuning objectives that improve the targeted reasoning properties without directly optimizing the computationally expensive metrics or compromising answer accuracy. Results indicate that accuracy and brevity provide incomplete assessments of reasoning quality: reliable CoT evaluation should also consider whether the LLM's predictive dependence is faithfully represented and efficiently allocated.
中文翻译
思维链（CoT）推理暴露中间步骤，但正确答案并不能说明LLM是否真正依赖这些步骤所表达的依赖关系或是否高效推理。我们在统一框架内操作化了CoT的忠实性和效率。具体而言，我们将CoT表示为语义推理图，并将模型的预测依赖分解为两个互补维度：由语义支持解释的依赖与额外推理历史的依赖，以及分配给达成答案的依赖与偏离路径推理的依赖。这一分解产生了全局和步骤级的不忠实与低效度量。受控干预表明，所提出的度量能按预期响应隐藏依赖和诱导的偏离路径推理。在数学和多跳推理任务中，答案层面的不忠实始终与更高的预测不确定性相关，而无论是不忠实还是低效，与答案正确性的关联均弱或不一致。我们进一步推导出理论上有根基、图感知的微调目标，可在不直接优化计算成本高昂的度量或牺牲答案准确性的情况下，改善目标推理属性。结果表明，准确性和简洁性无法完整评估推理质量：可靠的CoT评估还应考虑LLM的预测依赖是否忠实呈现且高效分配。

ORION: Exact Canonical-State Supervision for Systematic Generalization in Transformers
ORION：Transformer系统泛化中的精确规范状态监督
Autoregressive Transformers generally fail on out-of-distribution reasoning due to two primary reasons: positional corruption over long sequences and structural state drift across deep execution steps. We introduce ORION, a framework that supervises exact canonical execution states via auxiliary heads to anchor latent trajectories to valid computational manifolds. Across Folding, GridShift, and CLRS-30, a pre-registered 2 x 2 factorial shows that RoPE and ORION address orthogonal axes. RoPE recovers length extrapolation (59.8%), while ORION recovers depth extrapolation (66.7%). Moreover, their combination complementarily achieves strict additive gain in joint length-depth extrapolation, while either method alone offers near satisfactory results. Negative controls confirm that the gain depends strictly on exact canonical alignment; hashed state statistics fail to constrain depth extrapolation.

Run One, Get Two: Near-Free Streaming Safety Checks for Local LLMs
运行一次，获得两次：面向本地大模型的近零成本流式安全检查
Local LLM assistants decode for one user at a time, so decoding is bound by memory bandwidth and leaves compute idle. Streaming safety checks today trade accuracy against cost. A dedicated guard model is accurate but nearly doubles the latency per token, while a hidden-state probe is cheap but less accurate. We present ToRA, an adapter that runs a second model as an extra row in the generator's decode step, sharing its weights and KV cache. Dual-row kernels read each weight once for both rows. On Qwen3-0.6B, ToRA matches or beats a dedicated streaming guard, with higher F1 on 12 of 17 benchmarks. It detects 11.8 points more unsafe responses than the probe at a similar cost, under 4% added latency per token.

Perceive Once, Escalate on Demand: Handing Off Visual KV Memory from Small to Large VLMs
感知一次，按需升级：将小型VLM的视觉KV记忆传递给大型VLM
An embodied agent cannot afford a large vision-language model (VLM) at every step of a long video, so a cascade runs a small VLM continuously and escalates only uncertain decisions to a large one. Each escalation, however, forces the large model to re-encode a history the small model has already perceived, and this dominates its cost. We present Baton, which hands the large model the small model's visual memory, its key-value (KV) cache over the history. Baton rests on one finding: the large model needs a usable memory, not a faithful copy of its own. A map trained to reproduce the large model's cache matches it most closely, yet brings no gain over the small model alone. Baton instead distills a per-layer linear map, without labels, from the large model's own answers, with the large model encoding only the current frame. Interventions on the map trace the gain to one direction, carried by the middle third of the large model's layers. On RoboMME, Baton beats the small model alone, a pruned or shortened re-watch, and a text memory; as the escalation channel of a cascade it matches re-watching to within about one point at 28-47% less compute, and 8x less per escalation at 64 frames. Its gain grows with the large model, from 8B to 32B, tracks the large model's headroom across eight model pairs from four families, and transfers zero-shot to OpenEQA.
中文翻译
一个具身智能体无法在长视频的每一步都使用大型视觉语言模型（VLM），因此采用级联架构持续运行小型VLM，并仅在决策不确定时升级到大型模型。然而，每次升级都会迫使大型模型重新编码小型模型已感知的历史内容，这成为其主要开销。我们提出Baton，将小型模型的视觉记忆（即对历史的键值缓存）直接传递给大型模型。Baton基于一个发现：大型模型需要的是可用的记忆，而非自身完整忠实的副本。训练一个映射网络以复现大型模型的缓存，尽管匹配度最高，但并未带来性能提升。Baton则通过无标签的方式，从大型模型自身的回答中蒸馏出每层的线性映射，且大型模型仅需编码当前帧。对映射的干预分析表明，性能增益来自大型模型中间三分之一层所携带的方向信息。在RoboMME基准上，Baton优于仅使用小型模型、剪枝或缩短重看、以及文本记忆的方法；作为级联中的升级通道，其在计算量减少28%-47%的情况下，性能与重看相当，且每次升级的计算量降低8倍（在64帧场景下）。该方法的增益随大型模型规模增长（从8B到32B），在四个模型家族的八组对比中均与大型模型的潜力保持一致，并可零样本迁移至OpenEQA。

Don't Discard Your Rollouts: Reusing Teacher RL Traces for Student Distillation
不要丢弃你的轨迹：重用教师强化学习轨迹进行学生蒸馏
RL post-training methods such as PPO and GRPO are computationally expensive, with substantial resources devoted to sampling rollouts, which contain multiple completions per prompt along with their respective rewards. Canonical distillation, however, discards these traces and uses more compute to regenerate data from the final teacher. We ask if and how we can reuse the saved teacher RL rollouts to distil student models. Reusing rollouts is non-trivial: naive SFT on the rollouts underperforms canonical distillation, and naive DPO, while performant, is unstable, collapsing on weaker students. We show that treating rollout reuse as a data selection problem using metadata already recorded during GRPO substantially reduces this gap. We use prompt pass rates to order the data, using the easiest prompts for an SFT warm start prior to using mixed-success prompts for DPO. For each prompt, we use saved teacher log-likelihoods as a proxy for selecting student-compatible completions. Across mathematical reasoning and Python coding tasks, with students from multiple model families, our two-stage recipe achieves comparable average performance to canonical distillation, while incurring no additional inference cost for dataset construction.
中文翻译
如PPO和GRPO等强化学习后训练方法计算成本高昂，大量资源用于采样轨迹，这些轨迹包含每个提示的多个完成结果及其对应的奖励。传统蒸馏方法会丢弃这些轨迹，转而使用更多计算重新生成数据。我们探讨了是否以及如何重用已保存的教师强化学习轨迹来蒸馏学生模型。重用轨迹并非易事：直接对轨迹进行SFT表现不佳，而朴素DPO虽性能良好但不稳定，尤其在较弱的学生模型上会出现崩溃。我们发现，将轨迹重用视为一个数据选择问题，利用GRPO过程中已记录的元数据可显著缩小差距。我们使用提示通过率对数据排序，先用较简单提示进行SFT预热，再用混合成功度的提示进行DPO。对于每个提示，使用保存的教师对数似然作为代理，筛选出与学生兼容的完成结果。在数学推理和Python编程任务中，针对多个模型家族的学生，我们的两阶段方案实现了与传统蒸馏相当的平均性能，且无需额外推理成本用于数据集构建。

TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator
TOUMAII：一个450万对工具调用偏好数据集及其源无关生成器
To autonomously execute multi-step tasks, enterprise agents must reliably interact with tools, call APIs, or query databases, making tool-calling a fundamental capability of AI models. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) optimises for producing the correct call, but never contrasts it with a plausible incorrect one. In an environment where even a minor formatting error can invalidate a call and an incorrect decision can derail the interaction, direct preference optimisation (DPO) targets exactly this contrast. DPO, therefore, requires preference pairs rather than plain demonstrations. For tool-calling, such data, especially in realistic multi-turn settings, remains extremely scarce. To address this scarcity, we introduce TOUMAII, a large-scale tool-calling preference dataset containing ~4.5M validated preference pairs, derived from Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M (apache-2.0) conversations. The dataset consists of multi- and single-turn interactions and includes parallel and sequential function calls. Additionally, we release TOUMAII-Gen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that consumes tool-calling SFT datasets and generates a preference dataset with naturally occurring model-generated tool-calling failure modes. To validate the effectiveness of our DPO data, we fine-tune a 3-billion-parameter model, achieving performance parity with other high-quality tool-calling preference datasets. Furthermore, we release a second small preference dataset derived from ToolACE (apache-2.0), showcasing the source-agnostic nature of our pipeline. We release the complete code and datasets: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen and https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets
中文翻译
为了自主执行多步骤任务，企业级智能体必须可靠地与工具交互、调用API或查询数据库，因此工具调用是AI模型的核心能力。监督微调（SFT）优化的是生成正确调用，但从不将其与可能的错误调用进行对比。在环境中，即使微小的格式错误也可能使调用失效，错误决策可能导致交互失败，因此直接偏好优化（DPO）恰好针对这种对比。DPO需要偏好对而非普通示范数据。然而，尤其是在真实多轮场景下，工具调用的偏好数据仍极度稀缺。为解决这一问题，我们引入TOUMAII，一个大规模工具调用偏好数据集，包含约450万条经验证的偏好对，源自Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M（Apache-2.0）对话。该数据集涵盖单轮与多轮交互，包括并行与串行函数调用。此外，我们发布了TOUMAII-Gen，一个合成数据生成管道，可接收工具调用SFT数据集，生成包含自然发生的模型调用失败模式的偏好数据集。为验证DPO数据的有效性，我们微调了一个30亿参数模型，性能达到与其他高质量工具调用偏好数据集相当的水平。同时，我们还发布了一个从ToolACE（Apache-2.0）衍生的较小偏好数据集，展示了我们管道的源无关特性。完整代码与数据集已开源：https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen 和 https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets。

VerifiQuant: Governance-by-Execution for Financial LLM Workflows
VerifiQuant：面向金融LLM工作流的执行治理机制
Large language models embedded in financial workflows fail most dangerously by emitting confident, well-formatted but wrong numbers with no signal distinguishing them from correct ones. We argue that the central deployment problem is governability: a system must determine when a question supports a numeric answer and when it should instead clarify, intercept, or refuse. We present VerifiQuant, a governance-by-execution framework that packages financial logic into inference contracts and evaluates each query through an executable diagnostic funnel before deterministic computation. This separation makes computation, clarification, and refusal structured and auditable while keeping financial arithmetic outside the language model. Against matched retrieval-and-execution baselines, and within a declared contract library, the principal benefit is not higher accuracy on well-posed questions but safer behavior when an input does not justify a number. When it finds no applicable contract, it says so instead of guessing. Coverage sweeps and failure analysis put a price on this boundary: safety is bought with coverage rather than with accuracy, and residual failures remain attributable to specific runtime frontiers.

嵌入金融工作流的大语言模型最危险的失败形式是：自信且格式良好的错误数值输出，且无法与正确结果区分。我们认为核心部署问题是可治理性：系统必须能判断一个问题是否支持数值回答，还是应澄清、拦截或拒绝。我们提出VerifiQuant，一种基于执行的治理框架，将金融逻辑封装为推理合约，并在确定性计算前通过可执行的诊断流程评估每个查询。这种分离使得计算、澄清和拒绝过程结构化且可审计，同时将金融运算保留在语言模型之外。相较于匹配的检索-执行基线，在声明的合约库内，其主要优势并非在良好定义问题上的更高准确率，而是在输入不支持数值回答时表现出更安全的行为。当找不到适用合约时，系统会明确说明，而非猜测。覆盖率扫描与故障分析揭示了这一边界的代价：安全性是以覆盖率换取的，而非准确性；残余故障仍可归因于特定运行时边界。

SDG: State-Dependent Gating at Chunk Boundaries for Linear Attention
SDG：在块边界处基于状态的门控机制用于线性注意力
Recent gated linear attention models use state-independent gates, computed from token representations without directly reading the recurrent memory. This design supports efficient chunkwise computation: sequences are divided into chunks of consecutive tokens, with parallel computation within each chunk. However, the same token representation produces the same gate, even when the layer stores different information. We introduce State-Dependent Gating (SDG), which computes element-wise gates from the change in memory over a chunk and applies them at the chunk boundary. This provides state feedback for forgetting while preserving parallel computation within each chunk. We develop fused kernels for Gated DeltaNet, Kimi Delta Attention, Gated DeltaNet-2, and Mamba-3. Our theoretical analysis quantifies the potential reconstruction benefits of state-dependent, element-wise gating under a signal--noise model. We also show that boundary decay is an element-wise ridge proximal step when the gate is held fixed. Empirical evaluation of 1.3B linear attention and hybrid models trained on 100B tokens shows improvements across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, synthetic and real-world retrieval, and long-context understanding. These results demonstrate that SDG is an effective approach to enhancing existing linear attention models.
中文翻译
近期的门控线性注意力模型采用与状态无关的门控机制，即从标记表示中计算门控值，而不直接读取循环记忆。这种设计支持高效的分块计算：将序列划分为连续标记的块，并在每个块内并行计算。然而，相同的标记表示会产生相同的门控值，即使层中存储的信息不同。我们提出了基于状态的门控（SDG），通过计算块内记忆变化来生成逐元素门控，并在块边界应用这些门控。这为遗忘提供了状态反馈，同时保持了每个块内的并行计算能力。我们为门控DeltaNet、Kimi Delta注意力、门控DeltaNet-2和Mamba-3开发了融合核函数。理论分析在信号-噪声模型下量化了基于状态的逐元素门控的潜在重建优势。我们还证明，当门控固定时，边界衰减相当于逐元素的岭近端步骤。对13亿参数的线性注意力模型及在1000亿标记上训练的混合模型进行的实证评估显示，在语言建模、常识推理、合成与真实世界检索以及长上下文理解任务中均有性能提升。结果表明，SDG是一种有效增强现有线性注意力模型的方法。
👤 Renjia Deng, Yuxi Liu, Yutong He, Xue Wang, Kun Yuan 📅 2026-10-16 🔗 arXiv

[2611.27762]
From Novel Combinations to Informative Absence: Structural permission, communicative opportunity, and form choice in language learning
从新颖组合到有意义的缺失：语言学习中的结构许可、交际机会与形式选择
Language learners must infer which novel expressions are grammatically licensed and when continued absence is evidence against them. Our generative theory unites these capacities via counterfactual expression opportunities, decomposing utterance generation into structural permission, communicative opportunity, and form choice. First, shared predictive continuations recover structural permission for unseen word-construction pairings across 30 artificial grammars (mean AUC 0.945 after 120 sentences). Second, under hierarchical uncertainty, we derive a joint evidence law linking omissions to context diversity and within-context repetition, and an exact finite-sample context-refinement inequality. Thus, observing related expressions calibrates expected use to sharpen negative evidence, and higher expected use can drive steeper posterior declines for stronger-permission candidates (reversing initial order after six omissions in a fixed-rate example). Third, we prove that while raw counts confound semantic availability and form selection, scene-level opportunity observations identify both. Empirically, reanalyzing adult and child artificial-language experiments shows meaning-conditioned restriction; judgments of restricted combinations improve held-out production predictions across 399 participants (+0.036 nats/production). Naturalistic child-directed and child speech (11 families, six corpora) shows pervasive source-level usage clustering beyond lexical, number, and dependency-role controls, grounding hierarchical rate uncertainty. Finally, modeling causative judgments across five languages shows that structural compatibility and calibrated usage probabilities draw on distinct distributional, semantic, and discriminative cues. Together, these findings show how the organization and calibration of communicative opportunities govern the evidential value of linguistic absence.
中文翻译
语言学习者必须推断哪些新表达是语法允许的，以及持续缺失是否构成反证据。我们的生成理论通过反事实表达机会统一了这两种能力，将话语生成分解为结构许可、交际机会和形式选择三个阶段。首先，在30个人工语法中，共享预测延续能够恢复对未见词构型配对的结构许可（120句话后平均AUC达0.945）。其次，在层级不确定性下，我们推导出一个联合证据定律，将缺失与语境多样性及同语境重复联系起来，并建立精确的有限样本语境精炼不等式。因此，观察到相关表达可校准预期使用频率以强化负证据，更高的预期使用频率可推动更强许可候选者的后验下降更陡峭（在固定速率示例中，六次缺失后逆转初始顺序）。第三，我们证明原始计数会混淆语义可用性与形式选择，而场景级机会观测则能同时识别两者。实证上，重新分析成人与儿童的人工语言实验显示意义条件限制；对受限组合的判断提升了保留生产预测的准确性（399名参与者，每生产提升+0.036纳特）。自然语境下的亲子对话与儿童言语（11个家庭，六个语料库）显示超越词汇、数量和依存角色控制的源级使用聚类现象，支持层级率不确定性。最后，跨五种语言的致使判断建模表明，结构兼容性与校准后的使用概率依赖于不同的分布、语义与判别线索。综上，这些发现揭示了交际机会的组织与校准如何决定语言缺失的证据价值。

[2611.27721]
Decision Models for Multilingual Emotion Classification: A Comparative Study Against LLMs and Encoders
多语言情感分类中的决策模型：与大语言模型和编码器的对比研究
Decision models receive a question with a closed list of answers and return a probability for each answer instead of free text. They promise low latency and robustness to prompt wording, but no study has compared several of them with fine-tuned encoders across languages. We ask whether systems used without training on labelled data (zero-shot) can replace fine-tuning, and what the decision format adds over a general-purpose large language model (LLM). We compare three decision models (Jev, OpenAI Decisions, Strands Decider 2B) and two LLMs (GPT-6 Luna, Qwen3.5-2B), given Italian instructions, with two fine-tuned multilingual encoders (tabularisai, XLM-EMO) on four emotions (joy, sadness, anger, fear) in 10 social-media datasets in five languages, with paired difference and equivalence tests. The three best zero-shot systems (two decision models and one LLM) score on average about one macro-F1 point below an encoder fine-tuned on the target dataset; our test sets cannot exclude a gap of 4-5 points, and on single datasets either approach wins by up to 22 points. They beat encoders fine-tuned on another dataset by 8-15 points, though against XLM-EMO this may not extend to new datasets, and instructions in each text's language change their results by under 1 point. Exploratorily, they beat the strongest public emotion model used as is by about 6 points. OpenAI's decision endpoint matches GPT-6 Luna, the LLM it runs on, at a fifth of its median latency, and a 2-billion-parameter decision model beats a same-size general LLM by 10 points, owing to its training, not the decision format. The best zero-shot systems, the LLM included, have an SD of about 1 point across prompts. Without labelled data they are a strong default, and decision models add lower latency and, unlike generative LLM use, per-option probabilities. Fine-tuning won by a wide margin only on the largest dataset, GoEmotions.
中文翻译
决策模型接收一个带有封闭答案列表的问题，返回每个答案的概率而非自由文本。它们承诺低延迟和对提示措辞的鲁棒性，但尚未有研究在跨语言场景中系统比较多种决策模型与微调编码器的表现。我们探讨无需标注数据训练（零样本）的系统是否能替代微调，以及决策格式相较于通用大语言模型（LLM）带来了哪些优势。我们在意大利语指令下，对比了三种决策模型（Jev、OpenAI Decisions、Strands Decider 2B）和两种LLM（GPT-6 Luna、Qwen3.5-2B），使用两个微调过的多语言编码器（tabularisai、XLM-EMO），在五种语言的十个社交媒体数据集上对四种情绪（喜悦、悲伤、愤怒、恐惧）进行评估，并采用配对差异与等价性检验。结果表明，表现最好的三个零样本系统（两个决策模型和一个LLM）平均比在目标数据集上微调的编码器低约1个宏F1分；我们的测试集无法排除4-5分的差距，且在单个数据集上任一方法最高可领先22分。这些系统在对抗其他数据集微调的编码器时领先8-15分，但与XLM-EMO相比，这一优势可能不适用于新数据集；每篇文本使用其语言的指令，结果变化不足1分。探索性分析显示，它们比最强的公开情感模型直接使用时高出约6分。OpenAI的决策端点在延迟仅为GPT-6 Luna的五分之一的情况下达到其性能，而一个20亿参数的决策模型在相同规模下比通用LLM高出10分，这归因于训练而非决策格式本身。表现最佳的零样本系统（包括LLM）在不同提示间标准差约为1分。在无标注数据情况下，它们是强有力的默认选择，且决策模型提供了更低延迟和每选项概率输出，而无需生成式LLM的不确定性。只有在最大数据集GoEmotions上，微调才显著胜出。

[2611.27673]
Hy-MT3: More Accurate, Robust and Practical Multilingual Translation Models in the Wild
Hy-MT3：更准确、更稳健且更具实用性的多语言翻译模型在真实场景中的表现
Hy-MT3 is the third generation of the Hunyuan multilingual translation model family, including \textbf{Hy-MT3-1.8B}, \textbf{Hy-MT3-7B} and \textbf{Hy-MT3-30B-A3B (MoE)}. Compared with Hy-MT2, Hy-MT3 translates \textbf{more accurately, more completely and more faithfully to user constraints}: on real-world scenario data, the share of samples with mistranslations is reduced by \textbf{43\%}, omissions or additions by \textbf{74\%}, and instruction-following errors by \textbf{40\%}, while language coverage is expanded from \textbf{38 to 102} languages. With only 3B activated parameters, \textbf{Hy-MT3-30B-A3B achieves the best overall performance among all evaluated models in non-thinking mode}, including flagship models such as Qwen3.8-Max, Kimi-K3.1-Pro and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, and approaches the strongest closed-source thinking models such as GPT-6.1-Sol and Gemini-4-Argon, while surpassing models that consume \textbf{more than 30$\times$} its inference compute. Beyond general translation, Hy-MT3 is optimized for two industrial scenarios. For \textbf{game localization}, built with high-quality game-domain data and a professional evaluation system from the Honor of Kings team, Hy-MT3-30B-A3B matches or outperforms strong closed-source models such as Gemini 3.1 Pro on all core metrics, and can be plugged into game development pipelines through an open-source toolchain. For \textbf{image translation}, Hy-MT3 improves context understanding and error correction to better tolerate OCR errors, achieving the best result among models of comparable size. Finally, we open-source \textbf{TransRubric}, a rubric-based automatic evaluation suite for translation, which outperforms other evaluation schemes on the WMT26 metrics shared task test set and is more stable with respect to human evaluation.

[2611.27506]
How Many Bytes Has a BPE Model Seen? Estimating Byte-Token Exposure in Pretraining from Corpus Statistics and Embedding Norms
BPE模型看到了多少字节？从语料统计和嵌入范数估算预训练中的字节-标记暴露量
Byte-level BPE vocabularies retain 256 single-byte fallback tokens. When converting such a model into a pure byte-level model, these tokens provide the starting embeddings. How much training have they received? We estimate byte-token exposure in Qwen3.8 using two independent routes. First, we tokenize 540 MB of public corpora covering 40 domains and 33 languages, then combine per-domain token shares with assumed pretraining mixtures. Single-byte tokens account for 13 to 36% of tokens, mostly ASCII; genuine UTF-8 fallback bytes (0x80-0xFF) account for 0.15 to 0.27% under mainstream mixtures, or about 2 billion occurrences per trillion pretraining tokens. Fallback rates vary by four orders of magnitude across languages, reflecting how well each script is covered by the vocabulary. Second, we analyze embedding and output-matrix row norms for four open models, using untrained padding rows as a zero-exposure baseline and ordinary tokens with known frequencies for calibration. In all four models, the 13 bytes that cannot occur in valid UTF-8 remain at initialization, while every legal high byte lies in a high-frequency band. For Qwen3.8, the norm-based estimate agrees with the corpus estimate within a factor of four. The norm-frequency relation eventually folds, so inversion is useful only as a model-specific, order-of-magnitude estimate. No private data were used.
中文翻译
字节级BPE词汇表保留了256个单字节回退标记。当将此类模型转换为纯字节级模型时，这些标记提供初始嵌入。它们经历了多少训练？我们通过两条独立路径估算Qwen3.8中的字节-标记暴露量。首先，我们对覆盖40个领域和33种语言的540MB公开语料进行分词，然后将各领域标记占比与假设的预训练混合比例结合。单字节标记占总标记的13%至36%，主要为ASCII；真正的UTF-8回退字节（0x80-0xFF）在主流混合比例下占0.15%至0.27%，约每万亿预训练标记出现20亿次。不同语言间的回退率相差达四个数量级，反映了各脚本在词汇表中的覆盖程度。其次，我们分析了四个开源模型的嵌入和输出矩阵行范数，使用未训练的填充行作为零暴露基线，普通标记则以其已知频率进行校准。在所有四个模型中，无法出现在有效UTF-8中的13个字节在初始化时仍保持原状，而每个合法的高字节均位于高频区间。对于Qwen3.8，基于范数的估计与语料估计结果相差不超过四倍。范数-频率关系最终会折叠，因此反演仅适用于特定模型的量级估计。未使用任何私有数据。
👤 Liwei Zhang, ZhiMing Tong, Mochen Guan, Linzhe Li 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.27491]
Alignment without Transfer: Tokenization Impacts Cross-lingual Representations during Multilingual Language Model Pretraining
无迁移的对齐：分词如何影响多语言预训练中的跨语言表示
Tokenization represents one of the most crucial steps in mulilingual NLP pipelines. Yet, tokenizers routinely under-allocate vocabulary to low-resource languages. While improved allocation leads to better performance, it remains poorly understood how tokenization shapes cross-lingual representations and transfer during pretraining. To address this, we pretrain 39 mono- and bilingual 1B decoder-only models on FineWeb2-HQ across English, German, French, Arabic and Chinese, varying only the tokenizer. Our results demonstrate that improved vocabulary allocation (i) substantially raises cross-lingual representation alignment in early-to-mid layers, an effect that prior work measuring only the output layer could not observe, (ii) increases cross-lingual parametric knowledge sharing in feed-forward neurons, supported by correlational and causal evidence, and (iii) an alignment-transfer dissociation, where cross-lingual transfer remains essentially unchanged despite higher alignment. Moreover, transfer is significantly lower for cross-script bilinguals even under fair vocabulary allocation, indicating that the script barrier is not merely a tokenizer artifact. All of our checkpoints and code are publicly available.

No Audio, No Transcripts, No Problem: LLM-based ASR Adaptation Using Only Domain Documents
无需音频，无需转录：仅使用领域文档的基于LLM的语音识别自适应
Can a speech recognizer be adapted to a new domain with no target audio and no transcripts, using only written documentation? This is the realistic onboarding setting: a new customer hands over raw documentation, not the transcripts that text-only adaptation normally assumes. We turn a domain's documents into adaptation text in two ways, directly as written sentences or rendered by a large language model (LLM) into synthetic spoken dialogs, and feed them to a recently proposed denoising-based text-only adaptation method. We show that, across in-domain, out-of-domain, and cross-domain settings on two corpora, document-only adaptation can match or closely approach real transcriptions; whether written or conversational rendering is best depends on speaking style and the amount of text generated. To our knowledge, this is the first study of document-only adaptation of LLM-based ASR. As no existing corpus pairs audio, transcripts, and documents, we also built and released a document-grounded benchmark.

[2611.27075]
When Do LLM-Elicited Priors Help? A Controlled Audit of Language Models as Priors for Small-Sample Bayesian Regression
大语言模型提供的先验何时有效？一项关于大语言模型作为小样本贝叶斯回归先验的受控审计
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly asked where to center the coefficients of a small-sample Bayesian regression, but the evidence is unclear: benchmarks are often built to match the prior and models may remember them. We audit this practice with one frontier and one flash-tier model, using hidden feature names, post-cutoff housing data and a pre-specified test that gives features the wrong descriptions. First, the models share their errors (correlation $0.92$--$0.99$ across draws, vendors and paraphrases): averaging $20$ draws is no more accurate than one, and treating draws as independent creates overconfidence. Much of this error comes from reporting each feature's effect alone, not with the others held fixed. Second, against the best classical prior, the LLM center helps clearly on one of four benchmarks (RMSE $-5.8\%$), marginally on two, and hurts mean RMSE on the fourth. Third, the LLM's stated uncertainty is unreliable; letting the data set the prior scale cuts the harm of a wrong center four-fold against a unit-variance prior. A pre-specified external test returned the verdict \textsc{partial}: on $18$ rule-selected problems the center helps on six and hurts on four, and a hard trust gate trusts a harmful prior while an evidence-weighted mixture is safer.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于确定小样本贝叶斯回归中系数的中心位置，但现有证据尚不明确：基准测试往往针对先验设计，模型可能记忆这些测试。我们使用前沿模型和快速迭代模型对这一做法进行了受控审计，采用隐藏特征名称、截断后的住房数据以及预先设定的测试，其中特征描述错误。首先，模型表现出高度一致的错误（不同抽样、供应商和改写之间的相关性为0.92至0.99）：平均20次抽样并不比单次更准确，将抽样视为独立会引发过度自信。大部分错误源于单独报告每个特征的影响，而非在其他特征固定的情况下分析。其次，与最佳经典先验相比，LLM提供的中心在四个基准中的一个上表现明显更好（RMSE降低5.8%），两个上略有改善，但在第四个上反而增加了均方根误差。第三，LLM声明的不确定性不可靠；让数据决定先验尺度可使错误中心带来的损害减少四倍，相较于单位方差先验。一个预先设定的外部测试得出结论“部分有效”：在18个规则选择的问题中，中心帮助了六个，伤害了四个；硬信任门会信任有害先验，而基于证据加权的混合方法更安全。

[2611.27053]
Isolating the Language Shell: A Content-Controlled Study of Multilingual Reasoning
隔离语言外壳：多语言推理的内容控制研究
Multilingual reasoning distillation requires choosing the language in which teacher reasoning is presented, yet existing recipes often entangle linguistic form with differences in generation, translation, and filtering. We construct content-controlled supervision by rendering the same verified English trajectories into English, translated target-language, and language-mixed shells, and fine-tune two pretrained students in three target languages, evaluating them on multilingual mathematical reasoning at two difficulty levels. Even with the underlying reasoning content held fixed, we find that shell choice can change downstream accuracy, with effects varying across students, languages, and difficulty levels. Pre-training compatibility does not consistently track these outcomes, and even when our tests find no clear accuracy difference between shells, their output-language behavior can still differ substantially. These results suggest that the supervision shell is a distinct design choice in multilingual reasoning distillation.
中文翻译
多语言推理蒸馏需要选择教师推理所使用的语言，但现有方法常将语言形式与生成、翻译和过滤差异混淆。我们通过将相同的经验证明的英文轨迹分别呈现为英文、目标语言翻译版以及语言混合外壳，构建了内容控制的监督信号，并在三种目标语言中微调两个预训练学生模型，评估它们在两种难度水平下的多语言数学推理能力。即使推理内容保持不变，外壳选择仍会影响下游准确率，且影响效果因学生、语言和难度水平而异。预训练兼容性并不能一致预测这些结果，即使测试未发现外壳间明显的准确率差异，其输出语言行为仍可能显著不同。这些结果表明，监督外壳是多语言推理蒸馏中的一个独立设计选择。

[2611.26965]
Beyond Prompt Heuristics: Assessing Child Safety Alignment in LLMs
超越提示启发式：评估大语言模型的儿童安全对齐情况
Child-safety taxonomies typically characterize safety risks through a predefined set of harm categories, and existing evaluations largely operationalize these categories using prompts that explicitly invoke them. However, child-safety considerations can also arise in seemingly benign requests that lack recognizable harm-category cues. We therefore investigate how child-safety alignment generalizes to such settings. Across six models, we find considerably higher misalignment rates on requests without recognizable harm-category cues than on those containing such cues (80.0, 54.0, and 48.4 percentage-point gaps for Qwen3.5-27B, GPT-5.4-mini, and Claude-Haiku-4.5, respectively). Through controlled behavioral and mechanistic analyses, we find that introducing a single harm-associated word reduces misalignment across most models, with mechanistic evidence suggesting that child-safety reasoning may rely in part on lexical and semantic associations with words in the prompt. Our findings motivate evaluations that treat child safety as context-dependent, and not reducible to a fixed set of harm categories.
中文翻译

[2611.26920]
Partially canonical axes in large language model representations
大语言模型表征中的部分规范轴
Large language models (LLMs) develop rich internal representations of language, but whether their individual coordinate axes carry meaning, or whether meaning is encoded only in directions in representation space, is still an open question. A common assumption is that individual axes in LLMs are arbitrary. We here challenge this view: across 12 LLMs from six model families, the native axes are reliably more aligned with other models' representations, with human brain responses to language, and with interpretable linguistic features than randomly rotated axes that preserve the same geometry. The native axes nonetheless fall well short of the best achievable alignment, so LLM coordinates are not fully arbitrary but also not fully canonical: they are partially canonical. A theory of how LLMs represent language must explain both the population geometry they learn and why individual units acquire patterns of response profiles that recur across systems.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）发展出丰富的语言内部表征，但其单个坐标轴是否具有语义意义，或仅在表征空间的方向中编码语义，仍是未解之谜。一种常见假设是LLM中的单个轴是任意的。我们在此挑战这一观点：在来自六个模型家族的12个LLM中，原始轴与其它模型的表征、人类大脑对语言的反应以及可解释的语言特征具有更高的对齐度，优于保持相同几何结构但随机旋转的轴。然而，原始轴仍远未达到最佳对齐程度，因此LLM坐标并非完全任意，也非完全规范，而是部分规范。关于LLM如何表征语言的理论必须解释它们所学习的群体几何结构，以及为何个体单元在不同系统中产生重复的响应模式。

Cross-Linguistic Distinctive-Feature Constraints on Segmental Triphones: Evidence for Contrastive Dispersion
音段三音素的跨语言区别特征约束：对比分散性的证据
Natural speech reflects articulatory and perceptual constraints, yet how these pressures shape consecutive segment transitions across languages remains underexplored. We analyze adjacent distinctive-feature distances in token-weighted segmental triphones across 17 Universal Dependencies corpora spanning nine language families, transcribed into IPA and mapped to 24 PanPhon features using Hamming and cosine metrics. Natural sequences exhibit an intermediate-distance mode (8-10 features under Hamming, except Vietnamese; 0.28-0.38 under cosine). Against an unrestricted within-word shuffled baseline, natural words reject the inventory-only null (W = 0, p < 10-4), showing higher expected distance, narrower dispersion, and lower joint triphone entropy in all 17 languages. Under a stricter within-word consonant-vowel template baseline, expected distance and dispersion are no longer separated, whereas joint entropy remains lower in 16 languages (p = 1.07x10-4). Entropy decomposition reveals higher mutual information between incoming and outgoing transitions in all 17 languages and all nine families (W = 0, p < 10-4), demonstrating sequential coupling beyond syllable-template alternation. Word-level mean-SD correlations and directional asymmetries do not replicate cross-linguistically.
中文翻译
自然语音反映了发音和感知的约束，但这些压力如何塑造跨语言的连续音段转换仍研究不足。我们分析了17个涵盖九个语系的Universal Dependencies语料库中，以词权重标记的音段三音素的相邻区别特征距离，使用IPA转录并映射到24个PanPhon特征，采用汉明距离和余弦度量。自然序列表现出中间距离模式（汉明距离为8-10个特征，越南语除外；余弦距离为0.28-0.38）。相较于不受限的词内随机打乱基线，自然词汇拒绝仅由音位库存决定的零假设（W = 0，p < 10⁻⁴），显示出更高的期望距离、更窄的分布范围和更低的联合三音素熵，所有17种语言均如此。在更严格的词内辅音-元音模板基线下，期望距离与分布不再分离，但联合熵在16种语言中仍较低（p = 1.07×10⁻⁴）。熵分解显示，所有17种语言和九个语系中，进入与离开转换之间的互信息更高（W = 0，p < 10⁻⁴），证明了超出音节模板交替的序列耦合。词级均值-标准差相关性和方向不对称性未能跨语言复现。

[2611.26833]
Rethinking Full-Weight LoRA with Domain and Task Adapter Scaling for Question Answering over Portuguese Well Drilling Reports
针对葡萄牙语钻井报告问答任务的全权重LoRA与领域和任务适配器缩放重思
Adapting large language models (LLMs) to specialized domains remains challenging, particularly for technical documents such as Portuguese well drilling reports, where annotated data are scarce and domain adaptation may compromise instruction-following capabilities. Although Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for parameter-efficient adaptation, its scaling after training remains insufficiently explored. This study investigates the impact of LoRA adapter scaling on question answering over Portuguese drilling reports using Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B. Domain-adaptive pretraining (DAPT) is performed either directly on instruction-tuned models or on their base counterparts, with subsequent adapter transfer, while task-specific adapters are trained on WellQuest, an open-source question-answering dataset derived from Portuguese drilling reports. The two adapters are also combined using complementary weights. Scaling factors ranging from 0.1 to 1.0 are evaluated across multiple training checkpoints using BERTScore. Results show that fractional scaling consistently improves DAPT performance over full-weight application, increasing F1 from 68.8\% to 71.9\% for Qwen2.5-7B and from 63.2\% to 68.5\% for Llama-3.1-8B. QA adapters perform best at high but fractional weights, while base-to-instruct transfer yields better results than direct DAPT on instruct-tuned models. Adapter merging provides a modest improvement for Llama-3.1-8B. Overall, these findings demonstrate that post-training adapter scaling can improve domain adaptation without additional data or training.
中文翻译

[2611.26832]
Neural Geometry as a Learning Signal
神经几何作为学习信号
We investigate whether the geometric structure of neural representations can provide a sufficient learning signal for improving model behavior without any supervision on model outputs. First, we fine-tune Llama-3.1-8B to answer questions such as "What month is four months after June?" by supervising internal representations of the input month (June = 6) and offset (4), which are encoded with Fourier harmonics. Second, we fine-tune the model to predict line breaks in text wrapped to a fixed width by supervising an internal representation of the number of characters since the preceding newline, which is represented by a low-dimensional helical structure. In both tasks, we supervise a low-dimensional subspace of the residual stream using an idealized target structure, and find that internal geometric supervision achieves similar performance to standard fine-tuning (despite no supervision on outputs). Moreover, we find that simply patching in an ideal geometry is not sufficient to recover fine-tuning performance, and thus demonstrate geometric supervision provides beneficial effects beyond the representations it targets. Together, these findings show how mechanistic understanding of neural geometry can be leveraged to shape the behavior of neural networks.
中文翻译
我们探究神经表示的几何结构是否能在无输出监督的情况下提供足够的学习信号以改善模型行为。首先，我们对Llama-3.1-8B进行微调，使其回答诸如“六月之后四个月是哪个月？”等问题，方法是监督输入月份（六月=6）和偏移量（4）的内部表示，这些表示采用傅里叶谐波编码。其次，我们微调模型以预测固定宽度文本换行处的断行位置，方法是监督自上次换行以来字符数的内部表示，该表示以低维螺旋结构呈现。在这两个任务中，我们通过理想化的目标结构监督残差流的一个低维子空间，发现内部几何监督的表现与标准微调相当（尽管没有对输出进行监督）。此外，我们发现仅简单插入理想几何结构不足以恢复微调性能，因此证明几何监督带来的益处超出了其所直接作用的表示。这些发现共同展示了如何利用对神经几何的机制理解来塑造神经网络的行为。
👤 Eric Todd, Tal Haklay, Nikhil Prakash, Matthew Finlayson, Michael Hanna et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

On the Challenges of Using LLMs in High-Risk AI Recruitment: Guiding Questions for Legal Conformity with the AI Act

The EU Artificial Intelligence Act (AI Act) imposes detailed obligations on providers of high-risk AI systems but offers limited operational guidance, particularly when these systems integrate third-party General-Purpose AI (GPAI) models. This paper introduces a toolkit that fosters the development of legal-by-design AI-assisted recruitment systems by translating the core legal obligations of the AI Act's conformity assessment---specifically Articles 9--15 and Annex IV---into techno-normative requirements and corresponding guiding questions. While recruitment pipelines may involve components with varying technical complexity and differing levels of algorithmic transparency, this paper focuses on the most challenging case: the integration of third-party GPAI models into critical stages of the selection process, including resume and job description parsing, applicant scoring, and shortlist generation. This scenario presents significant challenges for downstream providers of high-risk AI systems, as it underscores the difficulties in obtaining the technical information needed to demonstrate compliance with the AI Act's techno-normative requirements, both prior to and during market deployment. After introducing the toolkit and illustrating its application through a simplified example, the paper explores informational dependencies between upstream and downstream GPAI model providers. SAVIOR: Sample-efficient Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for OCR Representation
SAVIOR：面向OCR表征的视觉-语言模型样本高效适应
Optical Character Recognition (OCR) pipelines and vision-language models exhibit high accuracy on standard OCR and document-understanding benchmarks, yet they systematically underperform on document patterns that are operationally critical in financial workflows. These patterns include vertical or rotated text, logo-embedded vendor names, fine-print regulatory clauses, degraded scans, and complex multi-column layouts. While underrepresented in public datasets, they constitute a substantial portion of real-world failure cases. We introduce SAVIOR, a sample-efficient data curation methodology that targets such high-impact failure scenarios to adapt vision-language models for robust financial OCR. We further propose PaIRS, a structure-aware evaluation metric that measures layout fidelity by comparing pairwise spatial relationships between tokens. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct with SAVIOR-Train achieves a word recall of 0.93. When the resulting layout-aware OCR output is used as context for document question answering, it attains an F1 score of 0.89, outperforming both open- and closed-source models such as GPT-4o, Mistral-OCR, PaddleOCR-VL, and DeepSeek-OCR.

[2611.27092]
PLoQ: A Prompt-Specific Low-Rank Basis for 4-bit Quantization of LLM Activations
PLoQ：一种针对4比特量化大语言模型激活的提示自适应低秩基
Low-bit activation quantization of large language models remains challenging, as a small number of dominant directions dictate the quantization range. State-of-the-art methods isolate these directions in a high-precision subspace through transformations calibrated offline and folded into the weights, which fixes the subspace for all prompts, leaving each prompt's few unique directions unprotected, and rules it out wherever no weight can absorb the transformation. We present PLoQ, a prompt-adaptive low-rank decomposition that extracts the dominant activation components before quantization, quantizes them separately, and restores them within the layer's GEMM, without modifying the weights. The low-rank component's basis is estimated from each prompt during prefill and reused throughout decoding. At the same bit width as state-of-the-art mixed-precision quantization (4-bit weights, activations and K/V cache with an eighth of the channels at 8 bits, about 4.2 bits per activation on average), PLoQ outperforms it on all six Llama and Qwen models from 1B to 70B parameters, at up to 8k tokens (16k on two of them), recovering up to 46% of the perplexity that the state of the art still loses to 4-bit activations. On Llama-3.1-8B at 8k tokens it adds only 4% to prefill and 8% to decoding.
中文翻译
大语言模型的低比特激活量化仍然具有挑战性，因为少数主导方向决定了量化范围。现有最先进方法通过离线校准的变换将这些方向隔离在高精度子空间中，并将其折叠到权重中，但这种子空间对所有提示固定不变，导致每个提示中少数独特方向未受保护，且在权重无法吸收变换时无法使用。我们提出PLoQ，一种提示自适应的低秩分解方法，在量化前提取主导激活成分，分别进行量化，并在层的GEMM内恢复，无需修改权重。低秩成分的基由预填充阶段根据每个提示估计，并在解码过程中重复使用。在与最先进混合精度量化（4比特权重、激活和K/V缓存，其中八分之一通道为8比特，平均约4.2比特每激活）相同位宽下，PLoQ在从1B到70B参数的六个Llama和Qwen模型上均表现更优，最长支持8k token（其中两个模型达16k），恢复了最先进方法仍损失的高达46%困惑度。在Llama-3.1-8B模型上，8k token时仅增加4%预填充延迟和8%解码延迟。
👤 Faraz Tahmasebi, Michael Pellauer, Hyoukjun Kwon, Sitao Huang 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26935]
Self-similar Organization of Information in Language
语言中信息的自相似组织
How much information relevant to predicting the future of a passage (i.e. predictive information) is carried by the preceding text, and how is this information distributed along that history? These questions are fundamental to understanding both the statistical structure of language and what large language models (LLMs) can learn about it. Indeed, LLMs trained to predict the next token in text must exploit this predictive information, so its organization determines how the structure of language becomes available to the model to learn. We develop a theory for the organization of predictive information based on the scaling behavior of a family of information measures, and the theory predicts that if this organization has the same form at all measured length scales -- i.e., it has a self-similar structure -- then the information measures evaluated at different lengths collapse onto a common form. We observe this scaling collapse in long text documents, showing that predictive information is present at every length scale we measure and has a self-similar structure. Self-similarity is a considerably stronger property than showing the existence of long-range dependencies, and it constrains the process generating the text. More broadly, our results give quantitative support to the empirical observation that conditioning on more distant text continues to reveal new structure in language to LLMs.
中文翻译
一段文本中包含多少有助于预测其未来内容的预测性信息，以及这些信息如何沿历史分布？这些问题对于理解语言的统计结构以及大语言模型（LLMs）能从中学习到什么至关重要。事实上，训练用于预测文本下一个标记的LLM必须利用这种预测性信息，因此其组织方式决定了语言结构如何被模型所学习。我们基于一类信息度量的缩放行为，发展出一种关于预测性信息组织的理论，该理论预测：如果这种组织在所有测量尺度上都具有相同形式——即具有自相似结构——那么在不同长度下评估的信息度量将坍缩为一个共同形式。我们在长文本文档中观察到了这种缩放坍缩现象，表明预测性信息存在于我们测量的每一个尺度上，并且具有自相似结构。自相似性比仅仅证明长程依赖关系强得多，它对生成文本的过程施加了约束。更广泛地说，我们的结果为经验观察提供了定量支持：对更远距离文本进行条件化，仍会向LLM揭示语言中的新结构。
👤 Leon Lufkin, Cutter Dawes, Kamesh Krishnamurthy 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26919]
ERA: Embed, Route, Adapt for Cold-Start Cross-Model Latent Retrieval
ERA：用于冷启动跨模型潜在检索的嵌入、路由与适应
Production retrieval systems are tied to the embedding model that indexed their corpus. Moving queries to a different encoder normally requires re-embedding the whole corpus, and at cold start there are no query logs while every document embedded by the new encoder incurs an API or compute cost. We present ERA (Embed, Route, Adapt), a system for cold-start cross-model latent retrieval. ERA spends a small anchor budget on maximally informative documents, selected from the existing target index via Cosine Farthest Point Sampling, trains a lightweight adapter with a Manifold Alignment Loss that couples directional alignment with orthogonal regularization to prevent representation collapse, and pairs the adapter with a deterministic router that escalates the rare, hard queries that define perceived retrieval quality. Across five BEIR datasets, ERA matches or outperforms point-wise L2 and embedding-conversion baselines, with relative gains of up to 35% nDCG@10 on Arguana, and on clustered corpora its anchor selection also improves prior methods under a fixed anchor budget. With 1.2 ms of adapter and router overhead on top of a ~20 ms local encoder, ERA at a 20% nominal escalation budget reduces end-to-end query latency from roughly 160 ms to 38-86 ms while concentrating escalations on the queries where the projection fails most.
中文翻译
生产环境中的检索系统通常绑定于索引其语料库的嵌入模型。将查询迁移到不同的编码器通常需要重新嵌入整个语料库，而在冷启动阶段没有查询日志，而新编码器嵌入每个文档都会产生API或计算成本。我们提出了ERA（Embed, Route, Adapt），一种用于冷启动跨模型潜在检索的系统。ERA仅以少量锚点预算投入从现有目标索引中通过余弦最远点采样选出的最具信息量的文档，使用流形对齐损失训练一个轻量级适配器，该损失将方向对齐与正交正则化相结合以防止表示崩溃，并将适配器与一个确定性路由器配对，以提升那些定义感知检索质量的罕见困难查询。在五个BEIR数据集上，ERA的表现达到或超过了逐点L2和嵌入转换基线，Arguana数据集上nDCG@10的相对提升高达35%；在聚类语料库上，其锚点选择在固定锚点预算下也优于先前方法。在约20毫秒本地编码器的基础上，ERA增加1.2毫秒的适配器和路由器开销，当名义升级预算为20%时，端到端查询延迟从约160毫秒降低至38-86毫秒，同时将升级集中在投影失败最严重的查询上。
👤 Venkata Chivukula, Zifeng Wang, Jinsung Yoon 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26917]
What Does the Visual Encoder Buy? Visual Information Gain in Encoder-Free Multimodal Pretraining
视觉编码器带来了什么？无编码器多模态预训练中的视觉信息增益
Recent scaling studies ask whether vision-language models still need a visual encoder by comparing encoder-free and encoder-based models on the next-token loss of the text in image-text data. This loss can fall without any use of the image, and two runs with the same loss can differ in what they take from it. Visual information gain (VIG) is the loss increase when the image is replaced by a uniform one. It splits exactly into global information, carried by image statistics, and layout information, which shuffling the patches within the image destroys. We apply it to decoders with 158M to 1.04B parameters that read pixel patches through a linear embedding, evaluated every billion tokens up to 40B, and to 19 public checkpoints. In our decoders the image lowers the loss by 3% to 46%, depending on the image type. Global information is present after the first billion tokens. Layout information starts near zero, grows slowly with training and does not grow with model size on five of six image types, and a larger batch reaches the same loss with less of it. With a frozen SigLIP 2 encoder in front of the same decoder the loss is lower only when the arrangement of the image is intact. On shuffled images the losses with and without the encoder agree to within 0.04 nats on five of six image types, so the net advantage of the encoder in the loss is layout information, above all the ability to read text in images. Public encoder-free models at production scale (Gemma 4 12B, NEO, Mono-InternVL) do acquire layout information. On the same documents they have 0.53 to 0.76 nats of it, against 0.65 to 0.82 for recent encoder-based models and 0.04 to 0.07 for our decoders, and their released training stages show it rising. These results agree with the reported parity of the two families when both are trained from scratch and place the reported advantage of a pretrained encoder in layout information.
中文翻译
最近的扩展研究通过比较无编码器和有编码器模型在图像-文本数据中下一个标记的文本损失，来探讨视觉-语言模型是否仍需要视觉编码器。这种损失可以在不使用图像的情况下下降，且两次运行即使损失相同，也可能从图像中获取的内容不同。视觉信息增益（VIG）是指用均匀图像替换原图像后损失的增加量。它可精确分解为两部分：由图像统计携带的全局信息，以及通过打乱图像块破坏的布局信息。我们将该指标应用于参数量为158M至1.04B的解码器，这些解码器通过线性嵌入读取像素块，在每十亿个token处评估直至400亿个token，并涵盖19个公开检查点。在我们的解码器中，图像使损失降低了3%至46%，具体取决于图像类型。全局信息在第一个十亿token后即已存在。布局信息初始接近零，随训练缓慢增长，且在六种图像类型中的五种上不随模型规模增大，更大的批次可在较少布局信息下达到相同损失。在相同解码器前加入冻结的SigLIP 2编码器时，只有当图像排列保持完整时损失才更低。在打乱图像上，有无编码器的损失在五种图像类型上差异不超过0.04纳特，因此编码器在损失上的净优势主要来自布局信息，尤其是读取图像中文字的能力。公开的无编码器模型（如Gemma 4 12B、NEO、Mono-InternVL）在生产规模下确实获得了布局信息。在同一文档上，它们的布局信息量为0.53至0.76纳特，而近期有编码器模型为0.65至0.82纳特，我们的解码器仅为0.04至0.07纳特，且其发布的训练阶段显示该信息持续上升。这些结果与两类模型从头训练时表现相当的报告一致，并将预训练编码器的优势归因于布局信息。
👤 Zhixuan Ge, Anqi Li, Wei Qiu 📅 2026-11-20 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26904]
What Does a Zoom Actually Add? Decomposing Fine-Grained Perception Failures in Vision-Language Models
缩放究竟带来了什么？视觉-语言模型细粒度感知失败的分解
Zooming into a region often turns a wrong answer from a vision-language model (VLM) into a right one, and a growing literature takes this as evidence that the model lacked the pixels. But a zoom changes several things at once. It removes the surrounding context, re-tokenizes, at a higher density, pixels the model has already seen, and, if the first pass was downsampled, exposes detail the model never received. We separate these factors for each failure. Holding the first pass fixed, we vary only the second look and assign every failure to the cheapest one that fixes it: reusing cached tokens, isolating the region, re-tokenizing the seen pixels, or acquiring new ones. The study covers 2,753 zoom-rescued failures of Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL and InternVL3 on V*, ZoomBench and TextVQA, at first-pass budgets from 17x fewer pixels than the source to native resolution. First, most rescued failures did not lack the pixels: upsampling the pixels the model had already seen fixes 47-73% of them even at 17x downsampling, against 1-3% for a random region. Second, the first-pass budget decides how often new pixels are needed: depending on the benchmark, new pixels are required for 23-50% of the rescues at 17x and for 12-23% at 4x. The missing-pixel reading and the accounts that dispute it thus hold at different budgets. Third, the useful magnification is large and differs between objects and text: seen pixels fix more failures up to 6-12x their first-pass size per side on objects and saturate by 4x on text, and even native pixels gain from being rendered beyond native resolution. Allowing any magnification, 68-94% of the failures at 17x need no new pixels. These models more often need a better rendering of evidence they already have than new pixels, which motivates re-tokenizing a region before acquiring it.
中文翻译
对区域进行缩放常常能使视觉-语言模型（VLM）从错误答案变为正确答案，越来越多的研究将其视为模型缺乏像素的证据。但缩放实际上同时改变了多个因素：它移除了周围上下文，以更高密度重新标记模型已见过的像素，并且若首次处理是降采样的，则暴露了模型从未接收过的细节。我们针对每种失败分离这些因素。在固定首次处理的前提下，仅改变第二次观察，并将每种失败归因于能修复它的最廉价方式：复用缓存标记、隔离区域、重新标记已见像素，或获取新像素。研究覆盖Qwen2.5-VL、Qwen3-VL和InternVL3在V*、ZoomBench和TextVQA上的2,753个缩放修复失败案例，首次处理的像素预算从源图像的17倍少到原生分辨率。首先，大多数被修复的失败并非由于缺少像素：即使在17倍降采样下，仅对模型已见过的像素进行上采样即可修复47%-73%的失败，而随机区域仅修复1%-3%。其次，首次处理的预算决定了是否需要新像素：根据基准不同，17倍下23%-50%的修复需要新像素，4倍下为12%-23%。因此“缺像素”说法在不同预算下成立与否不同。第三，有效放大程度较大且在物体与文本间存在差异：对于物体，已见像素在每边放大至6-12倍时仍能修复更多失败，文本则在4倍时趋于饱和，甚至原生像素在超分辨率渲染下仍有收益。允许任意放大时，17倍下的68%-94%失败无需新像素。这些模型更常需要对其已有证据进行更好的重标记而非获取新像素，这促使我们在获取新区域前先对其进行重标记。
👤 Zhixuan Ge, Anqi Li, Kexin Geng, Wei Qiu 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
